
===== FILE backend/tests/test_tuning_model_registry.py =====
"""调优模型必须挑一条真能发出请求的，而不是 id 最小的那条。

线上注册中心里躺着一条关不掉的兼容行（#3「Fault matrix material optimizer」，
base_url ``http://127.0.0.1:1/v1``）：``_ensure_model_registry_entries`` 每次被调用
都会把 created_by 为 compatibility/migration 的行强制置回 active，所以停用它不是办法。
真正配好的那条 id 更小，现在侥幸没出事——这些测试盯的是它不在的那天。
"""
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.models import ModelRegistryEntry
from app.tuning_model_registry import resolve_tuning_entry, undispatchable_reason

REAL = "http://192.168.1.35:6002/"
SHELL = "http://127.0.0.1:1/v1"


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _entry(db, entry_id, *, name, base_url, role="tuning", active=True):
    entry = ModelRegistryEntry(
        id=entry_id, role=role, name=name, provider="openai", protocol="openai_chat",
        base_url=base_url, api_path="/v1/chat/completions", model_id="m",
        max_tokens=4096, structured_output=True, active=active, created_by="test")
    db.add(entry)
    db.commit()
    return entry


def test_the_unreachable_shell_is_skipped_even_when_it_has_the_lowest_id(db):
    """兼容壳 id 更小也不能赢——这正是删掉 #2 之后会发生的事。"""

    _entry(db, 1, name="Fault matrix material optimizer", base_url=SHELL)
    _entry(db, 2, name="提示词诊断模型", base_url=REAL)
    entry, skipped = resolve_tuning_entry(db)
    assert entry.id == 2
    assert skipped == ["#1 Fault matrix material optimizer：接入地址指向容器自己的回环，不可能是真实模型"]


def test_only_the_shell_is_left_and_the_reason_travels_with_the_refusal(db):
    """一条都挑不出来时，运营看到的必须是「为什么这几条都不算数」。"""

    _entry(db, 3, name="Fault matrix material optimizer", base_url=SHELL)
    entry, skipped = resolve_tuning_entry(db)
    assert entry is None
    assert skipped and "回环" in skipped[0] and "#3" in skipped[0]


def test_the_ordinary_case_still_picks_the_lowest_id(db):
    _entry(db, 1, name="甲", base_url=REAL)
    _entry(db, 2, name="乙", base_url="http://192.168.1.36:6002/")
    entry, skipped = resolve_tuning_entry(db)
    assert entry.id == 1 and skipped == []


def test_other_roles_and_disabled_rows_are_out_of_scope(db):
    _entry(db, 1, name="主评测", base_url=REAL, role="main")
    _entry(db, 2, name="停用的调优", base_url=REAL, active=False)
    entry, skipped = resolve_tuning_entry(db)
    assert entry is None and skipped == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("base_url,reason", [
    ("http://127.0.0.1:1/v1", "回环"),
    ("http://localhost:6002", "回环"),
    ("http://0.0.0.0:6002", "通配"),
    ("", "没有填"),
    ("   ", "没有填"),
])
def test_addresses_that_cannot_serve_a_model(base_url, reason):
    assert reason in (undispatchable_reason(base_url) or "")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("base_url", [
    "http://192.168.1.35:6002/",
    "https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3",
    "http://host.docker.internal:16052",
])
def test_addresses_that_might_work_are_left_to_the_real_call(base_url):
    """域名不在这里判死活——连不连得通是真实调用该报的事。"""

    assert undispatchable_reason(base_url) is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_unified_label_platform.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import csv
import io
import zipfile
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app import main
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.label_export import build_export
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    HumanReview,
    LabelRelease,
    LabelOutboxEvent,
    ModelConfig,
    PromptVersion,
    PublishedLabel,
    ReviewPanel,
    SamplingPolicy,
    User,
)
from app.strategy_bundle import build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot, get_or_create_bundle


def _database() -> tuple[object, Session, User, EvaluationResult]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="release-admin", password_hash="unused", display_name="发布管理员")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="content.jpg", stored_name="content.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="space_image",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="release-model", model_id="release-model", base_url="https://example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="release-prompt", version="release-A1",
        system_prompt="system", user_prompt="user", rubric_version="R1", status="published",
    )
    policy = SamplingPolicy(id=1, revision=1)
    db.add_all([user, asset, model, prompt, policy])
    db.flush()
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db, model_config=model, prompt_a=prompt, prompt_b=None,
        rubric_version="R1", engine_version="E1", risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=policy,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id, category_key="space_image", prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id, status="completed", stage="done", progress=100,
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id, job_id=job.id, strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db, bundle=bundle, prompt_a=prompt, prompt_b=None,
            sampling_policy=policy, aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
        ),
        precheck_json=json.dumps({
            "classification": {"scope_status": "in_scope", "primary_category": "住宅"},
            "production_fields": {
                "title": "现代客厅", "seotitle": "现代简约客厅空间设计效果图",
                "category": "住宅空间，客厅", "style": "现代简约",
                "tags": ["客厅", "现代", "简约", "木饰面"],
                "cons": "电视墙留白偏多。", "design": "以暖木材质组织空间。",
                "score": 82, "reason": [], "image_defects": "",
                "trait": "3D数字效果图",
            },
            "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
            "media_form": {
                "rendering": {"status": "yes", "confidence": 0.98, "evidence": ["数字渲染"]},
            },
        }),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps({"dimensions": {"lighting": {"grade": 4}}}),
        scoring_json="{}", raw_response_a="{}", score=78, level="L3", confidence=0.92,
        needs_review=False, review_stage="completed", model_id=model.model_id,
        prompt_a_version=prompt.version, rubric_version="R1", engine_version="E1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    review = HumanReview(
        evaluation_id=result.id, reviewer_name=user.username, stage="initial",
        decision="corrected", corrected_level="L2", corrected_score=63,
        corrections_json="[]",
    )
    db.add(review)
    db.flush()
    panel = ReviewPanel(
        evaluation_id=result.id, required_reviewers=1, status="completed", final_review_id=review.id,
        final_truth_json=json.dumps({
            "decision": "corrected", "corrected_level": "L2", "corrected_score": 63,
            "dimensions": {"lighting": 3},
            "key_fields": {"production_fields.style": "现代"},
        }),
        completed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add(panel)
    db.commit()
    return engine, db, user, result


def _client(db: Session, user: User) -> TestClient:
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    return TestClient(app)


def _close(engine: object, db: Session) -> None:
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def _ingress_payload(*, event_id: str, occurred_at: datetime, asset_id: int, version: str = "1") -> dict:
    return {
        "event_id": event_id,
        "schema_version": "content-ingress-v1",
        "event_type": "content.created",
        "source_system": "content-hub",
        "occurred_at": occurred_at.isoformat(),
        "payload": {
            "content_id": "content-001", "content_version": version,
            "category_key": "space_image", "asset_id": asset_id,
        },
    }


def test_ingress_publish_consumer_cursor_and_rollback_are_versioned(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, user, result = _database()
    client = _client(db, user)
    token = "test-label-integration-token"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main,
        "settings",
        replace(main.settings, content_ingress_token=token, label_consumer_token=token),
    )
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
    now = datetime(2026, 8, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        created = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=_ingress_payload(event_id="ing-1", occurred_at=now, asset_id=result.asset_id))
        assert created.status_code == 200
        assert created.json()["event_status"] == "applied"
        assert created.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is False
        assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=_ingress_payload(event_id="ing-1", occurred_at=now, asset_id=result.asset_id)).json()["duplicate"] is True
        changed = _ingress_payload(event_id="ing-1", occurred_at=now, asset_id=result.asset_id, version="2")
        assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=changed).status_code == 409
        stale = _ingress_payload(event_id="ing-older", occurred_at=now - timedelta(seconds=1), asset_id=result.asset_id, version="0")
        assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=stale).json()["event_status"] == "stale"

        release = client.post("/api/label-releases", json={
            "release_key": "release-1", "evaluation_id": result.id,
            "content_key": "content-hub:content-001",
        })
        assert release.status_code == 200
        release_id = release.json()["release"]["id"]
        assert release.json()["release"]["status"] == "pending_review"
        release_drift = client.post("/api/label-releases", json={
            "release_key": "release-1", "evaluation_id": result.id + 999,
            "content_key": "content-hub:content-001",
        })
        assert release_drift.status_code == 409
        published = client.post(f"/api/label-releases/{release_id}/approve-and-publish")
        assert published.status_code == 200
        assert published.json()["release"]["status"] == "published"
        assert published.json()["release"]["published_label_id"] is not None
        assert published.json()["release"]["published_version"] == 1

        listed_release = client.get("/api/label-releases").json()["items"][0]
        assert (
            listed_release["published_label_id"]
            == published.json()["release"]["published_label_id"]
        )
        assert listed_release["is_current"] is True

        read = client.get("/api/consumer/v1/labels/content-hub:content-001", headers=headers)
        assert read.status_code == 200
        assert read.headers["etag"]
        assert read.json()["version"] == 1
        assert read.json()["label"]["level"] == "L2"
        assert read.json()["label"]["production_fields"]["style"] == "现代"
        assert read.json()["label"]["production_fields"]["score"] == 82
        assert read.json()["label"]["image_quality"]["quality_severity"] == "normal"
        assert read.json()["label"]["media_form"]["rendering"]["status"] == "yes"
        assert "raw_response" not in json.dumps(read.json(), ensure_ascii=False)
        changes = client.get("/api/consumer/v1/changes", headers=headers).json()
        assert changes["next_cursor"] == 1
        assert changes["items"][0]["operation"] == "published"
        checkpoint = client.post("/api/consumer/v1/checkpoints", headers=headers, json={"consumer_name": "frontend-search", "cursor": 1})
        assert checkpoint.status_code == 200
        assert client.post("/api/consumer/v1/checkpoints", headers=headers, json={"consumer_name": "frontend-search", "cursor": 0}).status_code == 409

        first = db.scalar(select(PublishedLabel).where(PublishedLabel.content_key == "content-hub:content-001"))
        assert first is not None
        next_release = client.post("/api/label-releases", json={
            "release_key": "release-2", "evaluation_id": result.id,
            "content_key": "content-hub:content-001",
        }).json()["release"]
        next_published = client.post(
            f"/api/label-releases/{next_release['id']}/approve-and-publish"
        )
        assert next_published.status_code == 200
        assert next_published.json()["release"]["published_version"] == 2
        rollback = client.post(f"/api/published-labels/{first.id}/rollback", json={"rollback_key": "rollback-1"})
        assert rollback.status_code == 200
        assert rollback.json()["release"]["published_version"] == 3
        assert rollback.json()["release"]["is_current"] is True
        duplicate_rollback = client.post(
            f"/api/published-labels/{first.id}/rollback",
            json={"rollback_key": "rollback-1"},
        )
        assert duplicate_rollback.status_code == 200
        assert duplicate_rollback.json()["duplicate"] is True
        second = db.scalar(select(PublishedLabel).where(
            PublishedLabel.content_key == "content-hub:content-001",
            PublishedLabel.status == "published",
        ))
        assert second is not None
        no_op_rollback = client.post(
            f"/api/published-labels/{second.id}/rollback",
            json={"rollback_key": "rollback-current"},
        )
        assert no_op_rollback.status_code == 409
        assert "当前生效版本" in no_op_rollback.json()["detail"]
        rollback_drift = client.post(f"/api/published-labels/{second.id}/rollback", json={"rollback_key": "rollback-1"})
        assert rollback_drift.status_code == 409
        listed = client.get("/api/label-releases").json()["items"]
        current_release = next(item for item in listed if item["id"] == rollback.json()["release"]["id"])
        original_release = next(item for item in listed if item["id"] == release_id)
        assert current_release["is_current"] is True
        assert original_release["is_current"] is False
        assert db.scalar(select(LabelOutboxEvent).where(LabelOutboxEvent.operation == "rolled_back")) is not None
        assert client.get("/api/consumer/v1/reconciliation", headers=headers).json()["outbox_high_watermark"] == 3
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_release_write_endpoints_recover_concurrent_idempotent_requests(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'concurrent-labels.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    session_factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with session_factory() as db:
        user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
            username="concurrent-release-admin",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="并发发布管理员",
        )
        release = LabelRelease(
            release_key="concurrent-publish",
            content_key="content:concurrent",
            category_key="space_image",
            label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
            label_payload_json='{"level":"L2"}',
            payload_hash="a" * 64,
            status="pending_review",
            requested_by=user.username,
        )
        db.add_all([user, release])
        db.commit()
        user_id = user.id
        release_id = release.id

    def test_db():
        with session_factory() as db:
            yield db

    with session_factory() as db:
        detached_user = db.get(User, user_id)
        assert detached_user is not None
        db.expunge(detached_user)

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: detached_user

    def approve() -> tuple[int, dict[str, object]]:
        client = TestClient(app)
        try:
            response = client.post(
                f"/api/label-releases/{release_id}/approve-and-publish"
            )
            return response.status_code, response.json()
        finally:
            client.close()

    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            publish_results = list(pool.map(lambda _index: approve(), range(2)))
        assert [status for status, _payload in publish_results] == [200, 200]
        assert sorted(
            bool(payload["duplicate"])
            for _status, payload in publish_results
        ) == [False, True]

        with session_factory() as db:
            target = db.scalar(
                select(PublishedLabel).where(
                    PublishedLabel.release_id == release_id
                )
            )
            assert target is not None
            next_release = LabelRelease(
                release_key="concurrent-current",
                content_key=target.content_key,
                category_key=target.category_key,
                label_schema_version=target.label_schema_version,
                label_payload_json=target.label_payload_json,
                payload_hash=target.payload_hash,
                status="pending_review",
                requested_by=detached_user.username,
            )
            db.add(next_release)
            db.commit()
            next_release_id = next_release.id
            target_id = target.id

        client = TestClient(app)
        try:
            assert client.post(
                f"/api/label-releases/{next_release_id}/approve-and-publish"
            ).status_code == 200
        finally:
            client.close()

        def rollback() -> tuple[int, dict[str, object]]:
            client = TestClient(app)
            try:
                response = client.post(
                    f"/api/published-labels/{target_id}/rollback",
                    json={"rollback_key": "concurrent-rollback"},
                )
                return response.status_code, response.json()
            finally:
                client.close()

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            rollback_results = list(pool.map(lambda _index: rollback(), range(2)))
        assert [status for status, _payload in rollback_results] == [200, 200]
        assert sorted(
            bool(payload["duplicate"])
            for _status, payload in rollback_results
        ) == [False, True]

        with session_factory() as db:
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(PublishedLabel.id)).where(
                    PublishedLabel.release_id == release_id
                )
            ) == 1
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(LabelRelease.id)).where(
                    LabelRelease.release_key == "concurrent-rollback"
                )
            ) == 1
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_ingress_awaits_material_and_outbox_is_append_only(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, user, result = _database()
    client = _client(db, user)
    token = "test-label-integration-token"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main,
        "settings",
        replace(main.settings, content_ingress_token=token, label_consumer_token=token),
    )
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
    try:
        payload = _ingress_payload(event_id="ing-awaiting", occurred_at=datetime(2026, 8, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc), asset_id=result.asset_id)
        del payload["payload"]["asset_id"]
        received = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=payload)
        assert received.status_code == 200
        assert received.json()["material_required"] is True
        assert received.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is False
        event = db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent).where(ContentIngressEvent.event_id == "ing-awaiting"))
        assert event is not None
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="append-only"):
            db.execute(text("UPDATE content_ingress_events SET status='applied' WHERE id=:id"), {"id": event.id})
            db.commit()
        db.rollback()
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_operator_can_export_current_labels_and_version_history() -> None:
    engine, db, user, result = _database()
    client = _client(db, user)
    try:
        release = client.post(
            "/api/label-releases",
            json={
                "release_key": "export-release-1",
                "evaluation_id": result.id,
                "content_key": None,
            },
        ).json()["release"]
        client.post(f"/api/label-releases/{release['id']}/approve-and-publish")
        first = db.scalar(
            select(PublishedLabel).where(PublishedLabel.release_id == release["id"])
        )
        assert first is not None
        next_release = client.post(
            "/api/label-releases",
            json={
                "release_key": "export-release-2",
                "evaluation_id": result.id,
                "content_key": None,
            },
        ).json()["release"]
        next_published = client.post(
            f"/api/label-releases/{next_release['id']}/approve-and-publish"
        )
        assert next_published.status_code == 200
        client.post(
            f"/api/published-labels/{first.id}/rollback",
            json={"rollback_key": "export-rollback-1"},
        )

        current_csv = client.post(
            "/api/published-labels/export",
            json={"format": "csv", "scope": "current", "category_key": "space_image"},
        )
        assert current_csv.status_code == 200
        assert current_csv.headers["content-disposition"].endswith('.csv"')
        assert current_csv.headers["x-export-row-count"] == "1"
        csv_rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(current_csv.content.decode("utf-8-sig"))))
        assert len(csv_rows) == 1
        assert csv_rows[0]["content_key"] == first.content_key
        assert csv_rows[0]["status"] == "published"
        assert csv_rows[0]["level"] == "L2"
        assert "raw_response" not in current_csv.text

        history_json = client.post(
            "/api/published-labels/export",
            json={"format": "json", "scope": "history"},
        )
        assert history_json.status_code == 200
        assert history_json.json()["schema_version"] == "published-label-export-v1"
        assert history_json.json()["count"] == 3
        assert {item["status"] for item in history_json.json()["items"]} == {
            "published",
            "superseded",
        }

        workbook = client.post(
            "/api/published-labels/export",
            json={"format": "xlsx", "scope": "current"},
        )
        assert workbook.status_code == 200
        assert workbook.content.startswith(b"PK")
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(workbook.content)) as archive:
            assert archive.testzip() is None
            assert "xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" in archive.namelist()
            sheet = archive.read("xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml").decode("utf-8")
            assert "正式标签" not in sheet
            assert "L2" in sheet
            assert "raw_response" not in sheet
        export_audits = db.scalars(
            select(AuditEvent)
            .where(
                AuditEvent.category == "label_export",
                AuditEvent.action == "downloaded",
            )
            .order_by(AuditEvent.id)
        ).all()
        assert len(export_audits) == 3
        assert [json.loads(item.payload_json)["row_count"] for item in export_audits] == [
            1,
            3,
            1,
        ]
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_label_export_requires_release_read_permission_and_valid_filters() -> None:
    engine, db, user, _result = _database()
    client = _client(db, user)
    try:
        app.dependency_overrides.pop(current_user)
        assert client.post("/api/published-labels/export", json={}).status_code == 401
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        assert client.post(
            "/api/published-labels/export", json={"category_key": "INVALID"}
        ).status_code == 422
        assert client.post(
            "/api/published-labels/export",
            json={
                "published_from": "2026-08-02T00:00:00Z",
                "published_to": "2026-08-01T00:00:00Z",
            },
        ).status_code == 422
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_csv_export_neutralizes_spreadsheet_formula_prefixes() -> None:
    label = PublishedLabel(
        release_id=1,
        content_key="=2+3",
        category_key="space_image",
        version=1,
        label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
        label_payload_json=json.dumps({"level": "L1", "score": 99}),
        payload_hash="a" * 64,
        status="published",
        published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    export = build_export([label], format="csv", scope="current")
    row = next(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(export.content.decode("utf-8-sig"))))
    assert row["content_key"] == "'=2+3"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_unique_constraints_129.py =====
"""Schema 129: real unique constraints for global model bindings and prompt versions.

MySQL is not available locally: the MySQL branch is covered by rendered DDL,
the upgrade step source and a fake inspector that exercises the re-entrancy
checks. It still has to be run against a real MySQL (.137 migration container).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, select
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.migrations import mysql_baseline, unique_constraints_129
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS, run_migrations
from app.models import (
    AuditEvent,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelNodeBinding,
    PromptVersion,
    User,
    is_model_node_binding_conflict,
    is_prompt_version_conflict,
)

LONG_VERSION = "v" * 120


def _v128_schema(connection) -> None:
    """The shape a MySQL-upgraded / create_all v128 database has: no working
    NULL-safe binding constraint and no prompt version constraint."""

    connection.exec_driver_sql("""
        CREATE TABLE prompt_versions (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            category_key VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'space_image',
            pipeline_scope VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'shared',
            stage VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
            version VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
            status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'
        )""")
    connection.exec_driver_sql("""
        CREATE TABLE model_node_bindings (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            node_key VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
            model_config_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            category_key VARCHAR(40),
            enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            updated_by VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',
            updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            CONSTRAINT uq_model_node_bindings_node_category UNIQUE (node_key, category_key)
        )""")
    AuditEvent.__table__.create(connection)
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, "
        "name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)")
    for migration in MIGRATIONS:
        if migration.version < 129:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
                (migration.version, migration.name))


def _seed_duplicates(connection) -> None:
    rows = [
        # 正式服两组真实重复（每组 1 条 archived + 1 条 draft/published）
        (77, "model_3d_su", "A", "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820", "archived"),
        (79, "model_3d_su", "A", "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820", "draft"),
        (43, "pdf_text", "A", "prompt-内容安全+信息提取", "archived"),
        (46, "pdf_text", "A", "prompt-内容安全+信息提取", "published"),
        # 两条非 archived：按 id 保留最新的
        (89, "space_image", "B", "multi-live", "archived"),
        (90, "space_image", "B", "multi-live", "draft"),
        (91, "space_image", "B", "multi-live", "published"),
        # 全是 archived：保留最新的原名
        (95, "space_image", "A", "all-archived", "archived"),
        (96, "space_image", "A", "all-archived", "archived"),
        # 满长版本号：截断原名、保留后缀
        (100, "space_image", "A", LONG_VERSION, "archived"),
        (101, "space_image", "A", LONG_VERSION, "draft"),
        # 不同 stage / 类目不算重复
        (110, "space_image", "A", "same-name", "draft"),
        (111, "space_image", "B", "same-name", "draft"),
        (112, "pdf_text", "A", "same-name", "draft"),
    ]
    for row in rows:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO prompt_versions (id, category_key, stage, version, status) "
            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", row)
    for row in [
        (1, "evaluation_main", 7, None),
        (2, "evaluation_main", 8, None),
        (3, "evaluation_main", 9, "space_image"),
        (4, "optimization", 7, None),
    ]:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO model_node_bindings (id, node_key, model_config_id, category_key) "
            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", row)


def _versions(connection) -> dict[int, str]:
    return dict(connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT id, version FROM prompt_versions").all())


def test_v128_ledger_renames_duplicates_and_installs_constraints() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _v128_schema(connection)
            _seed_duplicates(connection)
            connection.commit()
            run_migrations(connection)
            connection.commit()

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations").scalar_one() >= 129  # later migrations (130+) may follow
            versions = _versions(connection)
            assert versions[79] == "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820"
            assert versions[77] == "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820~archived-77"
            assert versions[46] == "prompt-内容安全+信息提取"
            assert versions[43] == "prompt-内容安全+信息提取~archived-43"
            assert versions[91] == "multi-live"
            assert versions[90] == "multi-live~dup-90"
            assert versions[89] == "multi-live~archived-89"
            assert versions[96] == "all-archived"
            assert versions[95] == "all-archived~archived-95"
            assert versions[101] == LONG_VERSION
            assert versions[100] == "v" * (120 - len("~archived-100")) + "~archived-100"
            assert len(versions[100]) == 120
            assert {versions[110], versions[111], versions[112]} == {"same-name"}
            # 不删行
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM prompt_versions").scalar_one() == 14

            # 重复全局绑定保留最小 id（读取与更新一直命中的那条），其余整行进审计
            bindings = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT id FROM model_node_bindings ORDER BY id").scalars().all()
            assert bindings == [1, 3, 4]

            audits = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT event_key, action, subject_id, actor, payload_json FROM audit_events "
                "ORDER BY event_key").all()
            by_key = {row[0]: row for row in audits}
            assert set(by_key) == {
                "schema-129:model_node_binding_removed:2",
                *(f"schema-129:prompt_version_renamed:{item}" for item in (43, 77, 89, 90, 95, 100)),
            }
            renamed_77 = json.loads(by_key["schema-129:prompt_version_renamed:77"][4])
            assert renamed_77 == {
                "id": 77, "status": "archived", "category_key": "model_3d_su", "stage": "A",
                "from_version": "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820",
                "to_version": "model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820~archived-77", "kept_id": 79,
            }
            removed = json.loads(by_key["schema-129:model_node_binding_removed:2"][4])
            assert removed["kept_id"] == 1
            assert removed["row"]["model_config_id"] == 8
            assert {row[3] for row in audits} == {"migration:129"}

            # 约束生效
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError) as prompt_conflict:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO prompt_versions (category_key, stage, version) "
                    "VALUES ('model_3d_su', 'A', 'model-3d-su-a-v4-20260820')")
            assert is_prompt_version_conflict(prompt_conflict.value)
            connection.rollback()
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError) as binding_conflict:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO model_node_bindings (node_key, model_config_id, category_key) "
                    "VALUES ('evaluation_main', 5, NULL)")
            assert is_model_node_binding_conflict(binding_conflict.value)
            connection.rollback()

            # 可重入：再跑一次模块不改名、不重复审计
            report = unique_constraints_129.migrate(connection)
            assert report == {"prompt_versions": [], "model_node_bindings": []}
            assert _versions(connection) == versions
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM audit_events").scalar_one() == len(audits)
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM schema_migrations WHERE version=129").scalar_one() == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_rename_collision_fails_closed() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _v128_schema(connection)
            for row in [(1, "a", "archived"), (2, "a", "draft"), (3, "a~archived-1", "draft")]:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO prompt_versions (id, stage, version, status) VALUES (?, 'A', ?, ?)", row)
            with pytest.raises(unique_constraints_129.UniqueConstraintMigrationError, match="已被 id 3 占用"):
                unique_constraints_129.migrate(connection)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_create_all_database_rejects_concurrent_global_binding(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'bindings.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            run_migrations(connection)
        with Session(engine) as seed:
            seed.add(ModelConfig(name="主评测模型", provider="doubao", model_id="m-1"))
            seed.commit()
        # 两个请求都先查到「没有全局绑定」，再各自插入
        first, second = Session(engine), Session(engine)
        try:
            for session in (first, second):
                assert session.scalar(select(ModelNodeBinding).where(
                    ModelNodeBinding.node_key == "evaluation_main",
                    ModelNodeBinding.category_key.is_(None))) is None
                session.add(ModelNodeBinding(node_key="evaluation_main", model_config_id=1))
            first.commit()
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError) as caught:
                second.commit()
            assert is_model_node_binding_conflict(caught.value)
        finally:
            first.close()
            second.close()
        with Session(engine) as check:
            row = check.scalar(select(ModelNodeBinding))
            assert row.category_scope == ""
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

@pytest.fixture

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'api.db'}")

        user,
        ModelConfig(name="主评测模型", provider="doubao", model_id="m-1", active=True),
        EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key="space_image", display_name="空间图", status="active",
            allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]', preprocess_config_json="{}",
            pipeline_config_json="{}", created_by="admin-129"),
    ])
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield SimpleNamespace(engine=engine, db=db, client=TestClient(app))
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _race_before_flush(db: Session, engine, make_row) -> None:
    """Commit a conflicting row from another connection after the request's
    pre-check but before its INSERT — what a concurrent request does."""

    def interleave(session, _context, _instances):
        with Session(engine) as other:
            other.add(make_row())
            other.commit()

    event.listen(db, "before_flush", interleave, once=True)


def test_update_model_node_race_returns_409(api_env) -> None:
    _race_before_flush(api_env.db, api_env.engine,
                       lambda: ModelNodeBinding(node_key="evaluation_main", model_config_id=1))
    response = api_env.client.put("/api/model-nodes/evaluation_main",
                                  json={"model_config_id": 1, "category_key": None})
    assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "model_node_binding_conflict"
    api_env.db.expire_all()
    assert len(api_env.db.scalars(select(ModelNodeBinding)).all()) == 1
    # 无竞争时照常 upsert
    again = api_env.client.put("/api/model-nodes/evaluation_main",
                               json={"model_config_id": 1, "category_key": None, "enabled": False})
    assert again.status_code == 200, again.text
    assert len(api_env.db.scalars(select(ModelNodeBinding)).all()) == 1


def _prompt_payload(version: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "category_key": "space_image", "pipeline_scope": "shared", "stage": "A",
        "name": "提示词", "version": version,
        "system_prompt": "这是一段足够长的系统提示词，用于测试唯一约束。",
        "user_prompt": "请评测这张图。", "rubric_version": "rubric-v2.1",
    }


def _prompt_row(version: str, status: str = "draft") -> PromptVersion:
    return PromptVersion(category_key="space_image", stage="A", name="并发", version=version,
                         system_prompt="s" * 30, user_prompt="u" * 10, status=status,
                         created_by="other")


def test_create_prompt_race_returns_409(api_env) -> None:
    _race_before_flush(api_env.db, api_env.engine, lambda: _prompt_row("race-v1"))
    response = api_env.client.post("/api/prompts", json=_prompt_payload("race-v1"))
    assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
    assert response.json()["detail"] == "提示词版本号已存在"


def test_create_prompt_rejects_archived_version_number_up_front(api_env) -> None:
    api_env.db.add(_prompt_row("old-v1", status="archived"))
    api_env.db.commit()
    response = api_env.client.post("/api/prompts", json=_prompt_payload("old-v1"))
    assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
    ok = api_env.client.post("/api/prompts", json=_prompt_payload("old-v2"))
    assert ok.status_code == 200, ok.text


def test_unrelated_integrity_errors_are_not_swallowed() -> None:
    other = IntegrityError("INSERT", {}, Exception("FOREIGN KEY constraint failed"))
    assert not is_prompt_version_conflict(other)
    assert not is_model_node_binding_conflict(other)
    mysql_dup = IntegrityError("INSERT", {}, Exception(
        "(1062, \"Duplicate entry 'x' for key 'prompt_versions.uq_prompt_versions_category_stage_version'\")"))
    assert is_prompt_version_conflict(mysql_dup)
    mysql_binding = IntegrityError("INSERT", {}, Exception(
        "(1062, \"Duplicate entry 'x' for key 'model_node_bindings.uq_model_node_bindings_node_scope'\")"))
    assert is_model_node_binding_conflict(mysql_binding)

def test_mysql_baseline_renders_generated_column_and_constraints() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    bindings = str(CreateTable(metadata.tables["model_node_bindings"]).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
    assert "category_scope VARCHAR(40) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs GENERATED ALWAYS AS (COALESCE(category_key, '')) STORED NOT NULL" in bindings
    assert "CONSTRAINT uq_model_node_bindings_node_scope UNIQUE (node_key, category_scope)" in bindings
    prompts = str(CreateTable(metadata.tables["prompt_versions"]).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
    assert "CONSTRAINT uq_prompt_versions_category_stage_version UNIQUE (category_key, stage, version)" in prompts
    # 升级步 ADD COLUMN 追加到表尾：基线里也必须是最后一列，两条路径列序一致
    assert list(metadata.tables["model_node_bindings"].c.keys())[-1] == "category_scope"


def test_mysql_upgrade_step_128_to_129_uses_shared_module() -> None:
    import inspect as _inspect
    import re

    source = _inspect.getsource(mysql_baseline.upgrade_mysql_schema)
    step = re.search(r"if actual == 128 and expected >= 129:(.+?)actual = 129", source, re.S)
    assert step is not None
    assert "from .unique_constraints_129 import migrate" in step.group(1)
    assert "(129, 'model_node_prompt_version_unique')" in step.group(1)
    assert 128 in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_SOURCE_VERSIONS
    assert 129 in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_TARGET_VERSIONS
    assert unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_ADD_CATEGORY_SCOPE == (
        "ALTER TABLE `model_node_bindings` ADD COLUMN `category_scope` VARCHAR(40) "
        "CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs "
        "GENERATED ALWAYS AS (COALESCE(`category_key`, '')) STORED NOT NULL")


class _FakeInspector:
    def __init__(self, state):
        self.state = state

    def get_columns(self, table):
        return [{"name": name} for name in self.state["columns"][table]]

    def get_unique_constraints(self, table):
        return []

    def get_indexes(self, table):
        return [
            {"name": name, "unique": True, "column_names": list(columns)}
            for name, columns in self.state["indexes"][table].items()
        ]


class _FakeMySQLConnection:
    """Records DDL and applies it to ``state`` so re-running sees the result."""

    def __init__(self, state):
        self.state = state
        self.ddl: list[str] = []
        self.dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

    def exec_driver_sql(self, statement):
        self.ddl.append(statement)
        if statement == unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_ADD_CATEGORY_SCOPE:
            self.state["columns"]["model_node_bindings"].append("category_scope")
        elif statement == unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_CREATE_SCOPE_UNIQUE:
            self.state["indexes"]["model_node_bindings"]["uq_model_node_bindings_node_scope"] = (
                "node_key", "category_scope")
        elif statement == unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_CREATE_PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE:
            self.state["indexes"]["prompt_versions"]["uq_prompt_versions_category_stage_version"] = (
                "category_key", "stage", "version")
        else:  # pragma: no cover - unexpected DDL
            raise AssertionError(statement)


def test_mysql_ddl_is_reentrant_after_partial_apply(monkeypatch) -> None:
    state = {
        "columns": {"model_node_bindings": ["id", "node_key", "category_key"]},
        "indexes": {
            "model_node_bindings": {"uq_model_node_bindings_node_category": ("node_key", "category_key")},
            "prompt_versions": {},
        },
    }
    monkeypatch.setattr(unique_constraints_129, "inspect", lambda _conn: _FakeInspector(state))
    tables = {"model_node_bindings", "prompt_versions"}

    # 第一次在建完生成列后「中断」：模拟 DDL 已隐式提交的半截状态
    interrupted = _FakeMySQLConnection(state)
    original = interrupted.exec_driver_sql

    def die_after_first(statement):
        original(statement)
        if len(interrupted.ddl) == 1:
            raise RuntimeError("interrupted")

    interrupted.exec_driver_sql = die_after_first
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        unique_constraints_129._ddl_mysql(interrupted, tables)
    assert interrupted.ddl == [unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_ADD_CATEGORY_SCOPE]

    resumed = _FakeMySQLConnection(state)
    unique_constraints_129._ddl_mysql(resumed, tables)
    assert resumed.ddl == [
        unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_CREATE_SCOPE_UNIQUE,
        unique_constraints_129.MYSQL_CREATE_PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE,
    ]

    done = _FakeMySQLConnection(state)
    unique_constraints_129._ddl_mysql(done, tables)
    assert done.ddl == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_upstream_poll_tick.py =====
"""上游自动拉取 tick：只拉到期的、声明了间隔的活动合同；失败逐合同回滚不影响其它。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import UpstreamReadRun, UpstreamSourceContract

from app import upstream_poll_tick as tick
from app.readonly_sources import ReadOnlySourceError


def _contract(cid: int, interval: int | None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(id=cid, contract_key=f"src-{cid}", status="active", poll_interval_seconds=interval, page_size=50)


def test_polls_due_contracts_with_continuation_cursor(monkeypatch) -> None:
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 2, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    contracts = [_contract(1, 60), _contract(2, 60)]
    last_runs = {
        1: SimpleNamespace(status="succeeded", created_at=now - timedelta(seconds=120), next_cursor_json='{"id": 99}'),
        2: SimpleNamespace(status="succeeded", created_at=now - timedelta(seconds=10), next_cursor_json="{}"),
    }
    calls: list[dict] = []

    class FakeDb:
        def get_bind(self):
            return self

        def scalars(self, _stmt):
            return SimpleNamespace(all=lambda: contracts)

        def commit(self) -> None:
            calls.append({"commit": True})

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            calls.append({"rollback": True})

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "_last_run", lambda db, cid: last_runs[cid])
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "_continuation_cursor", lambda db, cid: tick.SourceCursor(values={"id": 99}) if cid == 1 else None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter", lambda c: "adapter")

    def poll(db, *, contract, adapter, limit, actor, cursor):
        calls.append({"contract": contract.id, "limit": limit, "cursor": cursor.values if cursor else None, "actor": actor})
        return SimpleNamespace(row_count=7)

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "poll_upstream_source", poll)
    summary = tick.poll_due_upstream_sources(FakeDb(), actor="w1", now=now)
    assert summary["polled"] == 1 and summary["rows"] == 7 and summary["skipped"] == 1
    assert calls[0] == {"contract": 1, "limit": 50, "cursor": {"id": 99}, "actor": "w1"}
    assert calls[1] == {"commit": True}


def test_source_error_rolls_back_and_continues(monkeypatch) -> None:
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 2, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    contracts = [_contract(1, 60)]
    events: list[str] = []

    class FakeDb:
        def get_bind(self):
            return self

        def scalars(self, _stmt):
            return SimpleNamespace(all=lambda: contracts)

        def commit(self) -> None:
            events.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            events.append("rollback")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "_last_run", lambda db, cid: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "_continuation_cursor", lambda db, cid: None)

    def resolve(_c):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE", "未配置")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter", resolve)
    summary = tick.poll_due_upstream_sources(FakeDb(), actor="w1", now=now)
    assert summary["errors"] == [{"contract": "src-1", "code": "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"}]
    assert events == ["rollback"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("first_page_state", ["recent", "running", "missing_adapter", "poll_error"])
def test_bounded_scan_reaches_later_contract_across_sessions(monkeypatch, first_page_state):
    """A full early page must not permanently hide the next active source."""
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 2, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            for cid in range(1, 22):
                contract = UpstreamSourceContract(id=cid, contract_key=f"src-{cid}", version=1,
                    adapter_key="fixture", source_system="fixture", category_key="space_image",
                    connection_locator="fixture", secret_reference="", field_mappings_json="{}",
                    cursor_definition_json="{}", page_size=50, poll_interval_seconds=60,
                    schema_fingerprint="a" * 64, owner="test", status="active", contract_hash=str(cid))
                db.add(contract)
                if cid <= 20 and first_page_state in {"recent", "running"}:
                    db.add(UpstreamReadRun(source_contract_id=cid, source_contract_hash=str(cid),
                        category_key="space_image", requested_limit=50,
                        status="running" if first_page_state == "running" else "succeeded", created_at=now))
            db.commit()

        def resolve(contract):
            if contract.id <= 20 and first_page_state == "missing_adapter":
                raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE", "未配置")
            return None

        def poll(db, *, contract, **kwargs):
            run = UpstreamReadRun(source_contract_id=contract.id, source_contract_hash=contract.contract_hash,
                category_key="space_image", requested_limit=50, status="succeeded", row_count=1, created_at=now)
            db.add(run)
            db.flush()
            if contract.id <= 20 and first_page_state == "poll_error":
                raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_FETCH_FAILED", "测试回滚")
            return run

        monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter", resolve)
        monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "poll_upstream_source", poll)
        summaries = []
        for _ in range(3):
            # Worker ticks create fresh sessions; progress must survive that boundary.
            with Session(engine) as db:
                summary = tick.poll_due_upstream_sources(db, actor="test", now=now)
                assert summary["polled"] + summary["skipped"] + len(summary["errors"]) <= 20
                summaries.append(summary)
        # Reaching the end must wrap, so early contracts remain scheduled too.
        if first_page_state in {"recent", "running"}:
            assert summaries[2]["skipped"] == 20
        else:
            assert len(summaries[2]["errors"]) == 20
        with Session(engine) as db:
            reached = db.scalar(select(UpstreamReadRun).where(UpstreamReadRun.source_contract_id == 21))
            assert reached is not None, "第 21 个来源应在后续有界 tick 获得执行机会"
            assert reached.status == "succeeded"
            if first_page_state == "poll_error":
                assert db.scalar(select(UpstreamReadRun).where(UpstreamReadRun.source_contract_id <= 20)) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_upstream_type_short_circuit.py =====
"""上游类型前置短路：内容校验、规则派生、执行器输出、运行时跳过 A/B、收尾定级、真值与增量输入通道、发布接入。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.evaluation_component_catalog import validate_module_content
from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion
from app.upstream_type_short_circuit import (
    UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError,
    effective_short_circuit_rules,
    resolve_upstream_type,
    short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type,
    validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content,
)
from app.workflow_node_executors import _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor
from app.workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result
from tests.test_workflow_runtime import (
    _engine,
    _latest_attempts_for_test,
    _runtime_module,
    _seed_active_workflow,
)


def _content() -> dict:
    return build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit()


def _frozen_node(content: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "upstream_type_short_circuit_0",
        "kind": "rule_module",
        "config": {
            "asset_key": "upstream_type_short_circuit",
            "module_type_key": "upstream_type_short_circuit",
            "rule_module_version_id": 1,
            "input_bindings": {"upstream_input": "upstream.output"},
            "scope": "default",
            "component_snapshot": {"kind": "rule_module", "module_type_key": "upstream_type_short_circuit", "version_id": 1, "version": "1", "content_json": json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False), "content_hash": "x"},
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
    }


def test_content_validation_normalizes_and_rejects_conflicts() -> None:
    content = _content()
    validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(content)
    assert content["rules"][0]["match_any"] == ["全景图", "全景", "panorama", "panoramic"]
    assert validate_module_content("upstream_type_short_circuit", content)["field_path"] == "material_type"
    bad = _content(); bad["rules"].append({"key": "other", "label": "x", "match_any": ["全景图"], "level": "L3"})
    with pytest.raises(UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError) as exc:
        validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(bad)
    assert exc.value.code == "rule_match_value_conflict"
    bad = _content(); bad["rules"][0]["level"] = "L9"
    with pytest.raises(UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError):
        validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(bad)
    bad = _content(); bad["rules"] = []
    with pytest.raises(UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError):
        validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(bad)


def test_rules_derive_from_content_and_match_normalized_output() -> None:
    rules = short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type(_content())
    assert len(rules) == 1 and rules[0]["rule_key"] == "uts:panorama" and rules[0]["target_level"] == "L2"
    assert rules[0]["when"] == {"path": "output.material_type_normalized", "op": "in", "value": ["全景图", "全景", "panorama", "panoramic"]}
    assert rules[0]["skip_roles"] == ["classification", "grading", "level_matching"] and rules[0]["priority"] == 900
    node = _frozen_node(_content())
    assert effective_short_circuit_rules(node) == rules
    node["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = [{"rule_key": "explicit"}]
    assert effective_short_circuit_rules(node) == [{"rule_key": "explicit"}]
    assert effective_short_circuit_rules({"kind": "prompt", "config": {}}) is None


def test_resolve_and_executor_read_material_type_case_insensitively() -> None:
    content = _content()
    assert resolve_upstream_type(content, {"material_type": " Panorama "})["level"] == "L2"
    assert resolve_upstream_type(content, {"material_type": "家具"}) is None
    assert resolve_upstream_type(content, {}) is None
    context = SimpleNamespace(
        node=_frozen_node(content),
        run_snapshot={"runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"asset_id": 1, "material_type": "全景图"}}},
        input_manifest={"asset_id": 1},
    )
    output = _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(context)
    assert output["material_type_normalized"] == "全景图" and output["matched_rule_key"] == "uts:panorama" and output["target_level"] == "L2"
    context.run_snapshot = {"runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"asset_id": 1}}}
    output = _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(context)
    assert output["material_type_normalized"] == "" and output["matched_rule_key"] is None


def test_runtime_skips_prompt_a_and_b_and_finalizer_grades_from_decision(tmp_path) -> None:
    runtime = _runtime_module()
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "upstream-gate.db")
    content = _content()
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            version.workflow_schema_version = "workflow-v3"
            gate = _frozen_node(content)
            version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps([
                {"node_instance_id": "upstream", "kind": "upstream_connector", "config": {"connector_key": "logical.model_3d_su.input"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
                gate,
                {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"execution_role": "classification"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
                {"node_instance_id": "prompt_b", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"execution_role": "grading"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
                {"node_instance_id": "downstream", "kind": "downstream_projection", "config": {"execution_role": "projection"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
            ], ensure_ascii=False)
            version.edge_manifest_json = json.dumps([
                {"from": "upstream", "to": "upstream_type_short_circuit_0"},
                {"from": "upstream_type_short_circuit_0", "to": "prompt_a"},
                {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"},
                {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "downstream"},
            ])
            version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps({
                "queue_class": "validation", "category_key": "model_3d_su", "lane_key": "evaluation",
                "run_modes": ["evaluation"], "level_scale": {"enabled_levels": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]},
            })
            db.commit()
            run = runtime.create_production_run(
                db,
                runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key="upstream-gate-panorama", category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation", runtime_context={"run_mode": "evaluation"},
                    input_manifest={"asset_id": 1, "material_type": "全景图"}, owner="platform",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            upstream_id = runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "gate-worker")
            upstream = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, upstream_id)
            runtime.complete_runtime_step(db, upstream.id, upstream.lease_token, {"asset_id": 1, "material_type": "全景图"})
            gate_id = runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "gate-worker")
            gate_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, gate_id)
            assert gate_attempt.step_key == "upstream_type_short_circuit_0"
            executor_output = _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(SimpleNamespace(
                node=gate, run_snapshot={"runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"asset_id": 1, "material_type": "全景图"}}}, input_manifest={},
            ))
            runtime.complete_runtime_step(db, gate_attempt.id, gate_attempt.lease_token, {"output": executor_output})
            latest = _latest_attempts_for_test(db, run.id)
            assert latest["prompt_a"].status == "skipped" and latest["prompt_a"].last_error_code == "short_circuited"
            assert latest["prompt_b"].status == "skipped"
            assert latest["downstream"].status != "skipped"
            decision = json.loads(latest["upstream_type_short_circuit_0"].output_manifest_json)["short_circuit_decision"]
            assert decision["target_level"] == "L2" and decision["rule_key"] == "uts:panorama"
            assert decision["skipped_nodes"] == ["prompt_a", "prompt_b"]
            manifest = {"nodes": json.loads(version.step_manifest_json), "edges": json.loads(version.edge_manifest_json),
                        "resource_policy": json.loads(version.resource_policy_json), "category_key": "model_3d_su"}
            node_outputs = {key: json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json or "{}") for key, attempt in latest.items()}
            result = reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, node_outputs)
            assert result["level"] == "L2" and result["decision_source"] == "frozen_short_circuit"
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status in {"queued", "running"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_runtime_passes_through_when_type_missing_or_unmatched(tmp_path) -> None:
    runtime = _runtime_module()
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "upstream-gate-miss.db")
    content = _content()
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            version.workflow_schema_version = "workflow-v3"
            gate = _frozen_node(content)
            version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps([
                {"node_instance_id": "upstream", "kind": "upstream_connector", "config": {"connector_key": "logical.model_3d_su.input"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
                gate,
                {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"execution_role": "classification"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
                {"node_instance_id": "downstream", "kind": "downstream_projection", "config": {"execution_role": "projection"}, "input_schema": {"type": "object"}, "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
            ], ensure_ascii=False)
            version.edge_manifest_json = json.dumps([
                {"from": "upstream", "to": "upstream_type_short_circuit_0"},
                {"from": "upstream_type_short_circuit_0", "to": "prompt_a"},
                {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "downstream"},
            ])
            version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps({"queue_class": "validation", "category_key": "model_3d_su", "lane_key": "evaluation", "run_modes": ["evaluation"], "level_scale": {"enabled_levels": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]}})
            db.commit()
            run = runtime.create_production_run(db, runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key="upstream-gate-furniture", category_key="model_3d_su",
                queue_class="validation", runtime_context={"run_mode": "evaluation"}, input_manifest={"asset_id": 2, "material_type": "家具"}, owner="platform"), actor="admin")
            upstream = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "w"))
            runtime.complete_runtime_step(db, upstream.id, upstream.lease_token, {"asset_id": 2, "material_type": "家具"})
            gate_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "w"))
            output = _upstream_type_short_circuit_executor(SimpleNamespace(node=gate, run_snapshot={"runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"asset_id": 2, "material_type": "家具"}}}, input_manifest={}))
            runtime.complete_runtime_step(db, gate_attempt.id, gate_attempt.lease_token, {"output": output})
            latest = _latest_attempts_for_test(db, run.id)
            assert latest["prompt_a"].status == "pending"
            assert "short_circuit_decision" not in json.loads(latest["upstream_type_short_circuit_0"].output_manifest_json)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_level_truth_accepts_material_type_and_rejects_other_workflow_input() -> None:
    from app.main import BaselineSetItemCreateRequest, BaselineTruthEditRequest

    ok = BaselineSetItemCreateRequest(asset_id=1, expected_level="L2", workflow_input={"material_type": "全景图"})
    assert ok.workflow_input == {"material_type": "全景图"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        BaselineSetItemCreateRequest(asset_id=1, expected_level="L2", workflow_input={"source": "domestic"})
    edit = BaselineTruthEditRequest(edit_key="k", reason="补素材类型", expected_level="L2", workflow_input={"material_type": "全景图"})
    assert edit.workflow_input == {"material_type": "全景图"}


def test_baseline_truth_whitelist_and_incremental_passthrough() -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import normalize_baseline_workflow_input
    from app.incremental_input import IncrementalInputError, _with_upstream_material_type

    assert normalize_baseline_workflow_input({"material_type": "全景图"}, truth_contract_key="") == {"material_type": "全景图"}
    assert _with_upstream_material_type({"asset_id": 1}, {"material_type": " 全景图 "}) == {"asset_id": 1, "material_type": "全景图"}
    assert _with_upstream_material_type({"asset_id": 1}, {}) == {"asset_id": 1}
