        _with_upstream_material_type({"asset_id": 1}, {"material_type": 7})


def test_attach_upstream_gate_inserts_node_and_generates_version(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.model_3d_su_upstream_gate_release import attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    from app.workflow_working_copies import editable_workflow_template, get_or_create_workflow_working_copy
    from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == "model_3d_su.evaluation"))
            template = editable_workflow_template(db, definition.id)
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, initial_manifest=template.manifest, actor="system:gate")
            db.commit()
            preview = attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(db, actor="system:gate", apply=False)
            db.rollback()
            assert preview["mode"] == "preview" and preview["node"]["inserted"] is True
            assert preview["node_order"][:3] == ["upstream", "upstream_type_short_circuit_0", "prompt_a"]
            receipt = attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(db, actor="system:gate", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            assert receipt["validation_ok"] is True, receipt.get("validation_errors")
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, receipt["workflow_version_id"])
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            ids = [node["node_instance_id"] for node in manifest["nodes"]]
            assert ids[:3] == ["upstream", "upstream_type_short_circuit_0", "prompt_a"]
            edges = {(edge["from"], edge["to"]) for edge in manifest["edges"]}
            assert ("upstream", "upstream_type_short_circuit_0") in edges and ("upstream_type_short_circuit_0", "prompt_a") in edges
            gate = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["node_instance_id"] == "upstream_type_short_circuit_0")
            assert gate["config"]["component_snapshot"]["module_type_key"] == "upstream_type_short_circuit"
            assert effective_short_circuit_rules(gate)[0]["target_level"] == "L2"
            # 幂等：再次应用只刷新模块版本，不重复插节点
            again = attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(db, actor="system:gate", apply=False)
            db.rollback()
            assert again["node"]["inserted"] is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_create_level_baseline_set_freezes_material_type(tmp_path) -> None:
    """建基准集时等级真值条目可直接带 material_type，冻结进快照并被生效真值读出。"""
    from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from app.main import BaselineSetCreateRequest, BaselineSetItemCreateRequest, create_baseline_set
    from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, User
    from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            user = User(username="ops", password_hash="unused", display_name="运营", is_admin=True, role="admin")
            asset = Asset(original_name="全景_1.jpg", stored_name="p1.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=10, sha256="3" * 64, category_key="model_3d_su")
            db.add_all([user, asset])
            db.flush()
            summary = create_baseline_set(
                BaselineSetCreateRequest(name="全景图前置短路核验", category_key="model_3d_su", default_expected_level="L2",
                    items=[BaselineSetItemCreateRequest(asset_id=asset.id, expected_level="L2", workflow_input={"material_type": "全景图"})]),
                user, db,
            )
            item = db.scalar(select(BaselineSetItem).where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id == summary["id"]))
            assert json.loads(item.asset_snapshot_json)["workflow_input"] == {"material_type": "全景图"}
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            assert effective.expected_level == "L2" and effective.workflow_input == {"material_type": "全景图"}
            assert db.get(BaselineSet, summary["id"]) is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_v3_only_migration.py =====
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.dimension_schema_registry import space_schema_definition_for_version
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    DimensionSchema,
    User,
)


@pytest.fixture()
def api_db():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(
        username="v3-only-admin",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="v3-only 管理员",
        role="admin",
        is_admin=True,
    )
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield TestClient(app), db, user
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_full_enqueue_fails_closed_without_active_v3_config(api_db) -> None:
    client, _db, _user = api_db

    response = client.post(
        "/api/jobs/enqueue",
        json={"asset_ids": [999], "category_key": "space_image"},
    )

    assert response.status_code == 409
    assert response.json()["detail"] == {
        "code": "v3_active_config_missing",
        "message": "类目 space_image 缺少 active v3 合同，已拒绝回退 v1",
    }


def test_baseline_run_fails_closed_without_active_v3_config(api_db) -> None:
    client, db, user = api_db
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="baseline.jpg",
        stored_name="baseline.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    baseline_set = BaselineSet(
        name="v3 only",
        description="",
        category_key="space_image",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="b" * 64,
        created_by=user.username,
    )
    db.add(baseline_set)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level="L1",
            asset_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"id": asset.id}),
        )
    )
    db.commit()

    response = client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline_set.id}/runs")

    assert response.status_code == 409
    assert response.json()["detail"] == {
        "code": "v3_active_config_missing",
        "message": "类目 space_image 缺少 active v3 合同，已拒绝回退 v1",
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("method", "path", "json_body"),
    [
        (
            "post",
            "/api/dimension-schemas",
            {
                "schema_key": "legacy.schema",
                "version": "1",
                "schema_type": "family_pack",
                "family_key": "space",
                "display_name": "旧维度",
                "definition": space_schema_definition_for_version("1.3.0"),
            },
        ),
        (
            "put",
            "/api/dimension-schemas/999",
            {
                "display_name": "旧维度",
                "definition": space_schema_definition_for_version("1.3.0"),
            },
        ),
        ("delete", "/api/dimension-schemas/999", None),
        ("post", "/api/dimension-schemas/999/publish", None),
    ],
)
def test_dimension_schema_writes_are_gone(
    api_db, method: str, path: str, json_body: dict | None
) -> None:
    client, _db, _user = api_db

    response = client.request(method, path, json=json_body)

    assert response.status_code == 410
    assert response.json()["detail"] == {
        "code": "legacy_dimension_write_retired",
        "message": "旧类目维度写入口已下线，请使用“类目评测 v3 合同配置”。",
    }


def test_category_profile_dimension_changes_are_gone(api_db) -> None:
    client, _db, _user = api_db
    profile = next(
        item
        for item in client.get("/api/evaluation-categories").json()["items"]
        if item["category_key"] == "space_image"
    )
    pipeline = dict(profile["pipeline_config"])
    pipeline["dimensions"] = {
        "enabled": False,
        "mode": "none",
        "selected_keys": [],
        "enabled_keys": [],
    }

    response = client.put(
        "/api/evaluation-categories/space_image",
        json={
            **{key: profile[key] for key in (
                "display_name", "description", "status", "allowed_mime_types",
                "preprocess_config", "prompt_a_id", "prompt_b_id",
                "model_config_id", "rubric_version", "dimension_schema_key",
                "dimension_schema_version", "automation_config",
            )},
            "pipeline_config": pipeline,
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 410
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "legacy_dimension_write_retired"


def test_dimension_schema_reads_remain_available_for_history(api_db) -> None:
    client, db, _user = api_db
    schema = DimensionSchema(
        schema_key="history.schema",
        version="1",
        schema_type="family_pack",
        family_key="space",
        display_name="历史维度",
        status="published",
        definition_json="{}",
        canonical_hash="c" * 64,
        published_by="v3-only-admin",
        published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add(schema)
    db.commit()

    listed = client.get("/api/dimension-schemas")
    detailed = client.get("/api/dimension-schemas/history.schema/versions/1")

    assert listed.status_code == 200
    assert listed.json()["items"][0]["schema_key"] == "history.schema"
    assert detailed.status_code == 200
    assert detailed.json()["definition"] == {}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_v3_projection_guard.py =====
"""写入时挡住「运行时合同改了、冻结版本没跟上」——2026-09-03 全站宕机的形态。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.category_evaluation_v3_projection_guard import (
    ProjectedRevisionOutOfSyncError,
    assert_projection_synced,
    reconciling_projection,
)
from app.category_evaluation_v3_revisions import (
    ensure_projected_revision,
    sync_projected_revision,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config, CategoryEvaluationV3Revision

CONTRACT = '{"anchors":["a","b"],"spec_version":"guard-test-v1"}'
NEXT_CONTRACT = '{"anchors":["a"],"spec_version":"guard-test-v1"}'


def _session(tmp_path, name="guard.db"):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine, Session(engine)


def _seed(db) -> CategoryEvaluationV3Config:
    row = CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
        category_key="guard_demo",
        display_name="护栏演示",
        status="active",
        contract_json=CONTRACT,
        classification_map_json="{}",
        subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
        dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
        media_penalty_enabled=False,
        revision=1,
        contract_hash="hash-1",
        created_by="system:test",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    ensure_projected_revision(db, row)
    db.commit()
    return row


def test_editing_the_runtime_row_without_syncing_is_refused(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path)
    try:
        row = _seed(db)
        frozen_id = row.projected_revision_id
        assert frozen_id is not None

        # 事故复现：直接改运行时合同，不动冻结版本。
        row.contract_json = NEXT_CONTRACT
        row.contract_hash = "hash-2"
        with pytest.raises(ProjectedRevisionOutOfSyncError) as err:
            db.flush()
        # 报错要指名道姓：哪个类目、差在哪、该怎么改
        assert err.value.category_key == "guard_demo"
        assert "合同" in err.value.detail
        assert "sync_projected_revision" in str(err.value)

        db.rollback()
        # 库里没被写脏：重启时读到的仍是一致的一对
        fresh = db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config))
        assert fresh.contract_json == CONTRACT
        assert fresh.projected_revision_id == frozen_id
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_the_sanctioned_path_appends_a_revision_and_passes(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "sync.db")
    try:
        row = _seed(db)
        first_id = row.projected_revision_id

        sync_projected_revision(
            db, row,
            display_name="护栏演示",
            status="active",
            contract_json=NEXT_CONTRACT,
            classification_map_json="{}",
            subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
            dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
            media_penalty_enabled=False,
            contract_hash="hash-2",
            actor="system:test",
        )
        db.commit()

        assert row.contract_json == NEXT_CONTRACT
        assert row.projected_revision_id != first_id
        assert row.revision == 2
        # 旧版本留着，可溯源
        revisions = db.scalars(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).order_by(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision
            )
        ).all()
        assert [r.revision for r in revisions] == [1, 2]
        assert revisions[0].contract_json == CONTRACT
        assert revisions[1].parent_revision_id == first_id
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_a_dangling_revision_pointer_is_refused(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "dangling.db")
    try:
        row = _seed(db)
        row.projected_revision_id = 9999
        with pytest.raises(ProjectedRevisionOutOfSyncError) as err:
            db.flush()
        assert "指向的版本不存在" in err.value.detail
    finally:
        db.rollback(); db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_untouched_projection_fields_do_not_trip_the_guard(tmp_path) -> None:
    """只改不进冻结版本的字段（比如 updated_at）不该被拦。"""

    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "quiet.db")
    try:
        row = _seed(db)
        row.updated_at = row.created_at
        db.flush()
        db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_reconciling_scope_is_scoped_not_global(tmp_path) -> None:
    """豁免只在 with 块内生效，出块后立刻恢复拦截。"""

    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "scope.db")
    try:
        row = _seed(db)
        with reconciling_projection():
            row.contract_json = NEXT_CONTRACT
            db.flush()
        row.contract_hash = "hash-3"
        with pytest.raises(ProjectedRevisionOutOfSyncError):
            db.flush()
    finally:
        db.rollback(); db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_startup_upgrade_keeps_the_frozen_revision_in_step(tmp_path) -> None:
    """启动时跑的 v3 规则升级，改完运行时行必须同时挪冻结版本指针。

    这条链原本直接改行、手动 revision += 1，指针留在升级前的版本上。它在
    seed_defaults 里每次启动都跑，只要真有行要升级，下一次重启就会被冷启动
    自检拒绝——正是 2026-09-03 全站不可用的形态。
    """

    import json as _json

    from app.category_evaluation_v3_revisions import ensure_projected_revision
    from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
        build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
        build_inspiration_v3_contract,
    )
    from app.migrations.upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction import (
        upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction,
    )

    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "upgrade.db")
    try:
        contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
        contract["common_modifiers"]["media_type_penalty"].pop("enabled")
        dimensions = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()
        for config in dimensions.values():
            for dimension in config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]:
                dimension.pop("deduction_rules")
        row = CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
            category_key="inspiration_image",
            display_name="灵感图",
            status="active",
            contract_json=_json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
            classification_map_json='{"legacy":true}',
            subcategory_dimensions_json=_json.dumps(dimensions, ensure_ascii=False),
            revision=7,
            contract_hash="old",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        ensure_projected_revision(db, row)
        db.commit()

        result = upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction(db)
        db.commit()
        assert result["changed_keys"] == ["inspiration_image"]

        # 关键断言：升级后运行时行与它指向的冻结版本仍逐字段一致，
        # 也就是「下一次重启起得来」。
        assert_projection_synced(db, row)
        ensure_projected_revision(db, row)

        # 旧版本留档，可溯源到升级前的内容
        revisions = db.scalars(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).order_by(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision
            )
        ).all()
        assert [r.revision for r in revisions] == [7, 8]
        assert revisions[0].contract_hash == "old"
        assert row.projected_revision_id == revisions[1].id
    finally:
        db.rollback(); db.close(); engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_v3_replay_aesthetic_foundation.py =====
"""纠偏重放必须保住调用B美感基础分。

背景：`recompute_qualified_v3` 是所有节点纠偏的重算入口。它曾经既没有开启
`require_foundation`，也没有把调用B的 `aesthetic_score` 作为 `initial_score`
传给撮合器，于是任何触发重放的纠偏都会从「维度满分」重新起算——输入一个字段
都不改，86 分 / L2 会被抬成 100 分 / L5 反向的 L1。爆炸半径是维度规则、
precheck 字段、红线、赛道四类纠偏（调用A字段与最终等级不触发重放）。

这批用例把三条边界一起钉住，避免再次回退：
1. 有美感分时重放必须幂等（原缺陷）；
2. 没有美感分的历史结果不能因此纠不了偏（修复时容易引入的反向回归）；
3. 赛道纠偏会重置维度集合，但美感分属于图片、不属于赛道，不能跟着被清掉。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.category_evaluation_aggregator import aggregate_category_evaluation
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import (
    compose_rule_deductions,
    empty_deduction_output,
)
from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)

AESTHETIC_SCORE = 86


def _context() -> dict:
    return {
        "contract": build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        "classification_map": build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        "config_revision": 3,
    }


def _precheck() -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": "建筑设计",
            "primary_confidence": 0.96,
        },
        "production_fields": {"reason": [], "trait": "实景照片"},
        "hard_defects": [],
    }


def _output_with_foundation(config: dict) -> dict:
    """复刻一条正常评测落库的调用B输出：带美感分与可见证据。"""
    output = empty_deduction_output(config)
    output.update(
        {
            "aesthetic_score": AESTHETIC_SCORE,
            "aesthetic_evidence": ["构图均衡，主体清晰"],
            "aesthetic_confidence": 0.9,
            "raw_payload": {"provider": "fake"},
        }
    )
    # 正常结果没有失败告警；留着会被识别为「调用B失败」哨兵。
    output.pop("warning", None)
    return output


def _original_aggregate(context: dict, precheck: dict, output: dict) -> dict:
    """复刻 worker_v3_authoritative 的原始评测算法，作为重放的对照基准。"""
    config = context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    composed = compose_rule_deductions(
        config=config,
        dimension_output=output,
        require_foundation=True,
    )
    return aggregate_category_evaluation(
        context["contract"],
        precheck,
        composed,
        track_key="class_one",
        initial_score=composed.get("aesthetic_score"),
    )


def test_replay_keeps_call_b_score_so_corrections_do_not_inflate() -> None:
    """输入一字未改时，重放结果必须与原始评测完全一致。"""
    context = _context()
    precheck = _precheck()
    output = _output_with_foundation(context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"])

    original = _original_aggregate(context, precheck, output)
    replayed = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=output,
    )

    assert original["score"] == AESTHETIC_SCORE, "对照基准本身应以美感分为初始分"
    assert replayed["score"] == original["score"], (
        "重放丢失调用B美感分会把分数抬回维度满分，使每次纠偏都虚高"
    )
    assert replayed["level"] == original["level"]
    assert replayed["initial_score"] == AESTHETIC_SCORE


def test_replay_still_works_for_rows_stored_without_call_b_score() -> None:
    """美感基座之前落库的历史结果不能因为缺美感分就纠不了偏。"""
    context = _context()
    precheck = _precheck()
    # 没有 aesthetic_score，等同于美感基座上线前的历史行。
    legacy_output = empty_deduction_output(
        context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    )

    replayed = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=legacy_output,
    )

    assert replayed["initial_score"] is None, "历史行没有美感分，应走旧的基准分口径"
    assert isinstance(replayed["score"], (int, float))
    assert replayed["level"].startswith("L")


def test_track_correction_keeps_call_b_score_when_dimensions_reset() -> None:
    """改赛道会重置维度命中，但美感分属于图片本身，必须保留。"""
    context = _context()
    precheck = _precheck()
    output = _output_with_foundation(context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"])

    replayed = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=output,
        track_key="class_two",
    )

    assert replayed["track_key"] == "class_two"
    assert replayed["initial_score"] == AESTHETIC_SCORE, (
        "赛道纠偏重置维度集合时，不能把调用B美感分一起清掉"
    )


def test_redline_replay_reports_hit_without_needing_call_b_score() -> None:
    """红线在维度之前终止，缺少美感分也必须能重放。"""
    context = _context()
    precheck = _precheck()
    precheck["production_fields"]["reason"] = ["是截图"]
    output = empty_deduction_output(context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"])

    replayed = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=output,
    )

    assert replayed["hit_rules"], "命中红线应记录命中规则"
    assert replayed["caps"], "红线必须留下封顶证据"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_vision_animation_first_frame.py =====
"""动图首帧策略：显式声明后，显式图片策略路径也能处理 GIF。

背景（2026-09-03）：既定业务规则是「GIF 取首帧送审」。legacy-default 路径
通过 ModelMediaPolicy.target_max_frames=1 实现；但待评图迁移到显式
vision-input-policy-v1 后走 _prepare_explicit，该能力丢失，动图直接失败。
新链路刻意拒绝「静默」丢帧，因此补一个**显式**的 first_frame 策略。
"""

import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory

from PIL import Image

from app.media import prepare_vision_geometry
from app.vision_input_policy import (
    FirstFrameAnimation,
    OriginalPreprocess,
    RejectAnimation,
    ShortEdgePreprocess,
    VisionRolePolicy,
    DetailFidelity,
    parse_vision_input_policy,
    SEED_20_PROFILE,
    parse_vision_capability_profile,
)


def _capability():
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(
        SEED_20_PROFILE, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"
    )


def _write_gif(path: Path, frames: int) -> None:
    images = [
        Image.new("RGB", (64, 48), (index * 20 % 255, 90, 120))
        for index in range(frames)
    ]
    images[0].save(path, save_all=True, append_images=images[1:], duration=80, loop=0)


class AnimationPolicyParsingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_role_defaults_to_first_frame_animation(self):
        policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
            {
                "version": "vision-input-policy-v1",
                "provider_profile": SEED_20_PROFILE["profile_key"],
                "roles": {
                    "target": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                    "anchor": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                },
                "transport": {"mode": "lossless", "size_limit": "provider_profile"},
            },
            capability=_capability(),
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

        self.assertIsInstance(policy.roles["target"].animation, FirstFrameAnimation)

    def test_default_animation_is_omitted_from_serialization(self):
        """已冻结工作流的 policy hash 不能因新增字段而改变。"""
        role = VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"), preprocess=OriginalPreprocess()
        )

        self.assertNotIn("animation", role.as_dict())

    def test_explicit_reject_round_trips(self):
        """默认(first_frame)不落盘；显式 reject 是偏离默认，必须落盘可审计。"""
        role = VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
            preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
            animation=RejectAnimation(),
        )

        self.assertEqual({"mode": "reject"}, role.as_dict()["animation"])


class AnimationGeometryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_animation_rejected_when_explicitly_configured(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=5)

            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                prepare_vision_geometry(
                    source,
                    mime_type="image/gif",
                    content_sha256="deadbeef",
                    cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                    preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                    animation=RejectAnimation(),
                )

    def test_first_frame_converts_animation_to_static_png(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=5)

            path, mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/gif",
                content_sha256="deadbeef",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                animation=FirstFrameAnimation(),
            )

            self.assertEqual("image/png", mime)
            with Image.open(path) as produced:
                self.assertEqual(1, int(getattr(produced, "n_frames", 1) or 1))
                self.assertEqual((64, 48), produced.size)

    def test_first_frame_is_the_first_frame_not_a_later_one(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=5)
            with Image.open(source) as animation:
                animation.seek(0)
                expected = animation.convert("RGB").getpixel((0, 0))

            path, _ = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/gif",
                content_sha256="deadbeef",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                animation=FirstFrameAnimation(),
            )

            with Image.open(path) as produced:
                self.assertEqual(expected, produced.convert("RGB").getpixel((0, 0)))

    def test_first_frame_then_applies_geometry_policy(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=3)

            path, mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/gif",
                content_sha256="deadbeef",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(24),
                animation=FirstFrameAnimation(),
            )

            self.assertEqual("image/png", mime)
            with Image.open(path) as produced:
                self.assertEqual(24, min(produced.size))

    def test_static_image_is_untouched_by_first_frame_policy(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (40, 30), (10, 20, 30)).save(source)

            path, mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/png",
                content_sha256="cafe",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                animation=FirstFrameAnimation(),
            )

            self.assertEqual(source, path)
            self.assertEqual("image/png", mime)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class AnimationEndToEndTransportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """首帧产物必须能通过后续的无损传输 MIME 门禁（GIF 不在 allowed_mime_types）。"""

    def test_first_frame_output_passes_lossless_transport(self):
        from app.lossless_image_transport import prepare_lossless_transport
        import hashlib

        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=4)

            frame_path, frame_mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/gif",
                content_sha256="feed",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "geometry",
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                animation=FirstFrameAnimation(),
            )
            digest = hashlib.sha256(frame_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

            result = prepare_lossless_transport(
                frame_path,
                digest,
                frame_mime,
                _capability(),
                Path(tmp) / "transport",
            )

            self.assertTrue(result.pixel_equal)
            self.assertLessEqual(result.sent_bytes, _capability().max_single_image_bytes)

    def test_explicit_reject_still_blocks_end_to_end(self):
        """显式 reject 必须仍然拒绝：需要严格拒绝动图的类目保留退路。"""
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=4)

            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                prepare_vision_geometry(
                    source,
                    mime_type="image/gif",
                    content_sha256="feed",
                    cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "geometry",
                    preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(32),
                    animation=RejectAnimation(),
                )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_vision_capability_equivalence.py =====
"""图片能力档案比对必须按规范形态，不能被 150 与 150.0 这种字面量差异判成漂移。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from app.vision_input_policy import (
    SEED_20_PROFILE,
    parse_vision_capability_profile,
    same_vision_capability,
)

PROVIDER = "doubao"
MODEL = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"


def _profile(**over):
    raw = json.loads(json.dumps(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)))
    raw.update(over)
    return raw


def test_integer_and_float_aspect_ratio_are_equivalent() -> None:
    """设计案例工作流点检查流程报「档案不一致」的真实原因。"""

    frozen = _profile(max_aspect_ratio=150)          # 注册表里存的整数
    current = _profile(max_aspect_ratio=150.0)       # 解析后的浮点
    assert json.dumps(frozen, sort_keys=True) != json.dumps(current, sort_keys=True)
    assert same_vision_capability(frozen, current, provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)


def test_parsed_object_and_raw_mapping_compare_equal() -> None:
    raw = _profile(max_aspect_ratio=150)
    parsed = parse_vision_capability_profile(raw, provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)
    assert same_vision_capability(raw, parsed, provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)
    assert same_vision_capability(parsed, raw, provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)
    assert same_vision_capability(parsed, parsed, provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)


def test_real_capability_differences_are_still_caught() -> None:
    """放宽只针对等价形态，真实差异仍必须报漂移。"""

    base = _profile()
    for field, value in (
        ("max_source_pixels", 12_000_000),
        ("max_frames", 4),
        ("min_dimension_pixels", 32),
        ("allowed_mime_types", ["image/jpeg"]),
        ("detail_modes", ["low", "high"]),
    ):
        assert not same_vision_capability(
            base, _profile(**{field: value}), provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL
        ), field


def test_unparseable_snapshot_falls_back_to_literal_compare() -> None:
    broken = {"version": "vision-capability-profile-v1", "profile_key": "x"}
    assert same_vision_capability(broken, dict(broken), provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)
    assert not same_vision_capability(broken, _profile(), provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)
    assert not same_vision_capability(None, _profile(), provider=PROVIDER, model_id=MODEL)

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError

import pytest

from app.vision_input_policy import (
    SEED_20_PROFILE,
    CustomPreprocess,
    DetailFidelity,
    OriginalPreprocess,
    PixelLimitFidelity,
    ShortEdgePreprocess,
    VisionInputPolicyError,
    default_vision_input_policy,
    estimate_image_tokens,
    parse_vision_capability_profile,
    parse_vision_input_policy,
    seed_20_capability,
    vision_policy_hash,
)


SEED_MODEL_ID = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215"


def _capability():
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(
        SEED_20_PROFILE,
        provider="doubao",
        model_id=SEED_MODEL_ID,
    )


def _policy_dict() -> dict:
    return default_vision_input_policy(_capability()).as_dict()


def test_new_policy_defaults_to_high_original_target_and_pixel_limited_anchor() -> None:
    """目标图原图 high；锚点图默认降像素（≤1024²、短边 1024，2026-09-02 A/B 后定为默认）。"""
    capability = _capability()

    policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
        None,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )

    assert policy.version == "vision-input-policy-v1"
    assert policy.provider_profile == capability.profile_key
    assert policy.roles["target"].fidelity == DetailFidelity("high")
    assert policy.roles["anchor"].fidelity == PixelLimitFidelity(196 * 196, 1024 * 1024)
    assert policy.roles["target"].preprocess == OriginalPreprocess()
    assert policy.roles["anchor"].preprocess == ShortEdgePreprocess(1024)
    assert policy.transport.as_dict() == {
        "mode": "lossless",
        "size_limit": "provider_profile",
    }


def test_missing_policy_on_historical_workflow_is_explicit_legacy_default() -> None:
    policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
        None,
        capability=_capability(),
        legacy_if_missing=True,
    )

    assert policy.version == "legacy-default"
    assert policy.is_legacy_default is True
    assert policy.roles["target"].fidelity == DetailFidelity("high")
    assert policy.roles["anchor"].preprocess == ShortEdgePreprocess(600)
    assert policy.as_dict()["version"] == "legacy-default"


def test_pixel_limit_is_mutually_exclusive_with_detail() -> None:
    raw = _policy_dict()
    raw["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"] = {
        "mode": "pixel_limit",
        "min_pixels": 3136,
        "max_pixels": 3_014_080,
        "value": "high",
    }

    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
        parse_vision_input_policy(
            raw,
            capability=_capability(),
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

    assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_fidelity_fields_conflict"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "minimum,maximum",
    [
        (195, 3136),
        (3136, 36_000_001),
        (4000, 3999),
        (True, 3136),
    ],
)
def test_pixel_limit_enforces_provider_bounds(minimum, maximum) -> None:
    raw = _policy_dict()
    raw["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"] = {
        "mode": "pixel_limit",
        "min_pixels": minimum,
        "max_pixels": maximum,
    }

    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
        parse_vision_input_policy(
            raw,
            capability=_capability(),
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

    assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_pixel_limit_invalid"


def test_pixel_limit_requires_provider_support() -> None:
    profile = copy.deepcopy(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE))
    profile["profile_key"] = "explicit-no-pixel-limit-v1"
    profile["pixel_limit_supported"] = False
    capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
        profile,
        provider="custom-provider",
        model_id="custom-vision-model",
    )
    raw = default_vision_input_policy(capability).as_dict()
    raw["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"] = {
        "mode": "pixel_limit",
        "min_pixels": 3136,
        "max_pixels": 3_014_080,
    }

    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
        parse_vision_input_policy(
            raw,
            capability=capability,
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

    assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_pixel_limit_unsupported"


def test_detail_mode_must_be_declared_by_capability() -> None:

    profile["profile_key"] = "explicit-high-only-v1"

    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
        parse_vision_input_policy(

            capability=capability,
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

    assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_fidelity_unsupported"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "preprocess",
    [
        {"mode": "short_edge", "pixels": 14},
        {"mode": "short_edge", "pixels": 6001},
        {
            "mode": "custom",
            "max_width": 6000,
            "max_height": 6001,
            "allow_upscale": False,
        },
        {
            "mode": "custom",
            "max_width": 1280,
            "max_height": 720,
            "allow_upscale": True,
        },
    ],
)
def test_preprocess_bounds_and_no_upscale_are_strict(preprocess: dict) -> None:
    raw = _policy_dict()
    raw["roles"]["target"]["preprocess"] = preprocess

    with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
        parse_vision_input_policy(
            raw,
            capability=_capability(),
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

    assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_preprocess_invalid"


def test_valid_preprocess_variants_round_trip() -> None:
    raw = _policy_dict()
    raw["roles"]["target"]["preprocess"] = {
        "mode": "short_edge",
        "pixels": 600,
    }
    raw["roles"]["anchor"]["preprocess"] = {
        "mode": "custom",
        "max_width": 1920,
        "max_height": 1080,
        "allow_upscale": False,
    }

    policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
        raw,
        capability=_capability(),
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )

    assert policy.roles["target"].preprocess == ShortEdgePreprocess(600)
    assert policy.roles["anchor"].preprocess == CustomPreprocess(
        max_width=1920,
        max_height=1080,
        allow_upscale=False,
    )
    assert policy.as_dict() == raw


def test_policy_copies_roles_into_an_immutable_mapping() -> None:
    raw = _policy_dict()
    policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
        raw,
        capability=_capability(),
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )

    raw["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"]["value"] = "low"

    assert policy.roles["target"].fidelity == DetailFidelity("high")
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        policy.roles["target"] = policy.roles["anchor"]  # type: ignore[index]
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        policy.roles["target"].fidelity = DetailFidelity("low")  # type: ignore[misc]
