def test_capability_validates_mime_dimensions_aspect_and_frame_count() -> None:
    capability = _capability()

    capability.validate_media(
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        width=2560,
        height=1440,
        frame_count=1,
    )

    cases = [
        ("image/bmp", 2560, 1440, 1, "media_mime_type_unsupported"),
        ("image/jpeg", 14, 1440, 1, "media_pixel_constraints_invalid"),
        ("image/jpeg", 7000, 6000, 1, "media_pixel_constraints_invalid"),
        ("image/jpeg", 1501, 10, 1, "media_pixel_constraints_invalid"),
        ("image/jpeg", 2560, 1440, 2, "media_frame_limit_exceeded"),
    ]
    for mime, width, height, frames, code in cases:
        with pytest.raises(VisionInputPolicyError) as excinfo:
            capability.validate_media(
                mime_type=mime,
                width=width,
                height=height,
                frame_count=frames,
            )
        assert excinfo.value.code == code


def test_seed_20_high_estimate_is_fixed_1280() -> None:
    capability = _capability()
    role = default_vision_input_policy(capability).roles["target"]

    assert estimate_image_tokens(capability, role, 400, 300) == 1280
    assert estimate_image_tokens(capability, role, 4096, 4096) == 1280


def test_low_xhigh_and_pixel_limit_use_versioned_formula() -> None:
    capability = _capability()
    original = OriginalPreprocess()

    assert estimate_image_tokens(
        capability,
        role_policy=type(default_vision_input_policy(capability).roles["target"])(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("low"),
            preprocess=original,
        ),
        width=2560,
        height=1440,
    ) == 1280
    assert estimate_image_tokens(
        capability,
        role_policy=type(default_vision_input_policy(capability).roles["target"])(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("xhigh"),
            preprocess=original,
        ),
        width=400,
        height=300,
    ) == 1280
    assert estimate_image_tokens(
        capability,
        role_policy=type(default_vision_input_policy(capability).roles["target"])(
            fidelity=PixelLimitFidelity(3136, 3_014_080),
            preprocess=original,
        ),
        width=2560,
        height=1440,
    ) == 1709


def test_policy_hash_is_canonical_and_changes_with_role_policy() -> None:
    capability = _capability()
    original = _policy_dict()
    reordered = {
        "transport": original["transport"],
        "roles": {
            "anchor": original["roles"]["anchor"],
            "target": original["roles"]["target"],
        },
        "provider_profile": original["provider_profile"],
        "version": original["version"],
    }
    first = parse_vision_input_policy(
        original,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )
    second = parse_vision_input_policy(
        reordered,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )

    assert vision_policy_hash(first, capability) == vision_policy_hash(
        second,
        capability,
    )

    changed = copy.deepcopy(original)
    changed["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"]["value"] = "low"
    third = parse_vision_input_policy(
        changed,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )
    assert vision_policy_hash(first, capability) != vision_policy_hash(
        third,
        capability,
    )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_vision_input_policy_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import CheckConstraint, create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import (
    _migration_085_add_workflow_model_media_usage,
    _migration_094_add_vision_input_policy_evidence,
)


MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS = {
    "fidelity_mode",
    "fidelity_value",
    "preprocess_mode",
    "preprocess_value",
    "policy_hash",
    "capability_profile_key",
    "normalized_pixel_hash",
    "compression_ms",
    "saved_bytes",
    "saved_ratio_ppm",
    "provider_max_bytes",
    "operational_max_bytes",
    "size_gate_result",
    "estimation_formula_version",
}


def _legacy_engine():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE model_configs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(120))"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE model_registry_entries "
            "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(120))"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE assets (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations(id) VALUES (1)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO assets(id) VALUES (1)")
        _migration_085_add_workflow_model_media_usage(connection)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            """
            INSERT INTO workflow_model_media_usage (
                invocation_id, asset_id, media_role, ordinal, anchor_level,
                original_width, original_height, original_bytes, original_format,
                sent_width, sent_height, sent_bytes, sent_format, content_hash,
                sent_hash, cache_outcome, usage_source, evidence_json
            ) VALUES (
                1, 1, 'target', 0, NULL,
                100, 80, 1024, 'png',
                100, 80, 900, 'png', :content_hash,
                :sent_hash, 'miss', 'official_estimate', '{}'
            )
            """,
            {"content_hash": "a" * 64, "sent_hash": "b" * 64},
        )
    return engine


def test_v94_adds_policy_evidence_without_rewriting_historical_media() -> None:
    engine = _legacy_engine()
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            _migration_094_add_vision_input_policy_evidence(connection)
            _migration_094_add_vision_input_policy_evidence(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM workflow_model_media_usage"
            ).scalar_one() == 1
            media_columns = {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(workflow_model_media_usage)"
                )
            }
            assert MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS <= media_columns
            assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(model_configs)"
                )
            }
            assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(model_registry_entries)"
                )
            }
            historical = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT fidelity_mode, preprocess_mode, policy_hash, "
                "capability_profile_key, normalized_pixel_hash, compression_ms, "
                "saved_bytes, saved_ratio_ppm, provider_max_bytes, "
                "operational_max_bytes, size_gate_result, "
                "estimation_formula_version "
                "FROM workflow_model_media_usage"
            ).one()
            assert tuple(historical) == (
                "legacy_default",
                "legacy_default",
                None,
                "",
                None,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                "legacy_default",
                "legacy_default",
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT vision_capability_profile_json FROM model_configs"
            ).all() == []
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO model_configs(id, name) VALUES (1, 'legacy')"
            )
            assert json.loads(
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SELECT vision_capability_profile_json FROM model_configs WHERE id=1"
                ).scalar_one()
            ) == {}

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="prepared facts are immutable"):
            with engine.begin() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_media_usage "
                    "SET fidelity_mode='detail' WHERE id=1"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_current_metadata_declares_profile_and_media_policy_constraints() -> None:
    tables = Base.metadata.tables
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in tables["model_registry_entries"].c
    assert MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS <= {
        column.name for column in tables["workflow_model_media_usage"].c
    }

    constraint_names = {
        constraint.name
        for table_name in (
            "model_configs",
            "model_registry_entries",
            "workflow_model_media_usage",
        )
        for constraint in tables[table_name].constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }
    assert {
        "ck_model_configs_vision_capability_profile",
        "ck_model_registry_vision_capability_profile",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_vision_policy",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_vision_hashes",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_transport_metrics",
    } <= constraint_names

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        actual = inspect(engine)
        assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in {
            column["name"] for column in actual.get_columns("model_configs")
        }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_vision_platform_wide_defaults.py =====
"""两项图片准备能力必须平台级默认生效，不依赖逐个工作流改配置。

背景：全平台 165 个 active 工作流中，110 条显式策略 role 配置均无 animation
声明、其中 76 条 preprocess=original。若修复只在显式声明时生效，等于没有覆盖
生产。因此：
  1. animation 默认与 legacy-default 的 target_max_frames=1 对齐（取首帧）
  2. 超出供应商字节上限时，按 fidelity 的有效像素预算兜底降像素并留痕
"""

import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory

from PIL import Image

from app.media import prepare_vision_geometry
from app.vision_input_policy import (
    DetailFidelity,
    FirstFrameAnimation,
    OriginalPreprocess,
    RejectAnimation,
    SEED_20_PROFILE,
    VisionRolePolicy,
    parse_vision_capability_profile,
    parse_vision_input_policy,
)


def _capability():
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(
        SEED_20_PROFILE, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"
    )


def _write_gif(path: Path, frames: int) -> None:
    images = [Image.new("RGB", (64, 48), (i * 30 % 255, 90, 120)) for i in range(frames)]
    images[0].save(path, save_all=True, append_images=images[1:], duration=80, loop=0)


class PlatformWideAnimationDefaultTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_role_policy_defaults_to_first_frame(self):
        """与 legacy-default 的 target_max_frames=1 语义对齐。"""
        role = VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"), preprocess=OriginalPreprocess()
        )

        self.assertIsInstance(role.animation, FirstFrameAnimation)

    def test_existing_frozen_policy_without_animation_gets_first_frame(self):
        """110 条存量配置无 animation 键，必须自动获得首帧能力。"""
        policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
            {
                "version": "vision-input-policy-v1",
                "provider_profile": SEED_20_PROFILE["profile_key"],
                "roles": {
                    "target": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                    "anchor": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                },
                "transport": {"mode": "lossless", "size_limit": "provider_profile"},
            },
            capability=_capability(),
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )

        for role in ("target", "anchor"):
            self.assertIsInstance(policy.roles[role].animation, FirstFrameAnimation)

    def test_default_animation_still_omitted_from_serialization(self):
        """默认值不落盘，已冻结工作流的 policy hash 不变。"""
        role = VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"), preprocess=OriginalPreprocess()
        )

        self.assertNotIn("animation", role.as_dict())

    def test_reject_is_still_available_as_explicit_opt_out(self):
        role = VisionRolePolicy(
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
            preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
            animation=RejectAnimation(),
        )

        self.assertEqual({"mode": "reject"}, role.as_dict()["animation"])

    def test_geometry_extracts_first_frame_without_explicit_animation(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=6)

            path, mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/gif",
                content_sha256="platformwide",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
            )

            self.assertEqual("image/png", mime)
            with Image.open(path) as produced:
                self.assertEqual(1, int(getattr(produced, "n_frames", 1) or 1))

    def test_explicit_reject_still_blocks(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "a.gif"
            _write_gif(source, frames=6)

            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                prepare_vision_geometry(
                    source,
                    mime_type="image/gif",
                    content_sha256="platformwide",
                    cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                    preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
                    animation=RejectAnimation(),
                )


class OversizeFallbackTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """preprocess=original 的 76 条配置必须在超限时自动兜底，而非直接失败。"""

    def test_effective_pixel_budget_for_high_detail(self):
        from app.model_media_usage import oversize_fallback_pixels

        self.assertEqual(
            2_257_920, oversize_fallback_pixels(DetailFidelity("high"), _capability())
        )

    def test_fallback_preserves_original_semantics_for_normal_images(self):
        """正常尺寸图片不应被兜底逻辑改动。"""
        from app.model_media_usage import needs_oversize_fallback

        self.assertFalse(needs_oversize_fallback(1_000_000, _capability()))

    def test_fallback_triggers_only_above_operational_limit(self):
        from app.model_media_usage import needs_oversize_fallback

        cap = _capability()
        limit = cap.max_single_image_bytes - cap.single_image_safety_margin_bytes
        self.assertFalse(needs_oversize_fallback(limit, cap))
        self.assertTrue(needs_oversize_fallback(limit + 1, cap))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class OversizeFallbackPixelFloorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """兜底降像素只能降到「供应商反正会用」的预算，不能因取整而低于它。"""

    def _pixels_after(self, width, height, target):
        from app.model_media_usage import _oversize_fallback_preprocess
        from app.media import prepare_vision_geometry

        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "big.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (width, height), (7, 9, 11)).save(source)
            fallback = _oversize_fallback_preprocess(source, target)
            self.assertIsNotNone(fallback)
            path, _ = prepare_vision_geometry(
                source,
                mime_type="image/png",
                content_sha256=f"floor{width}x{height}",
                cache_dir=Path(tmp) / "cache",
                preprocess=fallback,
            )
            with Image.open(path) as produced:
                return produced.width * produced.height

    def test_fallback_never_drops_below_budget_for_tall_image(self):
        # 现场素材 asset 866 的形状：4812x6015 曾因取整降到 2,254,897 < 2,257,920
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self._pixels_after(4812, 6015, 2_257_920), 2_257_920)

    def test_fallback_never_drops_below_budget_for_wide_image(self):
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self._pixels_after(6015, 4812, 2_257_920), 2_257_920)

    def test_fallback_never_drops_below_budget_for_square_image(self):
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self._pixels_after(5000, 5000, 2_257_920), 2_257_920)

    def test_fallback_skipped_when_already_within_budget(self):
        from app.model_media_usage import _oversize_fallback_preprocess

        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            source = Path(tmp) / "small.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (800, 600), (7, 9, 11)).save(source)

            self.assertIsNone(_oversize_fallback_preprocess(source, 2_257_920))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_wait_cap_resubmit.py =====
"""命中本地等待上限后，重提交间隔要随命中次数拉开。

2026-09-16 实测 24 小时：1,589 次命中上限后的重提交里 1,066 次（67%）间隔正好 60 秒——
写死的 60 就是生效值。同一步骤反复命中（平均 2.17 次，最多 7 次）说明供应商在过载，
60 秒后原样再撞只是把负载推回去。
"""
import pytest

from app.batch_inference import (
    WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_MAX_SECONDS, wait_cap_resubmit_delay,
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("attempt_no,expected", [
    (1, 60.0),    # 第一次：供应商可能只是抖一下，保持原来的 60
    (2, 180.0),
    (3, 540.0),
    (4, 900.0),   # 封顶，与等待上限同量级
    (7, 900.0),   # 实测最多命中 7 次，不能再往上爬
])
def test_the_delay_escalates_then_caps(attempt_no, expected):
    assert wait_cap_resubmit_delay(attempt_no) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [None, 0, -3, "x"])
def test_unknown_attempt_counts_as_the_first_hit(bad):
    """拿不到尝试号时按第一次处理——宁可快一点重试，也不要因为缺个数字就等 15 分钟。"""

    assert wait_cap_resubmit_delay(bad) == 60.0


def test_the_cap_never_exceeds_the_wait_cap_order_of_magnitude():
    assert WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_MAX_SECONDS == 900
    assert all(wait_cap_resubmit_delay(n) <= 900 for n in range(1, 50))


def test_the_executor_uses_the_schedule_not_a_literal():
    """执行器里不能再出现写死的 60——那正是当初的放大器。"""

    import pathlib
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    source = pathlib.Path(executor.__file__).read_text()
    block = source[source.index("exc.wait_cap_hit = True"):source.index("_model_failure(", source.index("exc.wait_cap_hit = True"))]
    assert "recovery_delay = 60" not in block
    assert "wait_cap_resubmit_delay(timeout_recovery_attempt_no)" in block

===== FILE backend/tests/test_wide_material.py =====
"""素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：一条素材的当前结论从源表重算成一行，写入点同事务刷新。

盯五件事：3DSU/灵感图两种身份与维度键名各自落对列；纠偏/人工结论/真值/投递各自追加
对应 event_kind 且 row_hash 去重；钩子抛错不阻断主流程；对账能修被改坏的行；MySQL
升级步与视图 DDL 的写法（TEXT 无默认值、CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW、CONVERT_TZ）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.downstream_publish_ledger import mark_downstream_publish_outcome
from app.incremental_correction import correct_incremental_level
from app.models import (
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    DownstreamPublishLedger,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
)
from app.wide_material import build_material_row, compute_row_hash, reconcile, refresh_material
from app.wide_material_models import ROW_COLUMNS, WideMaterialCurrent, WideMaterialHistory

from test_incremental_correction_queue import (  # noqa: F401 - 复用同一套夹具
    db,
    _material,
)
from test_workflow_result_finalizer import _model_3d_su_grade_fixture


def _history(db, asset_id: int) -> list[WideMaterialHistory]:
    return list(db.scalars(
        select(WideMaterialHistory).where(WideMaterialHistory.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(WideMaterialHistory.id)))


def _real_3dsu_material(db, index: int = 1):
    """在夹具的运行/结果上换成真 3DSU 归并结果与调用 A/B 原始输出。"""
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result

    result, run = _material(db, index)
    manifest, node_outputs = _model_3d_su_grade_fixture()
    normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, node_outputs)
    outputs = json.loads(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    outputs["prompt_a"] = {"status": "succeeded", "node_kind": "prompt",
                           "output": node_outputs["prompt_a"]["output"]}
    outputs["prompt_b"] = {"status": "succeeded", "node_kind": "prompt",
                           "output": node_outputs["prompt_b"]["output"]}
    outputs["downstream"]["node_kind"] = "downstream_projection"
    outputs["downstream"]["output"]["final_level"] = normalized["level"]
    outputs["downstream"]["output"]["final_score"] = normalized["score"]
    outputs["downstream"]["output"]["normalized_result"] = normalized
    outputs["downstream"]["output"]["prompt_a_fields"] = {
        "production_fields": node_outputs["prompt_a"]["output"]["production_fields"]}
    result.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs, ensure_ascii=False)
    result.normalized_result_json = json.dumps(normalized, ensure_ascii=False)
    result.precheck_json = json.dumps(node_outputs["prompt_a"]["output"], ensure_ascii=False)
    result.level = normalized["level"]
    result.score = normalized["score"]
    result.confidence = normalized["confidence"]
    run.upstream_task_id, run.upstream_img_id, run.upstream_ll_id, run.upstream_class_id = 2588, 46272219, 34015031, 2124
    run.run_mode = "incremental"
    db.commit()
    return result, run


def test_a_3dsu_evaluation_lands_in_one_row_with_every_group_filled(db):
    result, run = _real_3dsu_material(db)
    assert refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated") is True
    db.commit()

    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row is not None
    # 身份
    assert row.category_key == "model_3d_su" and row.sha256 == f"{1:064d}"
    assert row.content_key == "factory:c1" and row.source_system == "factory"
    assert (row.upstream_task_id, row.upstream_img_id, row.upstream_ll_id, row.upstream_class_id) == (
        2588, 46272219, 34015031, 2124)
    assert row.insp_task_id is None and row.width == 8
    # 结论：等级/分数/档由真归并结果给出
    assert row.final_level == "L3" and row.final_score == 42 and row.tier == "regular"
    assert row.model_level == "L3" and row.model_score == 42 and row.confidence == 0.96
    assert row.track_key == "soft_furnishing" and row.hard_reject is False
    assert json.loads(row.hit_rules_json) == []
    # 业务字段来自调用 A
    assert row.title == "现代沙发模型" and row.seo_title == "现代布艺沙发3D模型"
    assert row.category_text == "软装" and row.style == "现代" and row.trait == "3D数字效果图"
    assert json.loads(row.tags_json) == ["现代", "沙发", "布艺", "家具"]
    # 五维档位；灵感图八维为空
    assert (row.dim_model_detail, row.dim_material_rendering, row.dim_lighting,
            row.dim_design_trend, row.dim_visual_composition) == (4, 4, 3, 4, 4)
    assert row.dim_composition_viewpoint is None
    # 人工/真值/投递/版本
    assert row.overridden is False and row.truth_level is None
    assert row.delivery_status == "delivered" and row.delivery_revision == 1
    assert row.production_run_id == run.id and row.run_key == run.run_key
    assert row.run_mode == "incremental" and row.run_status == "succeeded"
    assert row.workflow_version_id == 1 and row.mechanism_version_name == "v1"
    assert row.model_id == "m" and row.prompt_a_version == "a"
    assert row.evaluated_at is not None and row.evaluated_at.tzinfo is None
    assert row.updated_at.tzinfo is None and row.wide_schema_version == 1
    detail = json.loads(row.detail_json)
    assert detail["normalized_result"]["level"] == "L3" and "raw_response_a" not in detail
    history = _history(db, result.asset_id)
    # 夹具首发时台账钩子已登记一行（delivered），换成真归并结果后再追加 evaluated
    assert [entry.event_kind for entry in history] == ["delivered", "evaluated"]
    assert history[-1].row_hash == compute_row_hash({c: getattr(row, c) for c in ROW_COLUMNS})


def test_an_inspiration_material_takes_identity_from_the_task_and_eight_dimensions(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 3, category="inspiration_image")
    outputs = json.loads(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    outputs["downstream"]["output"] = {
        "schema_version": "inspiration-factory-result-v1",
        "a_fields": {"title": "北欧客厅", "seo_title": "北欧风客厅灵感图", "class_text": "客厅",
                     "style": "北欧", "tags": ["北欧", "客厅", "原木", "亮色"], "cons": "略平",
                     "design": "原木与白", "reason": "", "trait": "实景照片", "image_defects": ""},
        "final_level": "L2", "final_score": 80,
        "normalized_result": {"level": "L2", "score": 80},
        "inspiration_task": {"task_id": 9001, "cloud_id": "cloud-9", "project_id": "proj-9"},
    }
    result.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs, ensure_ascii=False)
    dims = ["composition_viewpoint", "lighting_atmosphere", "color_material", "spatial_design_furnishing",
            "visual_hierarchy", "detail_completion", "inspiration_reference", "presentation_integrity"]
    result.normalized_result_json = json.dumps({
        "level": "L2", "score": 80, "track_key": "class_one", "hard_reject": False, "hit_rules": [],
        "aesthetic_dimensions": {key: {"grade": index % 5 + 1} for index, key in enumerate(dims)},
        "recommendation_calibration": {"probability": 0.81, "recommended": True},
    })
    result.level, result.score = "L2", 80
    db.commit()

    assert refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row.category_key == "inspiration_image"
    assert row.upstream_task_id is None and row.upstream_img_id is None
    assert (row.insp_task_id, row.insp_cloud_id, row.insp_project_id) == (9001, "cloud-9", "proj-9")
    assert row.final_level == "L2" and row.tier == "recommend"
    assert row.rec_probability == 0.81 and row.rec_recommended is True
    assert row.title == "北欧客厅" and row.seo_title == "北欧风客厅灵感图" and row.category_text == "客厅"
    assert [getattr(row, f"dim_{key}") for key in dims] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 1, 2, 3]
    assert row.dim_model_detail is None


def test_a_level_correction_updates_current_and_appends_history_once(db):
    result, run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 2)
    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()

    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="人工看图后升档", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-lvl-2")
    db.commit()

    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row.final_level == "L2" and row.tier == "recommend" and row.model_level == "L3"
    assert row.overridden is True and row.override_field == "final_level"
    assert row.override_level == "L2" and row.override_actor == "ops" and row.override_reason == "人工看图后升档"
    assert row.override_at is not None and row.last_correction_at == row.override_at
    assert row.delivery_revision == 2 and row.delivery_status == "pending"
    kinds = [entry.event_kind for entry in _history(db, result.asset_id)]
    # 纠偏一次会经过三个钩子（等级覆盖、案例登记、重发台账）；只有内容变了才追加
    assert kinds[:2] == ["delivered", "evaluated"] and "corrected" in kinds
    assert set(kinds) == {"delivered", "evaluated", "corrected"}
    before = len(kinds)
    # 再刷一次，结论没变：current 不动，history 不追加
    assert refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="corrected")
    db.commit()
    assert len(_history(db, result.asset_id)) == before
    hashes = [entry.row_hash for entry in _history(db, result.asset_id)]
    assert len(hashes) == len(set(hashes))


def test_a_manual_conclusion_on_a_failed_run_becomes_the_current_verdict(db):
    from app.failure_console import resolve_failure
    from test_failure_console import GOOD_FIELDS, _failed_material

    run = _failed_material(db, 4)
    asset_id = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["asset_id"]
    assert db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id) is None
    resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                    reason="调用 A 反复超时，人工看图后定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-wide-4")
    db.commit()

    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id)
    assert row is not None
    assert row.final_level == "L3" and row.final_score == 68 and row.tier == "regular"
    assert row.manual_resolution == "published" and row.overridden is True
    assert row.override_actor == "ops" and row.override_level == "L3"
    assert row.title == GOOD_FIELDS["title"] and json.loads(row.tags_json) == GOOD_FIELDS["tags"]
    assert row.run_status == "failed" and row.upstream_task_id == 7004
    assert row.delivery_status == "pending" and row.delivery_revision == 1
    kinds = [entry.event_kind for entry in _history(db, asset_id)]
    assert "manual_resolution" in kinds and kinds[-1] == "manual_resolution"


def test_a_truth_revision_and_a_delivery_outcome_append_their_own_events(db):
    from app.baseline_truth import append_baseline_truth_revision

    result, run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 5)
    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()

    baseline = BaselineSet(category_key="model_3d_su", name="宽表真值集", description="",
                           default_expected_level="L3", fingerprint="d" * 64, created_by="ops")
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=result.asset_id, expected_level="L3",
                           expected_result_json=None, truth_contract_key=None, asset_snapshot_json="{}")
    db.add(item)
    db.commit()
    append_baseline_truth_revision(
        db, item=item, expected_level="L2", expected_result={"level": "L2"},
        truth_contract_key="model_3d_su.level.v1", workflow_input={},
        source_baseline_run_id=None, source_production_run_id=None,
        source_correction_key=None, reason="复核后改真值", actor="ops")
    db.commit()
    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert (row.truth_level, row.truth_revision) == ("L2", 1) and row.truth_updated_at is not None
    assert _history(db, result.asset_id)[-1].event_kind == "truth_revised"

    ledger = db.scalar(select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run.run_key))
    ledger.delivery_status = "pending"
    db.commit()
    when = datetime(2026, 9, 27, 3, 30, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    mark_downstream_publish_outcome(db, outbox_event_id=ledger.outbox_event_id, delivery_status="delivered",
                                    external_record_key="ext-5", now=when)
    db.commit()
    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row.delivery_status == "delivered" and row.external_record_key == "ext-5"
    assert row.delivered_at == datetime(2026, 9, 27, 3, 30)
    assert _history(db, result.asset_id)[-1].event_kind == "delivered"


def test_a_broken_hook_never_fails_the_main_flow(db, monkeypatch):
    import app.wide_material as wide

    result, run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 6)
    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()
    before = len(_history(db, result.asset_id))

    def boom(_db, _asset_id, **_kwargs):
        raise RuntimeError("宽表投影坏了")

    monkeypatch.setattr(wide, "build_material_row", boom)
    payload = correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L4", expected_level="L3",
                                        reason="降档", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-boom-6")
    db.commit()
    assert payload["republished"] is True
    assert db.get(EvaluationResult, result.id).level == "L4"
    # 宽表没刷成，留在纠偏前的样子；对账会补
    stale = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert stale.final_level == "L3" and len(_history(db, result.asset_id)) == before
    monkeypatch.undo()
    summary = reconcile(db, asset_ids=[result.asset_id])
    db.commit()
    assert summary["mismatched"] == 1 and db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id).final_level == "L4"


def test_reconcile_repairs_a_row_someone_edited_by_hand(db):
    result, _run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 7)
    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()
    clean = reconcile(db, asset_ids=[result.asset_id])
    assert clean["checked"] == 1 and clean["consistent"] == 1 and clean["repaired"] == 0

    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    row.final_level, row.title = "L1", "被手工改坏"
    db.commit()
    summary = reconcile(db, asset_ids=[result.asset_id])
    db.commit()
    assert summary["mismatched"] == 1 and summary["repaired"] == 1
    assert summary["mismatched_asset_ids"] == [result.asset_id]
    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row.final_level == "L3" and row.title == "现代沙发模型"
    # 修回的内容与最初一致：history 不因对账再长一行
    assert len(_history(db, result.asset_id)) == 2
    # since/全量候选也能找到这条
    assert result.asset_id in wide_candidates(db)


def wide_candidates(db):
    from app.wide_material import candidate_asset_ids
    return candidate_asset_ids(db)


def test_a_run_without_a_result_still_records_run_state(db):
    from app.wide_material import refresh_for_run, resolve_run_asset_id

    result, run = _material(db, 8, published=False)
    db.delete(result)
    db.commit()
    asset_id = run_key_asset(db, run)
    assert resolve_run_asset_id(db, run) == asset_id
    assert build_material_row(db, asset_id) is None  # 夹具没挂 content_records.asset_id，找不到运行
    row = build_material_row(db, asset_id, run_hint=run)
    assert row is not None and row["final_level"] is None and row["run_status"] == "succeeded"
    assert row["production_run_id"] == run.id
    assert refresh_for_run(db, run, event_kind="run_state") is True
    db.commit()
    assert [entry.event_kind for entry in _history(db, asset_id)] == ["run_state"]
    assert build_material_row(db, 999_999) is None


def run_key_asset(db, run: ProductionRun) -> int:
    return json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["asset_id"]


def test_mysql_upgrade_step_129_to_130_builds_tables_and_views() -> None:
    import ast
    import inspect as _inspect
    import re
    import textwrap

    from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
    from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline
    from app.migrations.wide_material_130 import CURRENT_VIEW, HISTORY_VIEW, view_ddl

    source = _inspect.getsource(mysql_baseline.upgrade_mysql_schema)
    step = re.search(r"if actual == 129 and expected >= 130:(.+?)actual = 130", source, re.S)
    assert step is not None
    assert "from .wide_material_130 import migrate" in step.group(1)
    assert "metadata=mysql_metadata()" in step.group(1)
    assert "(130, 'wide_material_tables')" in step.group(1)
    assert 129 in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_SOURCE_VERSIONS and 130 in mysql_baseline.UPGRADE_TARGET_VERSIONS
    tree = ast.parse(textwrap.dedent(step.group(1)))
    assert not [n for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.Attribute) and n.attr == "__table__"]

    metadata = mysql_baseline.mysql_metadata()
    for name in ("wide_material_current", "wide_material_history"):
        ddl = str(CreateTable(metadata.tables[name]).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
        for line in ddl.splitlines():
            if "LONGTEXT" in line:
                assert "DEFAULT" not in line, line
        assert "DATETIME(6)" in ddl
    current_sql, history_sql = view_ddl()
    assert current_sql.startswith(f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW `{CURRENT_VIEW}` AS SELECT")
    assert history_sql.startswith(f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW `{HISTORY_VIEW}` AS SELECT")
    for sql in (current_sql, history_sql):
        assert "detail_json" not in sql and "row_hash" not in sql
        assert "CONVERT_TZ(`evaluated_at`,'+00:00','+08:00') AS `evaluated_at_cst`" in sql
        assert "CONVERT_TZ(`updated_at`,'+00:00','+08:00') AS `updated_at_cst`" in sql
        assert "`final_level`" in sql and "`upstream_task_id`" in sql and "`insp_task_id`" in sql
    assert "CONVERT_TZ(`recorded_at`,'+00:00','+08:00') AS `recorded_at_cst`" in history_sql
    assert "`event_kind`" in history_sql


def test_sqlite_migration_130_creates_both_tables_and_is_rerunnable() -> None:
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect

    from app.migrations.wide_material_130 import migrate

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migrate(connection)
            migrate(connection)
            names = set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
        assert {"wide_material_current", "wide_material_history"} <= names
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("level,tier", [("L1", "recommend"), ("L2", "recommend"), ("L3", "regular"),
                                        ("L4", "regular"), ("L5", "filter"), (None, None)])
def test_tier_is_derived_from_level(level, tier):
    from app.wide_material import TIER_BY_LEVEL
    assert TIER_BY_LEVEL.get(level) == tier


def test_finalizing_a_native_run_writes_the_row_in_the_same_transaction(tmp_path) -> None:
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

    from app.database import Base
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import finalize_native_run
    from test_workflow_result_finalizer import _seed_succeeded_native_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'wide-finalizer.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as session:
            asset, workflow, run, _expected = _seed_succeeded_native_run(session)
            # 运行创建钩子已登记 run_state（queued），收尾后再追加 evaluated
            kinds_before = [entry.event_kind for entry in _history(session, asset.id)]
            assert kinds_before == ["run_state"]
            finalize_native_run(session, run)
            session.commit()
            row = session.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset.id)
            assert row is not None and row.final_level == "L2" and row.final_score == 88
            assert row.tier == "recommend" and row.production_run_id == run.id
            assert row.workflow_version_id == workflow.id and row.run_status == "succeeded"
            assert [entry.event_kind for entry in _history(session, asset.id)] == ["run_state", "evaluated"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _stock_result(db, asset_id: int, *, level: str, index: int):
    """给同一素材再挂一条存量回归结果（另一个 workflow_version、run_mode=stock_regression）。"""
    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    from app.models import EvaluationJob
    from test_incremental_correction_queue import _node_outputs, _snapshot
    from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest

    snapshot = _snapshot("model_3d_su", run_mode="stock_regression", frozen={"asset_id": asset_id})
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key=f"stock-run-{index}", idempotency_key=f"stock-idem-{index}", source_type="native_evaluation",
        source_id=asset_id, workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=2,
        snapshot_hash=hash_manifest(json.loads(snapshot)), snapshot_json=snapshot, run_mode="stock_regression",
        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="validation", status="succeeded", blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="t", owner="t", reason="", environment="dry_run", total_steps=3, completed_steps=3,
        failed_steps=0, created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset_id, category_key="model_3d_su", status="completed", stage="done",
                        progress=100, workflow_version_id=2, production_run_id=run.id)
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset_id, job_id=job.id, workflow_version_id=2, production_run_id=run.id,
        workflow_snapshot_hash=run.snapshot_hash, workflow_node_outputs_json=_node_outputs(level),
        normalized_result_json=json.dumps({"level": level, "score": 55}), precheck_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"level": level, "score": 55}), raw_response_a="{}", level=level, score=55,
        confidence=0.5, needs_review=False, model_id="m2", prompt_a_version="a2", rubric_version="r",
        engine_version="e")
    db.add(result)
    db.commit()
    return result, run


def test_an_experimental_stock_regression_never_overrides_the_formal_incremental_verdict(db):
    result, run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 9)
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="正式结论", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-formal-9")
    db.commit()
    assert db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id).final_level == "L2"
    before = len(_history(db, result.asset_id))

    stock_result, stock_run = _stock_result(db, result.asset_id, level="L3", index=9)
    assert stock_result.id > result.id and stock_run.id > run.id
    assert refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    # 运行终态钩子把实验回归运行递进来，也不能让它成为「当前运行」
    from app.wide_material import refresh_for_run
    assert refresh_for_run(db, stock_run, event_kind="run_state")
    db.commit()

    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id)
    assert row.final_level == "L2" and row.run_mode == "incremental"
    assert row.production_run_id == run.id and row.workflow_version_id == 1 and row.model_id == "m"
    assert len(_history(db, result.asset_id)) == before


def test_a_material_with_only_a_stock_regression_result_takes_it_and_says_so(db):
    from app.models import Asset

    asset = Asset(original_name="stock.jpg", stored_name="stock.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1,
                  width=4, height=4, sha256="e" * 64, category_key="model_3d_su")
    db.add(asset)
    db.commit()
    stock_result, stock_run = _stock_result(db, asset.id, level="L3", index=10)
    assert refresh_material(db, asset.id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()
    row = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset.id)
    assert row.final_level == "L3" and row.run_mode == "stock_regression"
    assert row.production_run_id == stock_run.id and row.workflow_version_id == 2 and row.model_id == "m2"


def test_row_hash_ignores_single_precision_float_roundtrip():
    from app.wide_material import compute_row_hash

    stored = {"rec_probability": 0.0172105, "final_score": 82.5, "title": "x"}
    rebuilt = {"rec_probability": 0.01721047058393287, "final_score": 82.5, "title": "x"}
    assert compute_row_hash(stored) == compute_row_hash(rebuilt)
    assert compute_row_hash(stored) != compute_row_hash({**stored, "rec_probability": 0.0172})
    # 7 位上的进位边界：单精度截断后再取 6 位才和库里回读一致
    assert compute_row_hash({"p": 0.911673}) == compute_row_hash({"p": 0.9116735009804339})
    assert compute_row_hash({"p": 0.596268}) == compute_row_hash({"p": 0.59626749465371})


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Astra 全仓复审 F3：记录改指新素材后，旧素材的运行不能挂到新素材上
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _repoint_to_new_asset(db, result, run, *, bind_old_event: bool):
    """同一 ContentRecord 从 A（v1）更新到 B（v2）：接入新版本、record 改指 B，旧运行仍冻结在 A。"""
    from app.models import Asset, ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord

    old_event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, old_event.content_record_id)
    if bind_old_event:
        identity = json.loads(old_event.identity_snapshot_json)
        identity["asset_binding"] = {"asset_id": result.asset_id}
        old_event.identity_snapshot_json = json.dumps(identity)
    new_asset = Asset(original_name="v2.jpg", stored_name="v2.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=10,
                      width=8, height=8, sha256="f" * 64, category_key=record.category_key)
    db.add(new_asset)
    db.flush()
    new_event = ContentIngressEvent(
        event_id=f"evt-v2-{record.id}", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.updated",
        source_system="factory", occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), payload_hash="e" * 64,
        payload_json=json.dumps({"content_id": record.source_content_id, "content_version": "v2"}),
        identity_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"content_key": record.content_key,
                                           "asset_binding": {"asset_id": new_asset.id}}),
        content_record_id=record.id, status="applied")
    db.add(new_event)
    record.asset_id, record.source_version = new_asset.id, "v2"
    db.commit()
    return new_asset, new_event


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bind_old_event", [True, False])
def test_a_repointed_record_never_lends_the_old_assets_run_to_the_new_asset(db, bind_old_event):
    """复现：旧失败运行冻结 A，record 改指 B。旧实现 _runs_for_asset(B) 经记录的全部接入事件把 A 的
    运行挂到 B（B 的宽表显示 A 的失败状态），候选发现又用 record 当前 asset_id 算成 B、漏掉 A。
    bind_old_event=False 覆盖接入时素材未到、事件没有素材绑定的情况（只能读运行冻结的素材）。"""
    from app.wide_material import _runs_for_asset, candidate_asset_ids, resolve_run_asset_id

    result, run = _material(db, 33, published=False)
    old_asset_id = result.asset_id
    db.delete(result)
    run.status = "failed"
    db.commit()
    new_asset, _event = _repoint_to_new_asset(db, result, run, bind_old_event=bind_old_event)

    assert resolve_run_asset_id(db, run) == old_asset_id
    assert [r.id for r in _runs_for_asset(db, new_asset.id)] == []
    assert build_material_row(db, new_asset.id) is None  # B 还没有自己的结果或运行，不能借 A 的
    assert old_asset_id in candidate_asset_ids(db)
    assert new_asset.id not in candidate_asset_ids(db)
    # A 自己的运行仍按钩子提示正常投影。
    row = build_material_row(db, old_asset_id, run_hint=run)
    assert row is not None and row["production_run_id"] == run.id and row["run_status"] == "failed"


def test_the_new_assets_own_run_is_still_found_through_the_record(db):
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from app.wide_material import _runs_for_asset, candidate_asset_ids
    from test_incremental_correction_queue import _snapshot

    result, run = _material(db, 34, published=False)
    old_asset_id = result.asset_id
    db.delete(result)
    run.status = "failed"
    db.commit()
    new_asset, new_event = _repoint_to_new_asset(db, result, run, bind_old_event=True)
    frozen = {"asset_id": new_asset.id, "source_version": "v2", "content_record_id": new_event.content_record_id,
              "content_ingress_event_db_id": new_event.id, "source_system": "factory"}
    new_run = ProductionRun(
        run_key="run-v2-0034", idempotency_key="idem-v2-34", source_type="content_ingress_event",
        source_id=new_event.id, workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_hash="0" * 64,
        snapshot_json=_snapshot("model_3d_su", run_mode="incremental", frozen=frozen),
        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="production_batch", status="running", blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="t", owner="t", reason="", environment="dry_run", total_steps=3, completed_steps=1,
        failed_steps=0)
    db.add(new_run)
    db.commit()

    assert [r.id for r in _runs_for_asset(db, new_asset.id)] == [new_run.id]
    row = build_material_row(db, new_asset.id)
    assert row is not None and row["production_run_id"] == new_run.id and row["run_status"] == "running"
    assert {old_asset_id, new_asset.id} <= set(candidate_asset_ids(db))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Astra 全仓复审 F4：比较后写入，旧的构建结果不能覆盖别的事务刚写的新值
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


@pytest.fixture
def file_sessions(tmp_path):
    """文件库 + 独立 Session：两个事务真正各自提交（StaticPool 的内存库共用一条连接，模拟不了）。"""
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

    from app.database import Base
    from test_incremental_correction_queue import _seed_reference_data

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'wide-race.db'}", connect_args={"timeout": 5})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        _seed_reference_data(db)
        db.commit()
    yield sessions
    engine.dispose()


def _race_after_first_build(monkeypatch, sessions, result_id: int, *, level: str = "L2"):
    """第一次构建读完源表之后，另一事务把权威结果改成 level 并照常刷新宽表、提交。"""
    import app.wide_material as wide

    real_build = wide.build_material_row
    seen: list[str | None] = []

    def racing_build(db, asset_id, *, run_hint=None):
        built = real_build(db, asset_id, run_hint=run_hint)
        if not seen:
            seen.append(built["final_level"] if built else None)
            with sessions() as other:
                other.get(EvaluationResult, result_id).level = level
                assert refresh_material(other, asset_id, event_kind="evaluated") is True
                other.commit()
        return built

    monkeypatch.setattr(wide, "build_material_row", racing_build)
    return seen


def _seed_projected(sessions, index: int, *, drop_current: bool = False, corrupt: bool = False):
    with sessions() as db:
        result, _run = _real_3dsu_material(db, index)
        assert refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated") is True
        db.commit()
        if corrupt:
            db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id).title = "被手工改坏"
        if drop_current:
            db.delete(db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id))
        db.commit()
        return result.asset_id, result.id


@pytest.mark.parametrize("drop_current", [False, True])
def test_reconcile_never_writes_back_what_a_concurrent_refresh_replaced(file_sessions, monkeypatch, drop_current):
    """复现复审交错：对账算出旧行 L3 后，另一事务把权威结果与宽表更新为 L2；旧实现仍无条件写回 L3，
    还追加一条旧 history 并报 repaired=1。drop_current 覆盖两边都以为还没有这一行（插入撞主键）。"""
    asset_id, result_id = _seed_projected(file_sessions, 41 + int(drop_current), corrupt=not drop_current,
                                          drop_current=drop_current)
    seen = _race_after_first_build(monkeypatch, file_sessions, result_id)
    with file_sessions() as db:
        summary = reconcile(db, asset_ids=[asset_id])
        db.commit()
        current = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id)
        history = _history(db, asset_id)
    assert seen == ["L3"]  # 第一次构建读到的是旧值
    assert current.final_level == "L2" and current.title == "现代沙发模型"
    assert summary["repaired"] == 0 and summary["consistent"] == 1 and summary["conflicts"] == 0
    assert history[-1].final_level == "L2"  # 新值之后没有再追加旧值


def test_a_hook_refresh_rebuilds_instead_of_overwriting_a_newer_row(file_sessions, monkeypatch):
    """钩子之间同样的交错：先开始构建的一方晚提交，不能用自己的旧快照覆盖对方刚写的新行。"""
    asset_id, result_id = _seed_projected(file_sessions, 45)
    _race_after_first_build(monkeypatch, file_sessions, result_id)
    with file_sessions() as db:
        assert refresh_material(db, asset_id, event_kind="run_state") is True
        db.commit()
        current = db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id)
        history = _history(db, asset_id)
    levels = [entry.final_level for entry in history]
    assert current.final_level == "L2"
    assert levels[-1] == "L2" and "L3" not in levels[levels.index("L2"):]  # 新值之后没有旧值


def test_reconcile_reports_a_row_that_keeps_changing_as_a_conflict(db, monkeypatch):
    """每次写之前 current 都已不是构建前读到的样子：重试用尽只记 conflicts，不写，也不算失败。

    （SQLite 的事务一旦开始就挡住别的连接提交，连续三次真实交错只能在 MySQL 上复现；这里用固定的
    旧哈希模拟"构建期间总有人刷新过"。）"""
    import app.wide_material as wide

    result, _run = _real_3dsu_material(db, 46)
    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="evaluated")
    db.commit()
    db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id).title = "被手工改坏"
    db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(wide, "_current_hash", lambda _db, _asset_id: "0" * 64)
    summary = reconcile(db, asset_ids=[result.asset_id])
    db.commit()
    assert summary["conflicts"] == 1 and summary["repaired"] == 0 and summary["failed"] == 0
    assert db.get(WideMaterialCurrent, result.asset_id).title == "被手工改坏"  # 没有写


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Astra 复审 R-F3：同一版本重新接入换了素材，旧运行按冻结依据归属
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_a_same_version_reingest_never_lends_the_old_run_to_the_new_asset(db):
    """复审反例原样：旧事件没有素材绑定、运行冻结 A；同一 content_version 经真实 ingest_content_event
    重新接入指向 B。旧实现因"版本相等"信当前 record，把 A 的运行借给 B，候选也算成 B。"""
    from datetime import timedelta

    from app.label_governance import ingest_content_event
    from app.models import Asset, ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord, EvaluationCategoryProfile
    from app.wide_material import _runs_for_asset, candidate_asset_ids, resolve_run_asset_id

    result, run = _material(db, 65, published=False)
    old_id = result.asset_id
    event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id)
    assert "asset_binding" not in json.loads(event.identity_snapshot_json or "{}")
    db.delete(result)
    run.status = "failed"
    db.commit()
    newer = Asset(original_name="replacement.jpg", stored_name="replacement.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
                  size_bytes=10, width=8, height=8, sha256="e" * 64, category_key=record.category_key)
    db.add(newer)
    db.add(EvaluationCategoryProfile(category_key=record.category_key, display_name="probe", status="active"))
    db.flush()
    new_event, record, duplicate = ingest_content_event(
        db, event_id="same-version-replacement-65", schema_version="content-ingress-v1",
        event_type="content.updated", source_system=record.source_system,
        occurred_at=record.source_occurred_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
        payload={"content_id": record.source_content_id, "category_key": record.category_key,
                 "content_version": record.source_version, "asset_id": newer.id},
        received_by="offline-review")
    db.commit()
    assert new_event.status == "applied" and record.asset_id == newer.id and not duplicate

    assert resolve_run_asset_id(db, run) == old_id
    assert [r.id for r in _runs_for_asset(db, newer.id)] == []
    assert build_material_row(db, newer.id) is None
    candidates = candidate_asset_ids(db)
    assert old_id in candidates and newer.id not in candidates


def test_a_run_without_any_frozen_asset_falls_back_to_the_record_only_on_its_current_version(db):
    """两种冻结依据都没有的历史运行：事件仍是记录当前版本才用记录当前素材；版本已前进则定不下来。"""
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord
    from app.wide_material import _runs_for_asset, resolve_run_asset_id

    result, run = _material(db, 66, published=False)
    asset_id = result.asset_id
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"].pop("asset_id")
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id).content_record_id)
    record.asset_id = asset_id
    db.delete(result)
    db.commit()
    assert resolve_run_asset_id(db, run) == asset_id
    assert [r.id for r in _runs_for_asset(db, asset_id)] == [run.id]

    record.source_version = "v2"  # 版本前进：旧事件不再是当前版本，记录的当前素材证明不了归属
    db.commit()
    assert resolve_run_asset_id(db, run) is None
    assert _runs_for_asset(db, asset_id) == []


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Astra 复审 R-F4：每次构建都读新鲜源数据，不用会话里缓存的旧源对象
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_reconcile_rebuilds_from_fresh_sources_while_holding_a_cached_result(file_sessions, monkeypatch):
    """复审反例原样：对账会话一直持有 L3 结果对象的强引用；另一事务把结果与宽表刷新为 L2。
    旧实现冲突后重建仍读缓存的 L3，拿 L2 的 current 哈希给旧源背书，写回 L3。"""
    asset_id, result_id = _seed_projected(file_sessions, 62, corrupt=True)
    _race_after_first_build(monkeypatch, file_sessions, result_id)
    with file_sessions() as first:
        held = first.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)  # 强引用，让源对象一直留在 identity map
        assert held.level == "L3"
        summary = reconcile(first, asset_ids=[asset_id])
        first.commit()
    with file_sessions() as check:
        assert check.get(EvaluationResult, result_id).level == "L2"
        assert check.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id).final_level == "L2"
        assert _history(check, asset_id)[-1].final_level == "L2"
    assert summary["repaired"] == 0 and summary["consistent"] == 1


def test_a_hook_ignores_source_objects_cached_in_the_business_session(file_sessions):
    """业务会话早先读过 L3 结果；另一事务提交 L2 并刷新宽表之后，这个会话里的钩子刷新不能写回 L3。"""
    asset_id, result_id = _seed_projected(file_sessions, 63)
    with file_sessions() as business:
        held = business.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert held.level == "L3"
        with file_sessions() as other:
            other.get(EvaluationResult, result_id).level = "L2"
            assert refresh_material(other, asset_id, event_kind="evaluated") is True
            other.commit()
        assert refresh_material(business, asset_id, event_kind="run_state") is True
        business.commit()
    with file_sessions() as check:
        levels = [entry.final_level for entry in _history(check, asset_id)]
        assert check.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id).final_level == "L2"
        assert levels[-1] == "L2" and "L3" not in levels[levels.index("L2"):]


def test_a_hook_still_sees_the_business_transactions_own_unflushed_change(file_sessions):
    """生产 SessionLocal 是 autoflush=False：钩子先 flush 再构建，业务事务自己还没落库的改动不能丢。"""
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

    asset_id, result_id = _seed_projected(file_sessions, 64)
    engine = file_sessions.kw["bind"]
    with Session(engine, autoflush=False, expire_on_commit=False) as business:
        business.get(EvaluationResult, result_id).level = "L1"  # 未 flush
        assert refresh_material(business, asset_id, event_kind="evaluated") is True
        business.commit()
    with file_sessions() as check:
        assert check.get(WideMaterialCurrent, asset_id).final_level == "L1"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_windows_deploy.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator

import pytest

from app import security, windows_deploy


FAKE_DPAPI_REFERENCE = "dpapi:v1:ZmFrZS1kcGFwaS1jaXBoZXJ0ZXh0"
FAKE_DPAPI_MACHINE_REFERENCE = (
    "dpapi-machine:v1:ZmFrZS1tYWNoaW5lLWRwYXBpLWNpcGhlcnRleHQ="
)
FAKE_TOKEN = "f" * 64


@contextmanager
def _sqlite_connection(path: Path) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    connection = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        with connection:
            yield connection
    finally:
        connection.close()


def _fake_dpapi_probe() -> str:
    return security.DPAPI_SCOPE_LOCAL_MACHINE


def _make_database(
    path: Path,
    *,
    marker: str = "source",
    include_secrets: bool = True,
) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with _sqlite_connection(path) as connection:
        connection.executescript(
            """
            CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
                version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE session_tokens (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                token_hash TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE model_configs (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                encrypted_api_key TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE optimizer_configs (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                encrypted_api_key TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE test_markers (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                value TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            """
        )
        connection.execute(
            "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
            (windows_deploy.SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION, "current"),
        )
        connection.execute("INSERT INTO test_markers(value) VALUES (?)", (marker,))
        if include_secrets:
            connection.execute(
                "INSERT INTO session_tokens(token_hash) VALUES (?)",
                (FAKE_TOKEN,),
            )
            connection.execute(
                "INSERT INTO model_configs(encrypted_api_key) VALUES (?)",
                (FAKE_DPAPI_REFERENCE,),
            )
            connection.execute(
                "INSERT INTO optimizer_configs(encrypted_api_key) VALUES (?)",
                ("ZmFrZS1sZWdhY3ktZHBhcGk=",),
            )


def _make_repo(root: Path, *, built: bool = True) -> Path:
    repo = root / "repo 中文"
    (repo / "backend" / "app").mkdir(parents=True)
    (repo / "backend" / "requirements.txt").write_text("pytest\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / "backend" / "app" / "launcher.py").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / "frontend").mkdir()
    (repo / "frontend" / "package-lock.json").write_text("{}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / "scripts" / "windows").mkdir(parents=True)
    (repo / "scripts" / "windows" / "start.ps1").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / ".venv" / "Scripts").mkdir(parents=True)
    (repo / ".venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe").write_bytes(b"fake")
    if built:
        (repo / "frontend" / "dist").mkdir()
        (repo / "frontend" / "dist" / "index.html").write_text(
            "<!doctype html>",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    return repo


def _make_data(root: Path, *, marker: str = "source", image: bytes = b"image") -> Path:
    data_dir = root / "数据 目录"
    _make_database(data_dir / "database" / "app.db", marker=marker)
    (data_dir / "images" / "嵌套 目录").mkdir(parents=True)
    (data_dir / "images" / "嵌套 目录" / "样例.jpg").write_bytes(image)
    (data_dir / "logs").mkdir()
    (data_dir / "logs" / "service.log").write_text(FAKE_TOKEN, encoding="utf-8")
    (data_dir / ".env").write_text("API_KEY=FAKE_DO_NOT_USE\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return data_dir


def _create_backup(tmp_path: Path, *, marker: str = "backup") -> tuple[Path, Path, Path]:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path / "repository")
    data_dir = _make_data(tmp_path / "source", marker=marker)
    backup = windows_deploy.create_backup(
        data_dir,
        backup_root=tmp_path / "备份 根目录",
        repo_root=repo,
        now=datetime(2026, 7, 30, 1, 2, 3, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    return repo, data_dir, backup


def _read_marker(database: Path) -> str:
    with _sqlite_connection(database) as connection:
        row = connection.execute("SELECT value FROM test_markers").fetchone()
    assert row is not None
    return str(row[0])


def _read_count(database: Path, table: str, where: str = "") -> int:
    with _sqlite_connection(database) as connection:
        row = connection.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table} {where}").fetchone()
    assert row is not None
    return int(row[0])


def test_readonly_connection_context_closes_handle(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    database = tmp_path / "app.db"
    _make_database(database)

    with windows_deploy._sqlite_connect_readonly(database) as connection:
        assert connection.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone() == (1,)

    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.ProgrammingError, match="closed database"):
        connection.execute("SELECT 1")


def test_database_replace_displaces_existing_file_before_installing_staged(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "database" / "app.db"
    staged = tmp_path / "stage" / "app.db"
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    staged.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    target.write_bytes(b"old")
    staged.write_bytes(b"new")
    real_replace = windows_deploy.os.replace
    calls: list[tuple[Path, Path]] = []

    def record_replace(source, destination) -> None:
        calls.append((Path(source), Path(destination)))
        real_replace(source, destination)

    monkeypatch.setattr(windows_deploy.os, "replace", record_replace)

    windows_deploy._replace_database(staged, target)

    assert target.read_bytes() == b"new"
    assert calls[0][0] == target
    assert calls[0][1].name.startswith(".app-db-displaced-")
    assert calls[1] == (staged, target)
    assert not list(target.parent.glob(".app-db-displaced-*"))


def test_database_replace_restores_displaced_file_when_install_fails(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "database" / "app.db"
    staged = tmp_path / "stage" / "app.db"
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    staged.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    target.write_bytes(b"old")
    staged.write_bytes(b"new")
    real_replace = windows_deploy.os.replace
    call_count = 0

    def fail_install(source, destination) -> None:
        nonlocal call_count
        call_count += 1
        if call_count == 2:
            raise PermissionError("injected staged install failure")
        real_replace(source, destination)

    monkeypatch.setattr(windows_deploy.os, "replace", fail_install)

    with pytest.raises(PermissionError, match="injected"):
        windows_deploy._replace_database(staged, target)

    assert target.read_bytes() == b"old"
    assert staged.read_bytes() == b"new"
    assert not list(target.parent.glob(".app-db-displaced-*"))


def test_windows_data_dir_priority_is_explicit_then_environment_then_dotenv_then_localappdata(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    dotenv_data = tmp_path / "dotenv data"
    (repo / ".env").write_text(f'DATA_DIR="{dotenv_data}"\n', encoding="utf-8")
    process_data = tmp_path / "process data"
    explicit_data = tmp_path / "explicit data"
    env = {
        "DATA_DIR": str(process_data),
        "LOCALAPPDATA": str(tmp_path / "Local App Data"),
    }

    assert windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(repo, explicit=explicit_data, env=env) == explicit_data
    assert windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(repo, env=env) == process_data
    assert windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(
        repo,
        env={"LOCALAPPDATA": str(tmp_path / "Local App Data")},
    ) == dotenv_data
    (repo / ".env").unlink()
    assert windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(repo, env=env | {"DATA_DIR": ""}) == (
        tmp_path / "Local App Data" / "3d66-label-system"
    )


def test_relative_data_dir_and_missing_localappdata_fail_closed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as relative_error:
        windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(repo, explicit=Path("relative-data"), env={})
    assert relative_error.value.code == "PATH_NOT_ABSOLUTE"

    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as default_error:
        windows_deploy.resolve_data_dir(repo, env={})
    assert default_error.value.code == "LOCALAPPDATA_MISSING"

    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    differently_cased = Path(str(repo / "data").upper())
    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as error:
        windows_deploy._require_outside_repo(differently_cased, repo, label="DATA_DIR")
    assert error.value.code == "PATH_INSIDE_REPOSITORY"

def test_doctor_validates_dpapi_references_and_runs_dpapi_probe(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    data_dir = _make_data(tmp_path / "runtime")
    probe_calls: list[bool] = []

    def probe() -> str:
        probe_calls.append(True)
        return security.DPAPI_SCOPE_CURRENT_USER

    report = windows_deploy.doctor(
        repo,
        data_dir=data_dir,
        platform_name="Windows",
        python_version=(3, 11, 9),
        node_version="v20.0.0",
        npm_version="10.0.0",
        dpapi_probe=probe,
    )

    assert report.database_exists is True
    assert report.schema_version == windows_deploy.SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION
    assert probe_calls == [True]
    assert "Windows DPAPI current-user 内存回环" in report.checks

    with _sqlite_connection(data_dir / "database" / "app.db") as connection:
        connection.execute(
            "UPDATE model_configs SET encrypted_api_key='keychain:v1:model-config'"
        )
    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as error:
        windows_deploy.doctor(
            repo,
            data_dir=data_dir,
            platform_name="Windows",
            python_version=(3, 11, 9),
            node_version="v20.0.0",
            npm_version="10.0.0",
            dpapi_probe=probe,
        )
    assert error.value.code == "CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE_UNSAFE"


def test_doctor_accepts_machine_scope_reference_and_rejects_failed_probe(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    data_dir = _make_data(tmp_path / "runtime")
    with _sqlite_connection(data_dir / "database" / "app.db") as connection:
        connection.execute(
            "UPDATE model_configs SET encrypted_api_key=?",
            (FAKE_DPAPI_MACHINE_REFERENCE,),
        )

    def failed_probe() -> str:
        raise security.SecretStorageError(
            "safe DPAPI failure",
            reason="DPAPI_PROTECT_FAILED",
            system_error=2148073483,
        )

    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as error:
        windows_deploy.doctor(
            repo,
            data_dir=data_dir,
            platform_name="Windows",
            python_version=(3, 11, 9),
            node_version="v20.0.0",
            npm_version="10.0.0",
            dpapi_probe=failed_probe,
        )

    assert error.value.code == "DPAPI_ROUND_TRIP_FAILED"
    assert "DPAPI_PROTECT_FAILED" in str(error.value)
    assert "2148073483" in str(error.value)


def test_doctor_cli_prints_checklist_summary_and_zero_exit(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path)
    data_dir = tmp_path / "runtime data"
    monkeypatch.setattr(windows_deploy.platform, "system", lambda: "Windows")
    monkeypatch.setattr(windows_deploy.sys, "version_info", (3, 12, 4))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        windows_deploy,
        "_command_version",
        lambda command: "v20.1.0" if command == "node.exe" else "10.2.0",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        windows_deploy,
        "probe_windows_dpapi",
        _fake_dpapi_probe,
    )

    exit_code = windows_deploy.main(
        ["doctor", "--repo-root", str(repo), "--data-dir", str(data_dir)]
    )

    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert "[OK] Windows 平台" in captured.out
    assert f"[OK] DATA_DIR={data_dir}" in captured.out
    assert "诊断汇总：全部门禁通过" in captured.out
    assert captured.err == ""

def test_backup_uses_sqlite_api_and_sanitizes_sensitive_data(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    _repo, _source, backup = _create_backup(tmp_path)
    database = backup / "database" / "app.db"
    manifest = json.loads((backup / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    assert backup.name == "3d66-backup-v1-20260730T010203Z"
    assert manifest["schema"] == windows_deploy.BACKUP_SCHEMA
    assert manifest["source"]["platform"] == "windows"
    assert _read_count(database, "session_tokens") == 0
    assert _read_count(
        database,
        "model_configs",
        "WHERE encrypted_api_key IS NOT NULL",
    ) == 0
    assert _read_count(
        database,
        "optimizer_configs",
        "WHERE encrypted_api_key IS NOT NULL",
    ) == 0
    assert not (backup / "logs").exists()
    assert not (backup / ".env").exists()
    all_bytes = b"".join(path.read_bytes() for path in backup.rglob("*") if path.is_file())
    assert FAKE_TOKEN.encode() not in all_bytes
    validation = windows_deploy.validate_backup(backup)
    assert validation.file_count == 2


def test_active_wal_database_backup_contains_latest_committed_state(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path / "repository")
    data_dir = _make_data(tmp_path / "source", marker="before")
    source_database = data_dir / "database" / "app.db"
    connection = sqlite3.connect(source_database)
    try:
        connection.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        connection.execute("UPDATE test_markers SET value='committed-in-wal'")
        connection.commit()
        backup = windows_deploy.create_backup(
            data_dir,
            backup_root=tmp_path / "backups",
            repo_root=repo,
        )
    finally:
        connection.close()

    assert _read_marker(backup / "database" / "app.db") == "committed-in-wal"


def test_backup_rejects_symlinked_image_and_backup_root(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    repo = _make_repo(tmp_path / "repository")
    data_dir = _make_data(tmp_path / "source")
    outside = tmp_path / "outside.jpg"
    outside.write_bytes(b"outside")
    (data_dir / "images" / "escape.jpg").symlink_to(outside)

    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as image_error:
        windows_deploy.create_backup(
            data_dir,
            backup_root=tmp_path / "backups",
            repo_root=repo,
        )
    assert image_error.value.code == "REPARSE_POINT_REJECTED"

    (data_dir / "images" / "escape.jpg").unlink()
    real_root = tmp_path / "real backups"
    real_root.mkdir()
    linked_root = tmp_path / "linked backups"
    linked_root.symlink_to(real_root, target_is_directory=True)
    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as root_error:
        windows_deploy.create_backup(
            data_dir,
            backup_root=linked_root,
            repo_root=repo,
        )
    assert root_error.value.code == "REPARSE_POINT_REJECTED"


def test_reparse_attribute_is_rejected_even_without_symlink(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "junction-like" / "data"
    junction = target.parent
    junction.mkdir()
    original = windows_deploy._is_reparse_point
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        windows_deploy,
        "_is_reparse_point",
        lambda path: path == junction or original(path),
    )
    with pytest.raises(windows_deploy.DeployError) as error:
        windows_deploy._normalized_path(target, label="DATA_DIR")
    assert error.value.code == "REPARSE_POINT_REJECTED"

def test_tampered_backup_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    _repo, _source, backup = _create_backup(tmp_path)
    image = backup / "images" / "嵌套 目录" / "样例.jpg"
    image.write_bytes(image.read_bytes() + b"tampered")
