def _engine(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-composer.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def _seed_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    stage: str = "A",
    version: str = "v1",
    status: str = "draft",
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> PromptVersion:
    if db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
        )
    ) is None:
        db.add(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key=category_key,
                display_name="工作流测试类目",
                status="draft",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                preprocess_config_json="{}",
                pipeline_config_json="{}",
                created_by="admin",
            )
        )
        db.flush()
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        category_key=category_key,
        pipeline_scope="shared",
        stage=stage,
        name=f"工作流提示词 {stage}",
        version=version,
        system_prompt=f"这是工作流提示词 {stage} 的完整系统提示词内容。",
        user_prompt="请按照工作流字段合同完成评测并返回结构化结果。",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
        status=status,
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(prompt)
    db.flush()
    return prompt


def _seed_model(db: Session, *, active: bool = True) -> ModelRegistryEntry:
    model = ModelRegistryEntry(
        role="main",
        name="工作流测试模型",
        provider="fixture",
        protocol="custom_json",
        capabilities_json='["text","structured_output"]',
        description="仅用于工作流 dry-run 合同测试",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id="fixture-workflow-model",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        level="standard",
        structured_output=True,
        active=active,
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(model)
    db.flush()
    return model


def _seed_rule(
    db: Session,
    *,
    revision: int = 1,
    status: str = "candidate",
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> CategoryEvaluationV3Revision:
    rule = CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
        category_key=category_key,
        display_name="工作流等级规则",
        revision=revision,
        status=status,
        contract_json='{"schema_version":"evaluation-category-profile-v3"}',
        classification_map_json='{"format_version":"classification-map-v1"}',
        subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
        dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
        media_penalty_enabled=True,
        contract_hash="a" * 64,
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(rule)
    db.flush()
    return rule


def _manifest(model_registry_id: int = 1, rule_revision_id: int = 1) -> dict:
    schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"payload": {"type": "object"}}}
    error_policy = {"kind": "fail"}
    retry_policy = {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 2, "delay_seconds": 0}
    evidence = ["request_id", "trace_id"]
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
        "category_key": "model_3d_su",
        "lane_key": "quality",
        "run_modes": ["labeling", "evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression"],
        "knowledge_graph_contract": {
            "schema_version": "kg-fields-v1",
            "fields": [
                {
                    "key": "quality_level",
                    "type": "string",
                    "direction": "write",
                    "required": False,
                }
            ],
        },
        "nodes": [
            {
                "key": "upstream",
                "kind": "upstream_connector",
                "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.input.a"},
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "prompt-a",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "config": {
                    "prompt_ref": "prompt:A@v1",
                    "model_registry_id": model_registry_id,
                    "max_tokens": 4096,
                    "thinking_level": "medium",
                },
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "split",
                "kind": "branch",
                "config": {
                    "branch_key": "review_paths",
                    "branches": ["redline", "contract"],
                },
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "redline",
                "kind": "rule_contract",
                "config": {
                    "contract_ref": "rule:model_3d_su@r1",
                    "rule_revision_id": rule_revision_id,
                },
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "contract",
                "kind": "level_matcher",
                "config": {
                    "matcher_ref": "matcher:model_3d_su@r1",
                    "rule_revision_id": rule_revision_id,
                },
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "join",
                "kind": "join",
                "config": {"join_for": "review_paths", "mode": "all"},
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
            {
                "key": "downstream",
                "kind": "downstream_projection",
                "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.output.a"},
                "input_schema": schema,
                "output_schema": schema,
                "error_policy": error_policy,
                "retry_policy": retry_policy,
                "evidence_fields": evidence,
            },
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt-a"},
            {"from": "prompt-a", "to": "split"},
            {"from": "split", "to": "redline", "branch": "redline"},
            {"from": "split", "to": "contract", "branch": "contract"},
            {"from": "redline", "to": "join"},
            {"from": "contract", "to": "join"},
            {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 2},
    }


def test_generic_category_workflow_does_not_require_knowledge_graph_contract(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with session() as db:
        _seed_prompt(db)
        model = _seed_model(db)
        rule = _seed_rule(db)
        manifest = _manifest(model.id, rule.id)
        manifest.pop("knowledge_graph_contract")

        report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest)
        assert report.ok is True

        definition = create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key="model_3d_su.generic_quality",
            name="通用评测类目工作流",
            description="不绑定知识图谱也可以编排",
            owner="platform",
            allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
            created_by="admin",
        )
        version = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version="1",
            manifest=manifest,
            created_by="admin",
        )
        persisted = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
        assert "knowledge_graph_contract" not in persisted
        assert version.canonical_hash == hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(persisted).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()


def test_v2_manifest_rejects_reserved_policy_keys_and_unapproved_config(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        _seed_prompt(db)
        model = _seed_model(db)
        rule = _seed_rule(db)
        manifest = _manifest(model.id, rule.id)
        manifest["resource_policy"]["knowledge_graph_contract"] = {
            "schema_version": "untrusted",
            "fields": [],
        }
        manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["sql_text"] = "not executable"
        manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["token"] = "must-not-persist"
        manifest["nodes"][0]["token"] = "must-not-persist"
        manifest["nodes"][0]["retry_policy"]["sql_text"] = "not executable"
        manifest["nodes"][1]["error_policy"]["password"] = "must-not-persist"
        manifest["resource_policy"]["api_key"] = "must-not-persist"
        manifest["edges"][0]["credential"] = "must-not-persist"

        report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest)
        codes = {item.code for item in report.errors}
        assert "workflow_resource_policy_reserved" in codes
        assert "workflow_node_config_field_unknown" in codes
        assert "workflow_contract_field_unknown" in codes
        rejected_paths = {item.path for item in report.errors}
        assert "nodes[0].token" in rejected_paths
        assert "nodes[0].retry_policy.sql_text" in rejected_paths
        assert "nodes[1].error_policy.password" in rejected_paths
        assert "resource_policy.api_key" in rejected_paths
        assert "edges[0].credential" in rejected_paths


def test_v2_definition_rejects_manifest_from_another_category(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        _seed_prompt(db)
        model = _seed_model(db)
        rule = _seed_rule(db)
        definition = create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key="category.bound",
            name="类目绑定工作流",
            description="",
            owner="platform",
            allowed_categories=["space_image"],
            created_by="admin",
        )

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRegistryError) as error:
            create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version="1",
                manifest=_manifest(model.id, rule.id),
                created_by="admin",
            )
        assert error.value.code == "workflow_category_not_allowed"


def test_v2_composer_manifest_accepts_parallel_join_and_prompt_refs(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            prompt = _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            rule = _seed_rule(db)
            report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, _manifest(model.id, rule.id))
            assert report.ok, report.as_dict()

            definition = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key="composer.test",
                name="Composer",
                description="",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
                created_by="admin",
            )
            version = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version="1",
                manifest=_manifest(model.id, rule.id),
                created_by="admin",
            )
            persisted = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
            assert persisted[1]["config"]["prompt_ref"] == "prompt:A@v1"

            snapshot, _ = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
                db,
                version.id,
                {"environment": "dry_run", "category_key": "model_3d_su"},
            )
            assert snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["lane_key"] == "quality"
            assert snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["run_modes"][-1] == "stock_regression"
            assert snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["knowledge_graph_contract"]["fields"][0]["key"] == "quality_level"
            assert snapshot["prompts"] == [
                {
                    "reference": "prompt:A@v1",
                    "prompt_id": prompt.id,
                    "category_key": "model_3d_su",
                    "stage": "A",
                    "version": "v1",
                    "status": "draft",
                    "rubric_version": "rubric-v2.1",
                    "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
                    "content_sha256": snapshot["prompts"][0]["content_sha256"],
                }
            ]
            assert len(snapshot["prompts"][0]["content_sha256"]) == 64
            assert snapshot["models"] == [
                {
                    "model_registry_id": model.id,
                    "name": "工作流测试模型",
                    "provider": "fixture",
                    "protocol": "custom_json",
                    "model_id": "fixture-workflow-model",
                    "role": "main",
                    "capabilities": ["text", "structured_output"],
                    "structured_output": True,
                }
            ]
            assert snapshot["nodes"][1]["config"]["max_tokens"] == 4096
            assert snapshot["nodes"][1]["config"]["thinking_level"] == "medium"
            assert snapshot["rules"][0]["rule_revision_id"] == rule.id
            assert snapshot["rules"][0]["revision"] == 1
            assert snapshot["rules"][0]["contract_hash"] == "a" * 64
            assert snapshot["rules"][0]["contract"]["schema_version"] == (
                "evaluation-category-profile-v3"
            )
            assert snapshot["nodes"][2]["kind"] == "branch"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v2_prompt_thinking_switch_is_explicit_but_legacy_compatible(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            _seed_rule(db)

            legacy = _manifest(model.id)
            assert validate_workflow_manifest(db, legacy).ok is True

            disabled = deepcopy(legacy)
            disabled["nodes"][1]["config"].update(
                {"thinking_level": "none", "thinking_enabled": False}
            )
            assert validate_workflow_manifest(db, disabled).ok is True

            inconsistent = deepcopy(disabled)
            inconsistent["nodes"][1]["config"]["thinking_level"] = "high"
            codes = {
                item.code
                for item in validate_workflow_manifest(db, inconsistent).errors
            }
            assert "workflow_model_thinking_configuration_inconsistent" in codes
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v2_foreach_nested_prompt_defaults_thinking_off(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            manifest = _manifest()
            prompt = manifest["nodes"][1]
            prompt["kind"] = "foreach_image"
            prompt["config"] = {
                "items_path": "$.case.images",
                "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                "max_items": 1000,
                "max_parallel_items": 4,
                "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                "item_executor": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "prompt_ref": "prompt:A@v1",
                    "model_registry_id": 1,
                    "max_tokens": 4096,
                },
            }

            normalized = normalize_composer_manifest(manifest)
            executor = normalized["nodes"][1]["config"]["item_executor"]

            assert executor["thinking_enabled"] is False
            assert executor["thinking_level"] == "none"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v2_foreach_accepts_strict_nested_prompt_and_rejects_expression_path(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            _seed_rule(db)
            manifest = _manifest(model.id)
            node = manifest["nodes"][1]
            node["kind"] = "foreach_image"
            node["config"] = {
                "items_path": "$.case.images",
                "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                "max_items": 1000,
                "max_parallel_items": 4,
                "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                "item_executor": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "prompt_ref": "prompt:A@v1",
                    "model_registry_id": model.id,
                    "max_tokens": 4096,
                    "thinking_level": "none",
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "structured_output": True,
                    "output_contract": {
                        "mode": "json_schema",
                        "name": "case_image_result",
                        "description": "案例单图结果",
                        "strict": True,
                        "schema": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                            "required": ["score"],
                            "additionalProperties": False,
                        },
                    },
                },
            }

            assert validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest).ok is True

            node["config"]["items_path"] = "$.images[*]"
            codes = {
                item.code for item in validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest).errors
            }
            assert "foreach_path_invalid" in codes
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_v2_composer_workflow_runs_end_to_end_with_controlled_fixtures(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            rule = _seed_rule(db)
            definition = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key="composer.runtime",
                name="Composer runtime",
                description="",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
                created_by="admin",
            )
            version = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version="1",
                manifest=_manifest(model.id, rule.id),
                created_by="admin",
            )
            version.status = "active"
            version.validation_report_json = '{"ok":true,"errors":[]}'
            db.commit()

            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="composer-runtime-1",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "fixture:001"},
                    owner="platform",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            db.commit()
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            assert len(snapshot["scripts"]) == 7
            assert all(
                item["executor_kind"] == "deterministic_fixture"
                for item in snapshot["scripts"]
            )
            attempts = db.query(ProductionStepAttempt).filter_by(run_id=run.id).all()
            assert {attempt.step_type for attempt in attempts} == {
                "connector",
                "model_call",
                "rule_eval",
                "projection",
                "transform",
                "reconcile",
            }
            run_id = run.id

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while executor.process_runtime_step_once("composer-fixture-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 7

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            completed = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert completed is not None
            assert completed.status == "succeeded"
            assert completed.completed_steps == 7
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v2_asset_keys_validate_category_kind_and_binding(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            prompt = _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            rule = _seed_rule(db)
            prompt_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="prompt_a",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="提示词 A",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            rule_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="rule_contract",
                kind="rule_contract",
                display_name="规则合同",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            matcher_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="level_matcher",
                kind="level_matcher",
                display_name="等级撮合",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_prompt_version(
                db,
                asset=prompt_asset,
                prompt_version=prompt,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_rule_revision(
                db,
                asset=rule_asset,
                rule_revision=rule,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_rule_revision(
                db,
                asset=matcher_asset,
                rule_revision=rule,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            manifest = _manifest(model.id, rule.id)
            manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            manifest["nodes"][3]["config"]["asset_key"] = "rule_contract"
            manifest["nodes"][4]["config"]["asset_key"] = "level_matcher"
            assert validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest).ok is True

            manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["asset_key"] = "rule_contract"
            report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest)
            assert "workflow_asset_kind_mismatch" in {
                item.code for item in report.errors
            }

            unbound = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="prompt_unbound",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="未绑定提示词",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            assert isinstance(unbound, WorkflowNodeAsset)
            manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_unbound"
            report = validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest)
            assert "workflow_asset_version_unbound" in {
                item.code for item in report.errors
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_pure_foundation_rule_revision_binds_and_freezes_in_workflow(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            prompt = _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            dimensions = deepcopy(build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions())
            for track in dimensions.values():
                for group_key in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
                    group = track.get(group_key)
                    if not isinstance(group, dict):
                        continue
                    for dimension in group["schema_definition"].get("dimensions", []):
                        dimension.pop("grade_points", None)
                        dimension["dimension_score_cap"] = 100
                        dimension["dimension_deduction_cap"] = 100
                        dimension["deduction_rules"] = []
                        dimension["bonus_rules"] = []
                validate_subcategory_dimensions(track)

            contract = build_model_3d_su_contract()
            rule = CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                display_name="纯基础分规则合同",
                revision=1,
                status="candidate",
                contract_json=canonical_json(contract),
                classification_map_json=canonical_json(
                    build_model_3d_su_classification_map()
                ),
                subcategory_dimensions_json=canonical_json(dimensions),
                dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
                media_penalty_enabled=False,
                contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(contract),
                created_by="admin",
            )
            db.add(rule)
            db.flush()

            prompt_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="prompt_a",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="提示词 A",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            rule_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="rule_contract",
                kind="rule_contract",
                display_name="纯基础分规则合同",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            matcher_asset = create_workflow_asset(
                db,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                asset_key="level_matcher",
                kind="level_matcher",
                display_name="纯基础分等级撮合",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_prompt_version(
                db,
                asset=prompt_asset,
                prompt_version=prompt,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_rule_revision(
                db,
                asset=rule_asset,
                rule_revision=rule,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            bind_rule_revision(
                db,
                asset=matcher_asset,
                rule_revision=rule,
                created_by="admin",
            )

            manifest = _manifest(model.id, rule.id)
            manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            manifest["nodes"][3]["config"]["asset_key"] = "rule_contract"
            manifest["nodes"][4]["config"]["asset_key"] = "level_matcher"
            assert validate_workflow_manifest(db, manifest).ok is True

            definition = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key="model_3d_su.pure_foundation",
                name="纯基础分评测工作流",
                description="验证零规则合同可被工作流安全冻结",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
                created_by="admin",
            )
            version = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version="1",
                manifest=manifest,
                created_by="admin",
            )
            snapshot, _ = canonical_workflow_snapshot(db, version.id, {})
            frozen_dimensions = snapshot["rules"][0]["subcategory_dimensions"]
            frozen_nodes = [
                dimension
                for track in frozen_dimensions.values()
                for group_key in ("common_group", "specific_group")
                if isinstance((group := track.get(group_key)), dict)
                for dimension in group["schema_definition"].get("dimensions", [])
            ]
            assert frozen_nodes
            assert all(node["deduction_rules"] == [] for node in frozen_nodes)
            assert all(node["bonus_rules"] == [] for node in frozen_nodes)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_saving_legacy_v2_manifest_appends_asset_keys_without_rewriting_input(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_prompt(db)
            model = _seed_model(db)
            rule = _seed_rule(db)
            definition = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key="model_3d_su.asset-compatibility",
                name="资产兼容工作流",
                description="",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
                created_by="admin",
            )
            legacy_manifest = _manifest(model.id, rule.id)
            version = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version="1",
                manifest=legacy_manifest,
                created_by="admin",
            )

            persisted = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            keyed = {
                node["key"]: node["config"].get("asset_key")
                for node in persisted["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] in {"prompt", "rule_contract", "level_matcher"}
            }
            assert keyed == {
                "prompt-a": "prompt_a",
                "redline": "redline",
                "contract": "contract",
            }
            assert all(
                "asset_key" not in node["config"]
                for node in legacy_manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] in {"prompt", "rule_contract", "level_matcher"}
            )
            assert len(
                db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                        WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "model_3d_su"
                    )
                ).all()
            ) == 3

            snapshot, _ = canonical_workflow_snapshot(db, version.id, {})
            assert {node["config"]["asset_key"] for node in snapshot["nodes"] if node["kind"] in {"prompt", "rule_contract", "level_matcher"}} == {
                "prompt_a",
                "redline",
                "contract",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_conditions.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest


def test_condition_evaluates_compound_frozen_facts_and_reports_references() -> None:
    """Changing an upstream route fact must change the real decision result."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import evaluate_condition

    condition = {
        "all": [
            {
                "op": "eq",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                "value": "domestic",
            },
            {
                "op": "eq",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.is_single",
                "value": 0,
            },
        ]
    }
    facts = {
        "steps": {
            "upstream": {
                "output": {"source": "domestic", "is_single": 0}
            }
        }
    }

    result = evaluate_condition(condition, facts)

    assert result.matched is True
    assert result.referenced_facts == {
        "steps.upstream.output.is_single": 0,
        "steps.upstream.output.source": "domestic",
    }
    assert len(result.condition_hash) == 64


def test_condition_hash_is_independent_of_runtime_values() -> None:
    """Route replay must identify the same frozen condition across inputs."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import evaluate_condition

    condition = {
        "op": "eq",
        "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
        "value": "domestic",
    }

    matched = evaluate_condition(
        condition,
        {"steps": {"upstream": {"output": {"source": "domestic"}}}},
    )
    missed = evaluate_condition(
        condition,
        {"steps": {"upstream": {"output": {"source": "overseas"}}}},
    )

    assert matched.matched is True
    assert missed.matched is False
    assert matched.condition_hash == missed.condition_hash


def test_condition_validation_rejects_code_and_unknown_fact_paths() -> None:
    """Manifest authors must not escape the approved operator and fact set."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import validate_condition

    unsafe = validate_condition(
        {"op": "eval", "path": "secrets.token", "expression": "True"},
        allowed_steps={"upstream"},
        allowed_roots={"steps"},
    )

    assert {error.code for error in unsafe} == {
        "condition_field_unknown",
        "condition_operator_unsupported",
        "condition_path_invalid",
    }


def test_condition_validation_rejects_unreachable_step_reference() -> None:
    """A branch may read only a declared reachable predecessor output."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import validate_condition

    errors = validate_condition(
        {
            "op": "eq",
            "path": "steps.downstream.output.source",
            "value": "domestic",
        },
        allowed_steps={"upstream"},
        allowed_roots={"steps"},
    )

    assert [error.code for error in errors] == ["condition_path_unknown"]


def test_condition_supports_membership_numeric_comparison_and_not() -> None:
    """The whitelist covers routing needs without evaluating expressions."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import evaluate_condition

    facts = {
        "steps": {
            "upstream": {
                "output": {"source": "domestic", "confidence": 0.92}
            }
        }
    }
    condition = {
        "all": [
            {
                "op": "in",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                "value": ["domestic", "overseas"],
            },
            {
                "op": "gte",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.confidence",
                "value": 0.9,
            },
            {
                "not": {
                    "op": "eq",
                    "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                    "value": "unknown",
                }
            },
        ]
    }

    assert evaluate_condition(condition, facts).matched is True


def test_condition_evaluation_rejects_secret_fact_root_even_when_present() -> None:
    """Providing a secret mapping must not make it an approved route input."""

    from app.workflow_conditions import (
        WorkflowConditionError,
        evaluate_condition,
    )

    with pytest.raises(WorkflowConditionError) as exc_info:
        evaluate_condition(
            {"op": "eq", "path": "secrets.token", "value": "hidden"},
            {"secrets": {"token": "hidden"}},
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "condition_path_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_connector_executor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import IntakeRequest, ingest_upstream_event
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version
from app.workflow_node_executors import NodeExecutionContext, execute_native_node
from app.workflow_registry import canonical_workflow_snapshot
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    complete_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
)


def _hash(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    key: str,
    direction: str,
    fingerprint: str,
):
    return create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key=key,
        direction=direction,
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        logical_locator=(
            "logical.knowledge_graph.input"
            if direction == "upstream"
            else "logical.knowledge_graph.output"
        ),
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"},
        cursor_contract={
            "field": "sequence",
            "kind": "integer",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": "delay",
        },
        idempotency_contract={"field": "event_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="platform",
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )


def _node(kind: str, key: str) -> dict:
    node_id = "upstream" if kind == "upstream_connector" else "downstream"
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": "读取上游" if kind == "upstream_connector" else "写入下游",
        "config": {"connector_key": key},
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 2},
        "evidence_fields": [],
    }


def _version(db: Session, nodes: list[dict]) -> WorkflowVersion:
    for node in nodes:
        ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, node["kind"])
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"knowledge_graph.connector.{len(nodes)}.{nodes[0]['kind']}",
        name="知识图谱连接器工作流",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["knowledge_graph"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    edges = [
        {
            "from": source["node_instance_id"],
            "to": target["node_instance_id"],
        }
        for source, target in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "lane_key": "incremental",
        "run_modes": ["stock_regression", "incremental"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
        "queue_class": "production_batch",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {},
        "layout": {},
    }
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(edges),
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["stock_regression", "incremental"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=_hash(manifest),
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def _snapshot(contract_id: int, direction: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "connector-contract-snapshot-v1",
        "connector_contract_id": contract_id,
        "contract_key": f"knowledge-graph-{direction}",
        "contract_version": 1,
        "direction": direction,
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "logical_locator": f"logical.knowledge_graph.{direction}",
        "adapter_key": "api-json-v1",
        "schema_fingerprint": "a" * 64,
        "field_mapping": {"content_id": "content_id"},
        "cursor_contract": {},
        "idempotency_contract": {"field": "event_id"},
        "batch_size": 100,
        "timeout_seconds": 30,
        "contract_hash": "b" * 64,
    }


def test_stock_regression_bypasses_external_io_but_keeps_contract_evidence() -> None:
    upstream = _node("upstream_connector", "knowledge-graph-upstream")
    upstream["config"]["connector_snapshot"] = _snapshot(11, "upstream")
    upstream_result = execute_native_node(
        NodeExecutionContext(
            run_snapshot={
                "workflow": {
                    "manifest": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                        "lane_key": "generic",
                    }
                },
                "runtime_context": {"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
            },
            node=upstream,
            input_manifest={"content_id": "asset-1", "token": "must-not-leak"},
            raw_fixture_output={},
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )
    )

    downstream = _node("downstream_projection", "knowledge-graph-downstream")
    downstream["config"]["connector_snapshot"] = _snapshot(12, "downstream")
    downstream_result = execute_native_node(
        NodeExecutionContext(
            run_snapshot={
                "workflow": {"manifest": {"schema_version": "workflow-v3"}},
                "runtime_context": {"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
            },
            node=downstream,
            input_manifest={"content_id": "asset-1"},
            raw_fixture_output={},
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )
    )

    assert upstream_result["output"] == {"content_id": "asset-1"}
    assert upstream_result["io_mode"] == "imported_sample"
    assert upstream_result["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert upstream_result["connector_contract_id"] == 11
    assert upstream_result["contract_version"] == 1
    assert upstream_result["schema_fingerprint"] == "a" * 64
    assert upstream_result["payload_hash"] == _hash(
        {"content_id": "asset-1", "token": "must-not-leak"}
    )
    assert downstream_result["io_mode"] == "evidence_only"
    assert downstream_result["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert downstream_result["connector_contract_id"] == 12


def test_incremental_snapshot_freezes_exact_connector_versions() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            upstream = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="a" * 64,
            )
            downstream = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-downstream",
                direction="downstream",
                fingerprint="b" * 64,
            )
            version = _version(
                db,
                [
                    _node("upstream_connector", upstream.contract_key),
                    _node("downstream_projection", downstream.contract_key),
                ],
            )

            snapshot, _digest = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
                db,
                version.id,
                {
                    "run_mode": "incremental",
                    "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                    "input_manifest": {"content_id": "asset-1"},
                },
            )
            frozen_nodes = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]

            assert frozen_nodes[0]["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                "connector_contract_id"
            ] == upstream.id
            assert frozen_nodes[1]["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                "connector_contract_id"
            ] == downstream.id
            assert frozen_nodes[0]["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                "schema_fingerprint"
            ] == "a" * 64
            assert "dsn" not in canonical_json(frozen_nodes).lower()
            assert "password" not in canonical_json(frozen_nodes).lower()

            newer = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="c" * 64,
            )
            assert newer.version == 2
            assert frozen_nodes[0]["config"]["connector_snapshot"][
                "connector_contract_id"
            ] == upstream.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stock_snapshot_does_not_drift_when_connector_is_registered_later() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _version(
                db,
                [_node("upstream_connector", "knowledge-graph-upstream")],
            )
            context = {
                "run_mode": "stock_regression",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "input_manifest": {"content_id": "asset-1"},
            }
            before, before_hash = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
                db,
                version.id,
                context,
            )
            _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="a" * 64,
            )
            after, after_hash = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
                db,
                version.id,
                context,
            )

            assert before_hash == after_hash
            assert before == after
            assert "connector_snapshot" not in after["workflow"]["manifest"][
                "nodes"
            ][0]["config"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_intake_event_override_applies_only_to_its_matching_upstream_node() -> None:
    from app.workflow_connector_executor import freeze_workflow_connector_contracts

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            primary = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="a" * 64,
            )
            secondary = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-metadata",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="b" * 64,
            )
            manifest = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "nodes": [
                    _node("upstream_connector", primary.contract_key),
                    {
                        **_node("upstream_connector", secondary.contract_key),
                        "node_instance_id": "metadata",
                    },
                ],
            }

            frozen = freeze_workflow_connector_contracts(
                db,
                manifest,
                runtime_context={
                    "run_mode": "incremental",
                    "upstream_intake_contract_version_id": primary.id,
                    "upstream_intake_contract_key": primary.contract_key,
                },
            )

            assert frozen["nodes"][0]["config"]["connector_contract_version_id"] == (
                primary.id
            )
            assert frozen["nodes"][1]["config"]["connector_contract_version_id"] == (
                secondary.id
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_accepted_intake_event_binds_one_incremental_run_with_exact_payload() -> None:
    from app.workflow_connector_executor import create_incremental_run_from_intake

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            upstream = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="a" * 64,
            )
            version = _version(
                db,
                [_node("upstream_connector", upstream.contract_key)],
            )
            payload = {"content_id": "asset-101", "sequence": 101}
            decision = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                upstream,
                IntakeRequest(
                    source_event_id="evt-101",
                    source_cursor="101",
                    source_occurred_at=None,
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    mechanism_key="",
                    payload=payload,
                    actor="connector",
                ),
            )

            first = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
                db,
                event_id=decision.event.id,
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                actor="connector-worker",
            )
            second = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
                db,
                event_id=decision.event.id,
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                actor="connector-worker",
            )
            db.commit()

            event = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, decision.event.id)
            assert event is not None
            assert event.production_run_id == first.id == second.id
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(type(first))) == 1
            frozen = json.loads(first.snapshot_json)
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"] == payload
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["upstream_intake_event_id"] == event.id
            assert frozen["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "connector_snapshot"
            ]["connector_contract_id"] == upstream.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_workflow_with_upstream_node_rejects_unbound_input() -> None:
    from app.workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            upstream = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
                direction="upstream",
                fingerprint="a" * 64,
            )
            version = _version(
                db,
                [_node("upstream_connector", upstream.contract_key)],
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc_info:
                create_production_run(
                    db,
                    ProductionRunRequest(
                        workflow_version_id=version.id,
                        idempotency_key="spoofed-incremental-input",
                        category_key="knowledge_graph",
                        queue_class="production_batch",
                        runtime_context={"run_mode": "incremental"},
                        input_manifest={"content_id": "not-an-intake-event"},
                        owner="test",
                        source_type="upstream_intake_event",
                        source_id=999,
                    ),
                    actor="test",
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "upstream_intake_binding_required"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_outbox_keeps_run_frozen_downstream_version() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            downstream_v1 = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-downstream",
                direction="downstream",
                fingerprint="b" * 64,
            )
            version = _version(
                db,
                [_node("downstream_projection", downstream_v1.contract_key)],
            )
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="kg-incremental-asset-201",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    queue_class="production_batch",
                    runtime_context={"run_mode": "incremental"},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "asset-201", "asset_id": 201},
                    owner="test",
                    source_type="upstream_intake_event",
                    source_id=201,
                ),
                actor="test",
            )
            frozen = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            assert frozen["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "connector_snapshot"
            ]["connector_contract_id"] == downstream_v1.id
            downstream_v2 = _contract(
                db,
                key="knowledge-graph-downstream",
                direction="downstream",
                fingerprint="c" * 64,
            )
            assert downstream_v2.version == 2
            db.commit()

            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, "projection-worker")
            assert attempt_id is not None
            db.commit()
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            assert attempt is not None and attempt.lease_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                    "node_instance_id": "downstream",
                    "node_kind": "downstream_projection",
                    "status": "succeeded",
                    "output": {"content_id": "asset-201", "labels": {}},
                },
            )
            db.commit()

            events = db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)).all()
            assert len(events) == 1
            assert events[0].target_contract_version_id == downstream_v1.id
            assert events[0].target_contract_version_id != downstream_v2.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_intake_to_native_workflow_to_outbox_keeps_frozen_contracts(
    mysql_engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        upstream = _contract(
            db,
            key="knowledge-graph-upstream",
            direction="upstream",
            fingerprint="a" * 64,
        )
        downstream = _contract(
            db,
            key="knowledge-graph-downstream",
            direction="downstream",
            fingerprint="b" * 64,
        )
        version = _version(
            db,
            [
                _node("upstream_connector", upstream.contract_key),
                _node("downstream_projection", downstream.contract_key),
            ],
        )
        decision = ingest_upstream_event(
            db,
            upstream,
            IntakeRequest(
                source_event_id="evt-mysql-301",
                source_cursor="301",
                source_occurred_at=None,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                mechanism_key="",
                payload={"content_id": "asset-301", "sequence": 301},
                actor="connector",
            ),
        )
        from app.workflow_connector_executor import create_incremental_run_from_intake

        run = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
            db,
            event_id=decision.event.id,
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            actor="connector-worker",
        )
        run_id = run.id
        db.commit()

        while True:
            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, "mysql-native-worker")
            db.commit()
            if attempt_id is None:
                break
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert attempt is not None and attempt.lease_token and run is not None
            run_snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            node = next(
                node
                for node in run_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if node["node_instance_id"] == attempt.step_key
            )
            result = execute_native_node(
                NodeExecutionContext(
                    run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                    raw_fixture_output={},
                    attempt_no=attempt.attempt_no,
                    started_at=attempt.started_at,
                    input_fingerprint=attempt.input_hash,
                )
            )
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                result,
            )
            db.commit()

        persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        event = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, decision.event.id)
        outbox = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent))
        assert persisted is not None and persisted.status == "succeeded"
        assert event is not None and event.production_run_id == run_id
        assert outbox is not None
        assert outbox.target_contract_version_id == downstream.id
        assert outbox.mechanism_version_id == version.id

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_continuation_acceptance.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline


def _module():
    script = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "scripts"
        / "workflow-continuation-acceptance.py"
    )
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "workflow_continuation_acceptance",
        script,
    )
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_workflow_continuation_acceptance_reconciles_every_runtime_mode(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    report = _module().run_acceptance(tmp_path / "acceptance.db")

    assert report["schema_version"] == "workflow-continuation-acceptance-v1"
    assert report["database_dialect"] == "sqlite"
    assert len(report["workflow_hash"]) == 64
    assert report["run_key"]
    assert report["node_ids"] == {
        "ordinary": ["read-initial", "write-first", "write-second"],
        "waiting": ["read-initial", "write-vendor", "read-vendor-result"],
        "first_seen": ["first-seen"],
    }
    assert len(report["outbox_ids"]) == 3
    assert report["wait_id"] > 0
    assert report["callback_event_id"] > 0
    assert report["status_timeline"] == {
        "continue": ["queued", "running", "queued", "running", "queued", "running", "succeeded"],
        "await_follow_up": ["queued", "running", "queued", "running", "waiting_external", "queued", "running", "succeeded"],
        "timeout": ["waiting_external", "blocked"],
        "cancel": ["waiting_external", "canceled"],
        "stock_first_seen": ["queued", "running", "succeeded"],
    }
    assert report["duplicate_counts"] == {
        "ordinary_outbox": 0,
        "waiting_outbox": 0,
        "callback_intake": 0,
        "callback_resume_dispatch": 0,
        "first_seen_wait": 0,
    }
    assert report["scenarios"] == {
        "continue": True,
        "await_follow_up": True,
        "timeout": True,
        "cancel": True,
        "duplicate_callback": True,
        "stock_first_seen": True,
    }
    assert report["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert report["secret_scan"] == {
        "passed": True,
        "forbidden_matches": [],
    }

    rendered = json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False).lower()
    for forbidden in (
        "api_key",
        "authorization",
        "bearer ",
        "password",
        "cookie",
        "secret-value",
    ):
        assert forbidden not in rendered


def test_workflow_continuation_acceptance_matches_mysql_84(
    mysql_engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)

    report = _module().run_acceptance(mysql_engine)

    assert report["database_dialect"] == "mysql"
    assert all(report["scenarios"].values())
    assert set(report["duplicate_counts"].values()) == {0}
    assert report["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert report["secret_scan"]["passed"] is True

def _runtime_snapshot() -> dict:
    prompt_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "style": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "风格",
                "description": "核对模型识别的视觉风格",
            }
        },
    }
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "input",
            "kind": "upstream_connector",
            "display_name": "读取待评测内容",
            "config": {},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "提示词 C",
            "config": {
                "prompt_version_id": 31,
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "version_id": 31,
                    "version": "C-1",
                    "content_hash": "a" * 64,
                },
            },
            "output_schema": prompt_schema,
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_d",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "提示词 D",
            "config": {
                "prompt_version_id": 32,
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "version_id": 32,
                    "version": "D-1",

                },
            },
            "output_schema": prompt_schema,
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "output",
            "kind": "downstream_projection",
            "display_name": "写入下游",
            "config": {},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        },
    ]
    return {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 1,
            "workflow_version_id": 2,
            "version": "3",
            "canonical_hash": "c" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "model_3d_su",
                "nodes": nodes,
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "input", "to": "prompt_c"},
                    {"from": "input", "to": "prompt_d"},
                    {"from": "prompt_c", "to": "output"},
                    {"from": "prompt_d", "to": "output"},
                ],
            },
        }
    }


def test_workflow_correction_contract_marks_unselected_route_read_only() -> None:
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view
    from app.workflow_correction import build_workflow_correction_contract

    node_results = {
        "input": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {}},
        "prompt_c": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {"style": "现代"},
        },
        "prompt_d": {
            "status": "skipped",
            "skipped_reason": "route_not_selected",
        },
        "output": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {}},
    }
    contract = build_workflow_correction_contract(
        SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=canonical_json(_runtime_snapshot())),
        node_results,
    )

    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in contract["nodes"]}
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_c.style"]["metadata"]["editable"] is True
    skipped = by_key["workflow.prompt_d.style"]
    assert skipped["metadata"]["editable"] is False
    assert skipped["metadata"]["runtime_status"] == "skipped"
    assert skipped["metadata"]["read_only_reason"] == "route_not_selected"
    assert by_key["workflow.input"]["metadata"]["editable"] is False
    assert by_key["workflow.output"]["metadata"]["editable"] is False

    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        review_revision=0,
        correction_history_json="[]",
        workflow_node_outputs_json=canonical_json(node_results),
        precheck_json="{}",
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json="{}",
        score=None,
        level=None,
        reviews=[],
    )
    baseline_run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=9,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        correction_contract_json=canonical_json(contract),
        correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
        execution_snapshot_json="{}",
    )
    item = SimpleNamespace(
        id=10,
        run_id=9,
        evaluation_id=11,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {"workflow_node_outputs": node_results}
        ),
    )
    view = build_correction_view(None, run=baseline_run, item=item)
    rendered = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}
    assert rendered["workflow.prompt_d.style"]["read_only"] is True
    assert (
        rendered["workflow.prompt_d.style"]["read_only_reason"]
        == "route_not_selected"
    )

def test_workflow_correction_contract_expands_one_prompt_visual_schema() -> None:
    """Catches the correction view falling back to one opaque JSON textarea."""

    from app.workflow_correction import build_workflow_correction_contract

    snapshot = _runtime_snapshot()
    manifest = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
    prompt = next(
        node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
    )
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["node_instance_id"] != "prompt_d"
    ]
    manifest["edges"] = [
        {"from": "input", "to": "prompt_c"},
        {"from": "prompt_c", "to": "output"},
    ]
    prompt["output_schema"] = {"type": "object"}
    prompt["config"]["output_contract"] = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "unified_aesthetic",
        "description": "一次提示词",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "composition": {"type": "string", "title": "构图"},
                "color": {"type": "string", "title": "色彩"},
                "evidence": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "title": "证据",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                },
            },
            "required": ["composition", "color", "evidence"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    node_results = {
        "input": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {}},
        "prompt_c": {
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": {
                "composition": "主体居中",
                "color": "冷暖平衡",
                "evidence": ["主体清晰"],
            },
        },
        "output": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {}},
    }

    contract = build_workflow_correction_contract(
        SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=canonical_json(snapshot)),
        node_results,
    )

    assert {
        node["node_key"]
        for node in contract["nodes"]
        if node["node_key"].startswith("workflow.prompt_c.")
    } == {
        "workflow.prompt_c.composition",
        "workflow.prompt_c.color",
        "workflow.prompt_c.evidence",
    }

        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {"workflow_node_outputs": node_results}
        ),
    )

    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)

    prompt_field = next(
        node
        for node in view["nodes"]
        if node["node_key"] == "workflow.prompt_c.style"
    )
    evidence = prompt_field["execution_evidence"]["model_invocation"]
    assert prompt_field["model_value"] == "现代"
    assert evidence["request_correlation_id"] == "request-safe-1"
    assert evidence["input_tokens"] == 321
    assert evidence["raw_payload"]["choices"]
    assert "authorization" not in evidence["raw_payload"]


def _create_knowledge_graph_native_run(db: Session) -> tuple[int, int]:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    asset = Asset(
        original_name="kg-correction.skp",
        stored_name="kg-correction.skp",
        mime_type="application/octet-stream",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="7" * 64,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        name="知识图谱原生纠偏测试集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="6" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level=None,
            expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                {"ll_id": "ll-correction-1", "is_single": 0}
            ),
            truth_contract_key="kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1",
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
            ),
        )
    )
    workflow = _mechanism(db, category_key="model_3d_su")
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(
        "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
    )
    assert contract is not None
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    prompt["config"]["output_contract_key"] = contract.contract_key
    prompt["config"]["output_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
    prompt["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
    workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
    db.flush()
    workflow.canonical_hash = hash_manifest(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow))
    db.flush()

    native_run = create_native_baseline_run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline,
        workflow_version=workflow,
        actor="ops",
        idempotency_key="kg-native-correction-1",
    )
    return native_run.id, native_run.items[0].production_run_id


def _create_level_native_run(db: Session) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    asset = Asset(
        original_name="level-correction.jpg",
        stored_name="level-correction.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="8" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="space_image",
        name="等级纠偏下一轮真值测试集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L2",
        fingerprint="9" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    source_item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_level="L2",
        expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "asset_id": asset.id,
                "sha256": asset.sha256,
                "workflow_fixture": {
                    "schema_version": "native-prompt-fixtures-v1",
                    "prompt_outputs": {"prompt_c": {"level": "L2"}},
                },
            }
        ),
    )
    db.add(source_item)
    workflow = _mechanism(db, category_key="space_image")
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c")
    prompt["output_schema"] = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]}
        },
        "required": ["level"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }
    workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
    db.flush()
    workflow.canonical_hash = hash_manifest(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow))
    db.flush()
    run = create_native_baseline_run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline,
        workflow_version=workflow,
        actor="ops",
        idempotency_key="level-native-correction-v0",
    )
    return run.id, run.items[0].production_run_id, workflow.id


def test_native_workflow_correction_replays_only_active_downstream(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
    )
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-correction.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            baseline_run_id, production_run_id = _create_knowledge_graph_native_run(
                db
            )
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        assert process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-worker") is False

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence)
            ).all()
            immutable_attempt_hashes = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt.output_hash for attempt in attempts
            }
            evaluation = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationResult).where(
                    EvaluationResult.production_run_id == production_run_id
                )
            )
            assert evaluation is not None
            original_outputs = json.loads(evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json)
            original_result_hash = hash_manifest(
                json.loads(evaluation.normalized_result_json)
            )

            replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_run_id,
                node_instance_id="prompt_c",
                field_path="style",
                value="现代",
                reason="人工确认该模型为现代风格",
                actor="运营甲",
            )
            db.flush()

            assert replay.affected_nodes == (
                "prompt_c",
                "redline_1",
                "downstream_1",
            )
            assert replay.normalized_result["style"] == "现代"
            assert replay.result_hash != original_result_hash
            corrected_outputs = json.loads(evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json)
            assert corrected_outputs["upstream_1"] == original_outputs["upstream_1"]
            assert (
                corrected_outputs["downstream_1"]["input_fingerprint"]
                != original_outputs["downstream_1"]["input_fingerprint"]
            )
            assert json.loads(evaluation.normalized_result_json)["style"] == "现代"
            item = db.scalar(
                select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
                    BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == baseline_run_id
                )
            )
            assert item is not None
            item_snapshot = json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json)
            assert item_snapshot["normalized_result"]["style"] == "现代"
            assert json.loads(item.run.metrics_json)["valid_predictions"] == 1
            history = json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json)
            assert history[-1]["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            assert history[-1]["downstream_recomputed"] is True
            assert history[-1]["affected_nodes"] == [
                "prompt_c",
                "redline_1",
                "downstream_1",
            ]

            rule_replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_run_id,
                node_instance_id="redline_1",
                field_path="hit",
                value=True,
                reason="人工确认命中准入红线",
                actor="运营甲",
            )
            assert rule_replay.affected_nodes == (
                "redline_1",
                "downstream_1",
            )
            corrected_rule_outputs = json.loads(
                evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json
            )
            assert corrected_rule_outputs["redline_1"]["output"]["hit"] is True

            unchanged_attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_run_id
                )
            ).all()
            assert {
                attempt.step_key: attempt.output_hash
                for attempt in unchanged_attempts
            } == immutable_attempt_hashes

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as forbidden:
                append_node_correction_and_replay(
                    db,
                    run_id=production_run_id,
                    node_instance_id="prompt_c",
                    field_path="ll_id",
                    value="ll-other",
                    reason="不允许改写路由身份",
                    actor="运营甲",
                )
            assert forbidden.value.code == "route_identity_correction_forbidden"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_confirmed_kg_field_appends_truth_without_adopting_unreviewed_model_fields(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from app.workflow_correction import append_node_correction_and_replay
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-confirmed-truth.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            baseline_run_id, production_run_id = _create_knowledge_graph_native_run(
                db
            )
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        while process_runtime_step_once("kg-confirmed-truth-worker"):
            pass

        with sessions() as db:
            replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_run_id,
                node_instance_id="prompt_c",
                field_path="style",
                value="现代简约",
                reason="运营逐字段确认风格",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key="kg-confirmed-style-1",
            )
            regression_item = db.scalar(
                select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
                    BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == baseline_run_id
                )
            )
            assert regression_item is not None
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(
                db,
