                regression_item.baseline_set_item,
            )

            assert effective.revision == 1
            assert effective.expected_result == {
                "ll_id": "ll-correction-1",
                "is_single": 0,
                "style": "现代简约",
            }
            assert replay.truth_revision == 1
            assert replay.truth_hash == effective.canonical_hash
            assert replay.effective_next_run is True
            assert set(effective.expected_result) == {"ll_id", "is_single", "style"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_level_correction_becomes_next_run_truth(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_correction import append_node_correction_and_replay
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'level-next-run-truth.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            baseline_run_id, production_run_id, workflow_id = (
                _create_level_native_run(db)
            )
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        while process_runtime_step_once("level-next-run-worker"):
            pass

        with sessions() as db:
            source_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, baseline_run_id)
            assert source_run is not None and source_run.status == "completed"
            source_item = source_run.items[0]
            replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_run_id,
                node_instance_id="prompt_c",
                field_path="level",
                value="L1",
                reason="运营确认最终等级应为 L1",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key="level-confirmed-l1",
            )
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(
                db,
                source_item.baseline_set_item,
            )
            assert effective.revision == 1
            assert effective.expected_level == "L1"
            assert effective.expected_result == {"level": "L1"}
            assert replay.truth_revision == 1
            assert replay.effective_next_run is True
            assert source_item.expected_level == "L2"

            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_id)
            assert workflow is not None
            later = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=source_run.baseline_set,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="level-native-correction-v1",
            )
            assert later.items[0].expected_level == "L1"
            assert json.loads(later.items[0].expected_result_json or "{}") == {
                "level": "L1"
            }
            frozen = json.loads(later.execution_snapshot_json)["truth_snapshot"][0]
            assert frozen["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert frozen["expected_level"] == "L1"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_confirmed_route_change_switches_contract_and_next_run_branch(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
        revise_knowledge_graph_route,
    )
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-route-revision.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-route-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(
                "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
            )
            assert contract is not None
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="kg-route-revision.jpg",
                stored_name="kg-route-revision.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=100,
                sha256="a" * 64,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            db.add(asset)
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                name="知识图谱路线修订测试集",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="b" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline)
            db.flush()
            original_result = {
                field: (
                    "ll-route-1"
                    if field == "ll_id"
                    else 0
                    if field == "is_single"
                    else ""
                )
                for field in contract.field_order
            }
            source_item = BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result_json=canonical_json(original_result),
                truth_contract_key=contract.contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "sha256": asset.sha256,
                        "workflow_input": {
                            "source": "domestic",
                            "is_single": 0,
                            "ll_id": "ll-route-1",
                            "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
                        },
                    }
                ),
            )
            db.add(source_item)
            db.flush()
            original_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="kg-route-original",
            )
            original_run_id = original_run.id
            original_production_id = original_run.items[0].production_run_id
            source_item_id = source_item.id
            workflow_id = workflow.id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        while process_runtime_step_once("kg-route-original-worker"):
            pass

        with sessions() as db:
            original_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, original_run_id)
            assert original_run is not None and original_run.status == "completed"
            original_frozen = json.loads(original_run.execution_snapshot_json)[
                "truth_snapshot"
            ][0]
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as not_confirmed:
                revise_knowledge_graph_route(
                    db,
                    baseline_set_item_id=source_item_id,
                    source="overseas",
                    is_single=2,
                    identity_value="res-route-2",
                    reason="改为海外单体路线",
                    actor="运营甲",
                    confirmed=False,
                    revision_key="kg-route-overseas-single-1",
                    source_baseline_run_id=original_run_id,
                    source_production_run_id=original_production_id,
                )
            assert not_confirmed.value.code == "kg_route_confirmation_required"

            revision = revise_knowledge_graph_route(
                db,
                baseline_set_item_id=source_item_id,
                source="overseas",
                is_single=2,
                identity_value="res-route-2",
                reason="改为海外单体路线",
                actor="运营甲",
                confirmed=True,
                revision_key="kg-route-overseas-single-1",
                source_baseline_run_id=original_run_id,
                source_production_run_id=original_production_id,
            )
            source_item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, source_item_id)
            assert source_item is not None
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, source_item)
            assert revision.revision == 1
            assert effective.truth_contract_key == (
                "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1"
            )
            assert effective.workflow_input == {
                "fixture_key": "kg.overseas.single.v1",
                "is_single": 2,
                "res_id": "res-route-2",
                "source": "overseas",
            }
            assert effective.expected_result["res_id"] == "res-route-2"
            assert effective.expected_result["is_single"] == 2
            assert "ll_id" not in effective.expected_result
            assert original_frozen["truth_contract_key"] == (
                "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
            )
            assert original_frozen["workflow_input"]["ll_id"] == "ll-route-1"

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as stale_route:
                append_node_correction_and_replay(
                    db,
                    run_id=original_production_id,
                    node_instance_id="prompt_domestic_whole",
                    field_path="style",
                    value="现代简约",
                    reason="不能把旧国内分支的判断写入新海外路线",
                    actor="运营甲",
                    correction_key="kg-route-stale-field-1",
                    evidence=[{"text": "当前运行仍是国内整体分支"}],
                )
            assert stale_route.value.code == (
                "workflow_correction_truth_route_stale"
            )

            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_id)
            baseline = source_item.baseline_set
            assert workflow is not None and baseline is not None
            later = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="kg-route-revised",
            )
            later_run_id = later.id
            later_production_id = later.items[0].production_run_id
            later_frozen = json.loads(later.execution_snapshot_json)[
                "truth_snapshot"
            ][0]
            assert later_frozen["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert later_frozen["truth_contract_key"] == (
                "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1"
            )
            db.commit()

        while process_runtime_step_once("kg-route-revised-worker"):
            pass

        with sessions() as db:
            later = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, later_run_id)
            assert later is not None and later.status == "completed"
            attempts = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == later_production_id
                    )
                ).all()
            }
            assert attempts["prompt_overseas_single"].status == "succeeded"
            assert attempts["project_overseas_single"].status == "succeeded"
            assert attempts["prompt_domestic_whole"].status == "skipped"
            assert attempts["prompt_domestic_single"].status == "skipped"
            assert attempts["prompt_overseas_whole"].status == "skipped"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_kg_route_revision_api_requires_confirmation_and_returns_revision() -> None:
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        run_id, production_run_id = _create_knowledge_graph_native_run(db)
        run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
        assert run is not None
        item = run.items[0]
        db.commit()
        user = SimpleNamespace(username="运营甲", is_admin=True, role="admin")
        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        client = TestClient(app)
        path = (
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{run.id}/items/{item.id}"
            "/route-revision"
        )
        payload = {
            "source": "overseas",
            "is_single": 2,
            "identity_value": "res-api-route-1",
            "reason": "运营确认素材属于海外单体",
            "confirmed": False,
            "idempotency_key": "kg-api-route-overseas-single-1",
        }

        rejected = client.post(path, json=payload)
        assert rejected.status_code == 422
        assert rejected.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "kg_route_confirmation_required"
        )

        payload["confirmed"] = True
        saved = client.post(path, json=payload)
        assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
        assert saved.json()["truth_revision"] == 1
        assert len(saved.json()["truth_hash"]) == 64
        assert saved.json()["truth_contract_key"] == (
            "kg.model_3d_su.overseas.single.v1"
        )
        assert saved.json()["workflow_input"] == {
            "fixture_key": "kg.overseas.single.v1",
            "is_single": 2,
            "res_id": "res-api-route-1",
            "source": "overseas",
        }
        assert saved.json()["effective_next_run"] is True
        assert saved.json()["source_production_run_id"] == production_run_id

        replay = client.post(path, json=payload)
        assert replay.status_code == 200
        assert replay.json()["truth_revision"] == 1
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_correction_view_submits_and_idempotently_replays(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, submit_correction_nodes
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-correction-view.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            baseline_run_id, _production_run_id = _create_knowledge_graph_native_run(
                db
            )
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("workflow-correction-view-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 4
        assert processed == 4

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun

            baseline_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, baseline_run_id)
            assert baseline_run is not None
            item = baseline_run.items[0]
            view = build_correction_view(
                db,
                run=baseline_run,
                item=item,
            )
            assert view["read_only"] is False
            by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}
            assert by_key["workflow.prompt_c.style"]["current_value"] == ""
            assert by_key["workflow.upstream_1"]["read_only"] is True

            corrected = submit_correction_nodes(
                db,
                run=baseline_run,
                item=item,
                contract_hash=view["contract"]["contract_hash"],
                nodes=[
                    {
                        "node_key": "workflow.prompt_c.style",
                        "human_value": "现代",
                        "reason": "人工确认视觉风格为现代",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "主体线条与材质呈现代风格"}],
                    }
                ],
                review_revision=0,
                idempotency_key="native-view-correction-1",
                actor="运营乙",
            )
            corrected_by_key = {
                node["node_key"]: node for node in corrected["nodes"]
            }
            prompt = corrected_by_key["workflow.prompt_c.style"]
            assert prompt["current_value"] == "现代"
            assert prompt["human_value"] == "现代"
            history = json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)
            assert len(history) == 1
            assert history[0]["affected_nodes"] == [
                "prompt_c",
                "redline_1",
                "downstream_1",
            ]
            assert history[0]["contract_hash"] == view["contract"][
                "contract_hash"
            ]
            assert corrected["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert len(corrected["truth_hash"]) == 64
            assert corrected["effective_next_run"] is True
            assert corrected["effective_truth"]["expected_result"] == {
                "ll_id": "ll-correction-1",
                "is_single": 0,
                "style": "现代",
            }

            replayed = submit_correction_nodes(
                db,
                run=baseline_run,
                item=item,
                contract_hash=view["contract"]["contract_hash"],
                nodes=[
                    {
                        "node_key": "workflow.prompt_c.style",
                        "human_value": "现代",
                        "reason": "人工确认视觉风格为现代",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "主体线条与材质呈现代风格"}],
                    }
                ],
                review_revision=0,
                idempotency_key="native-view-correction-1",
                actor="运营乙",
            )
            assert replayed["idempotent_replay"] is True
            assert replayed["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert replayed["effective_next_run"] is True
            assert len(json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("operator_edit,closed_enum", [(True, False), (False, False), (False, True)])
def test_four_route_knowledge_graph_correction_recommends_and_regresses_once(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path, operator_edit, closed_enum
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        get_component_working_copy,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.models import (
        BaselineCorrectionRun,
        BaselineRegressionRun,
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
        WorkflowNodeAsset,
    )
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-native-loop.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-loop-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            fixture = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
                "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
                "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                expected_asset_id="ll-loop-1",
            )
            if closed_enum:
                from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
                manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
                for node in manifest["nodes"]:
                    # Custom workflow enum, not a claim to match the catalogue's
                    # open-string output schema snapshot.
                    (node.get("config") or {}).pop("output_contract_key", None)
                    (node.get("config") or {}).pop("field_contract_key", None)
                    if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
                        from copy import deepcopy
                        node["config"]["output_contract"] = {"mode": "json_schema", "name": "custom_tags", "strict": True,
                            "schema": deepcopy(node["output_schema"])}
                    for schema in (node.get("output_schema"), (node.get("config") or {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("schema")):
                        if isinstance(schema, dict) and "style" in schema.get("properties", {}):
                            schema["properties"]["style"]["enum"] = list(dict.fromkeys([fixture["style"], "现代极简"]))
                workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(manifest["nodes"])
                workflow.canonical_hash = hash_manifest(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow))
                db.flush()
            expected = {**fixture, "style": "现代极简"}
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="kg-loop.skp",
                stored_name="kg-loop.skp",
                mime_type="application/octet-stream",
                size_bytes=120,
                sha256="4" * 64,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            db.add(asset)
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                name="知识图谱纠偏闭环",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="3" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                BaselineSetItem(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result_json=canonical_json(expected),
                    truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                    asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                        {
                            "asset_id": asset.id,
                            "sha256": asset.sha256,
                            "workflow_input": {
                                "source": "domestic",
                                "is_single": 0,
                                "ll_id": "ll-loop-1",
                                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
                            },
                        }
                    ),
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            source_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="kg-native-loop-source",
            )
            source_run_id = source_run.id
            source_item_id = source_run.items[0].id
            production_run_id = source_run.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("kg-native-loop-source-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 6
        assert processed == 5

        with sessions() as db:
            source_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, source_run_id)
            source_item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, source_item_id)
            assert source_run is not None and source_item is not None
            assert source_run.status == "completed"
            view = build_correction_view(db, run=source_run, item=source_item)
            fields = {node["node_key"]: node for node in view["nodes"]}
            assert fields[
                "workflow.prompt_domestic_whole.style"
            ]["editable"] is True
            assert fields[
                "workflow.prompt_domestic_single.style"
            ]["read_only"] is True
            assert fields[
                "workflow.prompt_domestic_single.style"
            ]["read_only_reason"] == "route_not_selected"

            replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_run_id,
                node_instance_id="prompt_domestic_whole",
                field_path="style",
                value="现代极简",
                reason="人工确认应使用更精确的受控风格词",
                actor="运营甲",
            )
            assert replay.affected_nodes == (
                "prompt_domestic_whole",
                "project_domestic_whole",
                "route_join",
            )
            assert replay.normalized_result["style"] == "现代极简"
            assert json.loads(source_item.result_snapshot_json)[
                "normalized_result"
            ]["style"] == "现代极简"

            # A later correction supersedes the same field; originals remain audit facts.
            from app.correction_view import submit_correction_nodes
            current_view = build_correction_view(db, run=source_run, item=source_item)
            submit_correction_nodes(db, run=source_run, item=source_item,
                contract_hash=source_run.correction_contract_hash,
                nodes=[{'node_key':'workflow.prompt_domestic_whole.style','human_value':'复古工业',
                        'reason':'核对材质与构造后修正风格','evidence':[{'text':'外露钢构与砖墙'}]}],
                review_revision=source_item.evaluation.review_revision,
                idempotency_key='final-style',actor='运营甲')
            if operator_edit:
                prompt_asset = db.scalar(
                    select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                        WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "knowledge_graph",
                        WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key
                        == "kg_domestic_whole_prompt",
                    )
                )
                assert prompt_asset is not None
                working = get_component_working_copy(db, prompt_asset.id)
                content = json.loads(working.content_json)
                content["system_prompt"] += (
                    "\n人工纠偏候选：现代极简与现代简约必须按可见证据严格区分。"
                )
                save_component_working_copy(
                    db,
                    asset_id=prompt_asset.id,
                    expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
                    content=content,
                    updated_by="运营甲",
                )
            correction = BaselineCorrectionRun(
                idempotency_key="kg-native-loop-correction",
                baseline_run_id=source_run.id,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                selected_item_ids_json=canonical_json([source_item.id]),
                input_snapshot_json="{}",
                created_by="运营甲",
            )
            db.add(correction)
            db.flush()

            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db, source_run.id, [source_item.id]
            )
            assert analysis.blockers == ()
            assert len(analysis.groups[0].samples) == 1
            assert analysis.groups[0].samples[0]['new_value'] == '复古工业'
            assert analysis.groups[0].samples[0]['old_value'] == fixture['style']
            assert [group.node_instance_id for group in analysis.groups] == [
                "prompt_domestic_whole"
            ]
            recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)
            assert len(recommendations) == 1
            assert recommendations[0].node_instance_id == (
                "prompt_domestic_whole"
            )
            assert recommendations[0].candidate_version_id != (
                recommendations[0].source_version_id
            )
            if not operator_edit:
                from app.models import PromptVersion
                generated_prompt=db.get(PromptVersion,recommendations[0].candidate_version_id)
                assert '复古工业' in generated_prompt.system_prompt
                assert '外露钢构与砖墙' in generated_prompt.system_prompt
            candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                workflow.id,
                recommendations,
                correction.id,
                "运营甲",
            )
            from app.native_baseline_regression import (
                prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot,
            )
            from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

            candidate_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(candidate)
            source_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
            assert (prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(candidate_manifest)
                    == prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(source_manifest)) is (not closed_enum)
            if closed_enum:
                node_id = recommendations[0].node_instance_id
                candidate_node = next(n for n in candidate_manifest["nodes"] if n["node_instance_id"] == node_id)
                source_node = next(n for n in source_manifest["nodes"] if n["node_instance_id"] == node_id)
                for candidate_schema, source_schema in (
                    (candidate_node["output_schema"], source_node["output_schema"]),
                    (candidate_node["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"], source_node["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"]),
                ):
                    assert "复古工业" in candidate_schema["properties"]["style"]["enum"]
                    assert "复古工业" not in source_schema["properties"]["style"]["enum"]
                changes = json.loads(correction.orchestration_json)["native_candidate"]["enum_extensions"]
                assert changes[0]["field_path"] == "style"
                assert len(changes[0]["changes"]) == 2
                assert all(c["added_values"] == ["复古工业"] for c in changes[0]["changes"])
            assert compose_candidate_workflow(db, workflow.id, recommendations, correction.id, "运营甲").id == candidate.id
            frozen_candidate = json.loads(correction.orchestration_json)[
                "native_candidate"
            ]
            assert frozen_candidate["prompt_contract_identities"] == list(
                prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(
                    persisted_workflow_manifest(candidate)
                )
            )
            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, correction, "运营甲"
            )
            candidate_run_id = candidate_run.id
            assert json.loads(candidate_run.items[0].expected_result_json)['style'] == '复古工业'
            assert start_single_candidate_regression(db, correction, '运营甲').id == candidate_run.id
            assert candidate_run.workflow_version_id == candidate.id
            db.commit()

        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("kg-native-loop-candidate-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 6
        assert processed == 5

        with sessions() as db:
            correction = db.scalar(
                select(BaselineCorrectionRun).where(
                    BaselineCorrectionRun.idempotency_key
                    == "kg-native-loop-correction"
                )
            )
            candidate_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, candidate_run_id)
            assert correction is not None and candidate_run is not None
            assert candidate_run.status == "completed"
            refresh_correction_run(db, correction)
            assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
            assert correction.stage == "decision"
            assert correction.regression_run_id == candidate_run.id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key
                    == "model_3d_su"
                )
            ) is None
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id
                    == candidate_run.items[0].production_run_id
                )
            ).all()
            statuses = {item.step_key: item.status for item in attempts}
            assert statuses["prompt_domestic_whole"] == "succeeded"
            assert statuses["prompt_domestic_single"] == "skipped"
            assert statuses["prompt_overseas_whole"] == "skipped"
            assert statuses["prompt_overseas_single"] == "skipped"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_correction_blob_snapshot.py =====
"""存量回归运行的快照被压缩进 workflow_snapshot_blobs 后，纠偏读取仍要能拿到冻结定义。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.models import WorkflowSnapshotBlob
from app.workflow_correction import WorkflowCorrectionError, _snapshot_from_run
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import canonical_snapshot_bytes


def _snapshot() -> dict:
    return {
        "workflow": {"manifest": {"schema_version": "workflow-v3", "nodes": [], "edges": []}},
        "runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"asset_id": 7205}},
    }


def _blob(snapshot: dict, *, blob_id: int = 80440) -> WorkflowSnapshotBlob:
    canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot)
    compressed = gzip.compress(canonical, mtime=0)
    blob = WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
        snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest(),
        canonical_size_bytes=len(canonical), compressed_size_bytes=len(compressed),
        compression="gzip", payload_bytes=compressed,
    )
    blob.id = blob_id
    return blob


def test_snapshot_from_compacted_run_reads_blob() -> None:
    snapshot = _snapshot()
    blob = _blob(snapshot)
    run = SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=None, snapshot_blob_id=blob.id, snapshot_blob=blob,
                          snapshot_hash=blob.snapshot_hash)
    assert _snapshot_from_run(run)["workflow"]["manifest"]["schema_version"] == "workflow-v3"


def test_snapshot_from_run_prefers_inline_text() -> None:
    snapshot = _snapshot()
    run = SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=canonical_snapshot_bytes(snapshot).decode(),
                          snapshot_blob_id=None, snapshot_blob=None, snapshot_hash="x")
    assert _snapshot_from_run(run)["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["asset_id"] == 7205


def test_snapshot_from_run_reports_missing_blob_as_correction_error() -> None:
    run = SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=None, snapshot_blob_id=1, snapshot_blob=None, snapshot_hash="x")
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as excinfo:
        _snapshot_from_run(run)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "workflow_correction_snapshot_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_external_wait.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import IntakeRequest, ingest_upstream_event
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowExternalWait,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.workflow_connector_executor import create_incremental_run_from_intake
from app.workflow_external_wait import (
    ExternalResumeRequest,
    ExternalWaitError,
    expire_external_waits,
    resume_external_wait,
)
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    complete_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
    terminalize_runtime_run,
)


def _hash(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    suffix: str,
    role: str,
    direction: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    return create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key=f"external-{role}-{suffix}",
        direction=direction,
        category_key="space_image",
        logical_locator=f"logical.external.{role}.{suffix}",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint=_hash({"role": role, "suffix": suffix}),
        field_mapping={"asset_id": "asset_id"},
        cursor_contract={
            "kind": "text",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": "accept",
        },
        idempotency_contract={"field": "source_event_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="test",
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )


def _node(
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    config: dict,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": node_id,
        "config": config,
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": [],
    }


def _waiting_writer(
    db: Session,
    *,
    suffix: str,
    timeout_policy: str = "manual_review",
    completed_at: datetime | None = None,
    run_mode: str = "incremental",
) -> tuple[
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
]:
    initial = _contract(
        db,
        suffix=suffix,
        role="initial",
        direction="upstream",
    )
    outbound = _contract(
        db,
        suffix=suffix,
        role="request",
        direction="downstream",
    )
    response = _contract(
        db,
        suffix=suffix,
        role="response",
        direction="upstream",
    )
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "read-initial",
            "upstream_connector",
            {
                "connector_key": initial.contract_key,
                "connector_contract_version_id": initial.id,
                "input_mode": "initial_intake",
            },
        ),
        _node(
            "write-vendor",
            "downstream_projection",
            {
                "connector_key": outbound.contract_key,
                "connector_contract_version_id": outbound.id,
                "completion_mode": "await_follow_up",
                "continuation_key": f"vendor-stage-{suffix}",
                "resume_node_instance_id": "read-vendor-result",
                "wait_timeout_seconds": 60,
                "timeout_policy": timeout_policy,
            },
        ),
        _node(
            "read-vendor-result",
            "upstream_connector",
            {
                "connector_key": response.contract_key,
                "connector_contract_version_id": response.id,
                "input_mode": "continuation_event",
                "continuation_key": f"vendor-stage-{suffix}",
            },
        ),
    ]
    edges = [
        {"from": "read-initial", "to": "write-vendor"},
        {"from": "write-vendor", "to": "read-vendor-result"},
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "incremental",
        "run_modes": ["incremental", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
        "queue_class": "production_batch",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {},
        "layout": {},
    }
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"runtime.external-wait.{suffix}",
        name="External wait test",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(edges),
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental", "stock_regression"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=_hash(manifest),
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    initial_payload = {"asset_id": 42, "source": "imported"}
    if run_mode == "incremental":
        initial_decision = ingest_upstream_event(
            db,
            initial,
            IntakeRequest(
                source_event_id=f"initial-{suffix}",
                source_cursor="1",
                source_occurred_at=None,
                category_key="space_image",
                mechanism_key=definition.workflow_key,
                payload=initial_payload,
                actor="test",
            ),
        )
        run = create_incremental_run_from_intake(
            db,
            event_id=initial_decision.event.id,
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            actor="test",
        )
    else:
        run = create_production_run(
            db,
            ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                idempotency_key=f"stock-external-wait-{suffix}",
                category_key="space_image",
                queue_class="production_batch",
                runtime_context={"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
                input_manifest=initial_payload,
                owner="test",
            ),
            actor="test",
        )
    db.commit()

    root_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, f"root-{suffix}", global_limit=1)
    assert root_id is not None
    db.commit()
    root = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, root_id)
    assert root is not None and root.lease_token
    complete_runtime_step(
        db,
        root.id,
        root.lease_token,
        {"asset_id": 42, "source": "imported"},
        now=(completed_at - timedelta(seconds=1)) if completed_at else None,
    )
    db.commit()

    writer_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, f"writer-{suffix}", global_limit=1)
    assert writer_id is not None
    db.commit()
    writer = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, writer_id)
    assert writer is not None and writer.lease_token
    return run, writer, response


def _waiting_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    suffix: str,
    timeout_policy: str = "manual_review",
    completed_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> tuple[
    ProductionRun,
    WorkflowExternalWait,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
]:
    run, writer, response = _waiting_writer(
        db,
        suffix=suffix,
        timeout_policy=timeout_policy,
        completed_at=completed_at,
    )
    complete_runtime_step(
        db,
        writer.id,
        writer.lease_token,
        {"external_asset_id": "vendor-42"},
        now=completed_at,
    )
    db.commit()
    wait = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(
            WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run.id
        )
    )
    assert wait is not None
    return run, wait, response


def _callback_event(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
    correlation_id: str,
    payload: dict,
    suffix: str,
) -> UpstreamIntakeEvent:
    decision = ingest_upstream_event(
        db,
        contract,
        IntakeRequest(
            source_event_id=f"{correlation_id}-{suffix}",
            source_cursor="",
            source_occurred_at=None,
            category_key="space_image",
            mechanism_key="external-callback",
            payload=payload,
            actor="vendor-fixture",
        ),
    )
    return decision.event


def test_wait_creation_is_atomic_with_step_and_outbox(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_runtime as runtime
    from app.workflow_external_wait import create_external_wait

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-atomic.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, writer, _response = _waiting_writer(db, suffix="atomic")
            run_id = run.id
            writer_id = writer.id
            lease_token = writer.lease_token
            assert lease_token

            class InjectedWaitFailure(RuntimeError):
                pass

            def fail_after_wait(session, request):
                create_external_wait(session, request)
                raise InjectedWaitFailure("fail before transaction commit")

            monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, "create_external_wait", fail_after_wait)
            with pytest.raises(InjectedWaitFailure):
                complete_runtime_step(
                    db,
                    writer_id,
                    lease_token,
                    {"external_asset_id": "vendor-42"},
                )
            db.rollback()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            persisted_run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            persisted_writer = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, writer_id)
            assert persisted_run is not None and persisted_run.status == "running"
            assert persisted_writer is not None
            assert persisted_writer.status in {"leased", "running"}
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 0
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowExternalWait)
            ) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stock_regression_never_waits_for_external_callback(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-stock.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, writer, _response = _waiting_writer(
                db,
                suffix="stock",
                run_mode="stock_regression",
            )
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                writer.id,
                writer.lease_token,
                {"external_asset_id": "fixture-42"},
            )
            db.commit()

            assert run.status == "queued"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 0
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowExternalWait)
            ) == 0
            resume_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "stock-continuation",
                global_limit=1,
            )
            assert resume_id is not None
            db.commit()
            resume = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, resume_id)
            assert resume is not None
            assert resume.step_key == "read-vendor-result"
            assert resume.lease_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                resume.id,
                resume.lease_token,
                {"asset_id": 42, "source": "fixture"},
            )
            db.commit()
            assert run.status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_waiting_projection_creates_one_wait_and_resumes_from_callback_fact(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-resume.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, wait, response = _waiting_run(db, suffix="resume")
            run_id = run.id
            assert run.status == "waiting_external"
            assert run.lease_owner is None
            assert run.lease_token is None
            assert wait.state == "waiting"
            assert wait.expected_contract_version_id == response.id
            assert wait.outbox_event.node_instance_id == "write-vendor"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1
            writer = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "write-vendor",
                )
            )
            assert writer is not None and writer.lease_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                writer.id,
                writer.lease_token,
                {"external_asset_id": "vendor-42"},
            )
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowExternalWait)
            ) == 1
            resume_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "read-vendor-result",
                )
            )
            assert resume_attempt is not None
            assert resume_attempt.status == "pending"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                .where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == resume_attempt.id,
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
                )
            ) == 0

            callback_payload = {
                "asset_id": 42,
                "vendor_status": "processed",
            }
            callback = _callback_event(
                db,
                contract=response,
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                payload=callback_payload,
                suffix="accepted",
            )
            request = ExternalResumeRequest(
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                callback_intake_event_id=callback.id,
                callback_payload_hash=callback.payload_hash,
                actor="callback-api",
            )
            resumed = resume_external_wait(db, request)
            repeated = resume_external_wait(db, request)
            db.commit()

            assert resumed.id == repeated.id == run_id
            assert resumed.status == "queued"
            assert wait.state == "resumed"
            assert wait.callback_intake_event_id == callback.id
            assert callback.production_run_id == run_id
            db.refresh(resume_attempt)
            assert json.loads(resume_attempt.input_manifest_json) == callback_payload
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                .where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == resume_attempt.id,
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
                )
            ) == 1

            conflicting = _callback_event(
                db,
                contract=response,
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                payload={"asset_id": 42, "vendor_status": "different"},
                suffix="conflict",
            )
            with pytest.raises(
                ExternalWaitError,
                match="external_wait_callback_conflict",
            ):
                resume_external_wait(
                    db,
                    ExternalResumeRequest(
                        correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                        callback_intake_event_id=conflicting.id,
                        callback_payload_hash=conflicting.payload_hash,
                        actor="callback-api",
                    ),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_resume_rejects_wrong_contract_and_payload_hash(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-reject.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, wait, response = _waiting_run(db, suffix="reject")
            wrong_contract = _contract(
                db,
                suffix="reject-wrong",
                role="response",
                direction="upstream",
            )
            callback = _callback_event(
                db,
                contract=wrong_contract,
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                payload={"asset_id": 42},
                suffix="wrong-contract",
            )
            with pytest.raises(
                ExternalWaitError,
                match="external_wait_callback_contract_mismatch",
            ):
                resume_external_wait(
                    db,
                    ExternalResumeRequest(
                        correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                        callback_intake_event_id=callback.id,
                        callback_payload_hash=callback.payload_hash,
                        actor="callback-api",
                    ),
                )
            wrong_category = _callback_event(
                db,
                contract=response,
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                payload={"asset_id": 42, "case": "wrong-category"},
                suffix="wrong-category",
            )
            wrong_category.category_key = "material_image"
            db.flush()
            with pytest.raises(
                ExternalWaitError,
                match="external_wait_callback_category_mismatch",
            ):
                resume_external_wait(
                    db,
                    ExternalResumeRequest(
                        correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                        callback_intake_event_id=wrong_category.id,
                        callback_payload_hash=wrong_category.payload_hash,
                        actor="callback-api",
                    ),
                )
            valid = _callback_event(
                db,
                contract=response,
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                payload={"asset_id": 42},
                suffix="bad-hash",
            )
            with pytest.raises(
                ExternalWaitError,
                match="external_wait_callback_hash_mismatch",
            ):
                resume_external_wait(
                    db,
                    ExternalResumeRequest(
                        correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                        callback_intake_event_id=valid.id,
                        callback_payload_hash="f" * 64,
                        actor="callback-api",
                    ),
                )
            db.rollback()
            persisted_run = db.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
            persisted_wait = db.get(WorkflowExternalWait, wait.id)
            assert persisted_run is not None
            assert persisted_run.status == "waiting_external"
            assert persisted_wait is not None and persisted_wait.state == "waiting"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("timeout_policy", "expected_run_status", "expected_error"),
    [
        ("manual_review", "blocked", "external_wait_manual_review"),
        ("fail", "failed", "external_wait_timed_out"),
    ],
)
def test_external_wait_expiry_follows_frozen_timeout_policy(
    tmp_path,
    timeout_policy: str,
    expected_run_status: str,
    expected_error: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'external-expiry-{timeout_policy}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    completed_at = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, wait, _response = _waiting_run(
                db,
                suffix=f"expiry-{timeout_policy}",
                timeout_policy=timeout_policy,
                completed_at=completed_at,
            )
            summary = expire_external_waits(
                db,
                now=completed_at + timedelta(seconds=61),
            )
            db.commit()

            assert summary.expired_count == 1
            assert summary.manual_review_count == (
                1 if timeout_policy == "manual_review" else 0
            )
            assert summary.failed_count == (1 if timeout_policy == "fail" else 0)
            assert wait.state == "expired"
            assert wait.expired_at is not None
            assert run.status == expected_run_status
            assert run.error_code == expected_error
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_cancel_waiting_run_cancels_external_wait(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-cancel.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, wait, _response = _waiting_run(db, suffix="cancel")
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                run,
                status="canceled",
                error_code="RUN_CANCELED",
                error_message="operator canceled",
            )
            db.commit()
            assert run.status == "canceled"
            assert wait.state == "cancelled"
            assert wait.cancelled_at is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_identical_callbacks_resume_once(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        run, wait, response = _waiting_run(db, suffix="mysql-concurrent")
        callback = _callback_event(
            db,
            contract=response,
            correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
            payload={"asset_id": 42, "vendor_status": "processed"},
            suffix="shared",
        )
        db.commit()
        run_id = run.id
        workflow_version_id = run.workflow_version_id
        wait_id = wait.id
        callback_id = callback.id
        callback_hash = callback.payload_hash
        correlation_id = wait.correlation_id

    def resume_once(worker_number: int) -> int:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            resumed = resume_external_wait(
                db,
                ExternalResumeRequest(
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    callback_intake_event_id=callback_id,
                    callback_payload_hash=callback_hash,
                    actor=f"callback-worker-{worker_number}",
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            return resumed.id

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=16) as pool:
        resumed_ids = list(pool.map(resume_once, range(16)))

    assert resumed_ids == [run_id] * 16
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        persisted_wait = db.get(WorkflowExternalWait, wait_id)
        callback = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, callback_id)
        assert persisted_wait is not None and persisted_wait.state == "resumed"
        assert persisted_wait.callback_intake_event_id == callback_id
        assert callback is not None and callback.production_run_id == run_id
        resume_attempt = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "read-vendor-result",
            )
        )
        assert resume_attempt is not None
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == resume_attempt.id)
        ) == 1
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.mechanism_version_id == workflow_version_id)
        ) == 1


def test_callback_resume_backfills_sla_projection_before_requeue(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """复核 ls-review-r21 F1：迁移 131 前进入 waiting_external 的旧 run，回调重排 queued 前必须
    经 ensure_sla_projection 从冻结快照补投影，否则冻结 high 被列谓词当普通。"""
    import app.incremental_sla as sla
    seen = []
    real = sla.ensure_sla_projection
    monkeypatch.setattr(sla, "ensure_sla_projection", lambda run: seen.append((run.id, run.status)) or real(run))
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-sla.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, wait, response = _waiting_run(db, suffix="sla")
            callback = _callback_event(db, contract=response, correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                                       payload={"asset_id": 42, "vendor_status": "processed"}, suffix="sla-ok")
            resumed = resume_external_wait(db, ExternalResumeRequest(
                correlation_id=wait.correlation_id, callback_intake_event_id=callback.id,
                callback_payload_hash=callback.payload_hash, actor="callback-api"))
            db.commit()
            assert resumed.status == "queued"
            # 调用发生在置 queued 之前，run 仍处于 waiting_external
            assert seen == [(run.id, "waiting_external")]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_external_wait_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app import models
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


def _legacy_schema(connection) -> None:
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        """
        CREATE TABLE production_runs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'planned'
                CHECK(status IN ('planned','queued','running','paused','succeeded',
                    'failed','retryable','blocked','canceled')),
            snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL,
            snapshot_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL
        )
        """
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE INDEX ix_production_runs_status_test "
        "ON production_runs(status)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE connector_contract_versions (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE runtime_outbox_events "
        "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, event_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL UNIQUE)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE upstream_intake_events "
        "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations "
        "(version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) "
        "VALUES (85, 'add_workflow_model_media_usage')"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "INSERT INTO production_runs(id, status, snapshot_json, snapshot_hash) "
        "VALUES (1, 'queued', '{\"frozen\":true}', ?)",
        ("a" * 64,),
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "INSERT INTO connector_contract_versions(id) VALUES (4)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "INSERT INTO runtime_outbox_events(id, event_key) VALUES (7, 'evt-7')"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "INSERT INTO upstream_intake_events(id, payload_hash) VALUES (9, ?)",
        ("b" * 64,),
    )


def test_schema_86_adds_external_wait_without_rewriting_history(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'external-wait-v86.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
            _legacy_schema(connection)
            before = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status, snapshot_json, snapshot_hash "
                "FROM production_runs WHERE id=1"
            ).one()

            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 86)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status, snapshot_json, snapshot_hash "
                "FROM production_runs WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == before
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master "
                "WHERE type='table' AND name='production_runs'"
            ).scalar_one().count("waiting_external") == 1
            assert "node_instance_id" in {
                item["name"]
                for item in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                    "runtime_outbox_events"
                )
            }
            wait_columns = {
                item["name"]
                for item in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                    "workflow_external_waits"
                )
            }
            assert {
                "production_run_id",
                "request_node_instance_id",
                "resume_node_instance_id",
                "continuation_key",
                "outbox_event_id",
                "expected_contract_version_id",
                "correlation_id",
                "state",
                "deadline_at",
                "timeout_policy",
                "callback_intake_event_id",
                "callback_payload_hash",
                "created_at",
                "resumed_at",
                "expired_at",
                "cancelled_at",
                "audit_actor",
            } <= wait_columns
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE production_runs SET status='waiting_external' WHERE id=1"
            )
            insert_wait = (
                "INSERT INTO workflow_external_waits "
                "(production_run_id, request_node_instance_id, "
                "resume_node_instance_id, continuation_key, outbox_event_id, "
                "expected_contract_version_id, correlation_id, state, deadline_at, "
                "timeout_policy, audit_actor) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
            )
            values = (
                1,
                "write_vendor",
                "receive_vendor",
                "vendor-stage",
                7,
                4,
                "wait_K4hH2K3t9q",
                "waiting",
                "2026-08-30 00:00:00",
                "manual_review",
                "ops",
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(insert_wait, values)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(insert_wait, values)
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_external_waits "
                    "SET request_node_instance_id='changed' WHERE id=1"
                )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_external_waits "
                    "(production_run_id, request_node_instance_id, "
                    "resume_node_instance_id, continuation_key, outbox_event_id, "
                    "expected_contract_version_id, correlation_id, state, deadline_at, "
                    "timeout_policy, audit_actor) VALUES "
                    "(1, 'write-2', 'receive-2', 'stage-2', 7, 4, "
                    "'wait_invalid', 'unknown', '2026-08-30', 'manual_review', 'ops')"
                )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).fetchall() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_external_wait_model_enforces_state_policy_and_callback_shape() -> None:
    assert models.WorkflowExternalWait.__tablename__ == "workflow_external_waits"
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
            production_sql = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master "
                "WHERE type='table' AND name='production_runs'"
            ).scalar_one()
            assert "waiting_external" in production_sql
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_external_waits "
                    "(production_run_id, request_node_instance_id, "
                    "resume_node_instance_id, continuation_key, outbox_event_id, "
                    "expected_contract_version_id, correlation_id, state, deadline_at, "
                    "timeout_policy, audit_actor, created_at) VALUES "
                    "(1, 'write', 'receive', 'stage', 1, 1, 'wait-1', "
                    "'waiting', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'skip', 'ops', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
                )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_external_waits "
                    "(production_run_id, request_node_instance_id, "
                    "resume_node_instance_id, continuation_key, outbox_event_id, "
                    "expected_contract_version_id, correlation_id, state, deadline_at, "
                    "timeout_policy, callback_payload_hash, audit_actor, created_at) "
                    "VALUES (1, 'write', 'receive', 'stage', 1, 1, 'wait-2', "
                    "'resumed', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'fail', 'bad-hash', 'ops', "
                    "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
